Result: 论文论证了文学学科的工具——比较阅读、叙事学与诗学分析、批评理论、世界文学和翻译——对于构建具有文化素养的AI是不可或缺的。通过分层框架，揭示了文学研究与AI发展之间的自然交叉点，并提出世界文学路径可作为解决全球AI文本性问题的有效方法论。

Conclusion: 文学学科的分析工具已成为构建文化素养型AI的必需品。通过分层框架整合文学方法与AI开发，并借助世界文学路径（宏观结构、流通、不可译性）应对结构性单语主义，有望实现更细腻的文本模型和更多元的AI阐释体系。

Abstract: LLMs stage a new form of cultural encounter that is massive, automated, and monolingual. Literary disciplines have always negotiated cultural struggles with comparative reading of literature, narratological and poetic analysis, critical theory, world literature, and translation. These tools have now become indispensable for building culturally literate AI. The essay develops a layered framework toward more nuanced textual models and pluralistic interpretations of AI, emphasizing the natural intersections of literature and AI development, connecting current debates in critical theory with structural monolingualism, and suggesting a new application of world literature approaches to address global AI textuality through macrostructure, circulation, and untranslatability.

</details>


### [174] [HULAT2 at MER-TRANS 2026: Governed Multi-Agent Simplification for Spanish Easy-to-Read Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02381)
*Lourdes Moreno,Paloma Martínez,Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero,Miguel Domínguez-Gómez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HULAT2-UC3M团队在MER-TRANS 2026多语言易读翻译共享任务西班牙语赛道中的参赛系统。团队提交了三种全自动运行方案：基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流（RUN1）、增加了词汇支持层的增强版（RUN2）以及基于RigoChat的生成-评估-再生成基线（RUN3）。结果显示，信号引导的多智能体路由方法优于线性再生成基线，但词汇支持并未自动提升基于参考的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与MER-TRANS 2026共享任务，旨在探索多语言易读翻译（Easy-to-Read translation）的自动化方法，特别是验证多智能体协作架构、信号引导路由策略以及词汇支持层在该任务中的有效性。

Method: 提出三种西班牙语易读翻译系统：RUN1采用基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流，集成Gemini 2.5 Flash和RigoChat-7B-v2模型，使用并行生成策略、内部质量信号、事件-条件-动作（ECA）路由、受控编辑及可追溯决策；RUN2在RUN1基础上增加基于术语表和词汇资源的词汇支持层；RUN3为基于RigoChat的生成-评估-再生成线性基线，结合提示工程和LoRA微调。评估采用BLEU-Orig、BLEU-Gold、SARI和BERTScore等指标，开发阶段还监控语义保真度、可读性、词汇简洁性、句法清晰度和事实一致性等内部信号。

Result: 根据官方SARI指标，RUN1得分最高（44.0543），RUN2其次（43.1049），RUN3最低（38.5136）。表明信号引导的多智能体路由显著优于线性再生成基线，但词汇支持层的加入并未自动改善基于参考的评价分数。

Conclusion: 在该任务设定下，信号引导的多智能体路由策略是提升易读翻译质量的关键因素，而词汇支持的简单叠加并不总能转化为参考指标上的提升。需要进一步的段落级和文档级分析来评估可读性、事实一致性及面向用户的充分性。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [175] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍MEDIAREF，一个公开的网页文档知识库，用于支持可复现、低成本的媒体背景核查（MBC）生成评估，覆盖200家媒体来源，旨在解决现有基于LLM的检索增强生成事实核查中证据源可靠性假设过强的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的自动事实核查系统默认检索到的证据是可靠的，但现实中信息可能冲突、过时或来自不可靠/有偏见的来源。Schlichtkrull (2024)提出的媒体背景核查（MBC）可评估证据源可信度，但其生成依赖昂贵的商业搜索API，限制了可复现性。因此需要一种低成本、可复现的替代方案。

Method: 作者构建了MEDIAREF——一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，并提出了一套可复现的构建与更新方法。基于该知识库，对主流LLM在MBC生成任务上进行评估，涵盖200个媒体来源，并通过自动评估和定性评估两种方式验证MEDIAREF对MBC生成质量的提升效果。

Result: MEDIAREF成功支持了更高质量的媒体背景核查生成。在自动评估和定性评估中，基于MEDIAREF的MBC生成均表现出优于依赖商业搜索API的方法，且具备低成本、可复现的优势。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF为媒体背景核查提供了一个可复现、低成本的知识库解决方案，降低了对商业搜索API的依赖，推动了源批判性推理在自动事实核查领域的实用化进程。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [176] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析2010至2026年间NLP论文发表数据，发现LLM兴起后NLP研究正从传统*ACL主会向Findings子刊及通用ML会议迁移，且迁移受ML会议引用溢价驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的进步模糊了NLP与通用机器学习的学科界限，作者越来越多地在两个领域的会议上发表论文。本文旨在探究NLP研究的学科重心是否正在发生转移。

Method: 使用2010至2026年NLP领域发表数据，分别分析资深作者在*ACL主会、Findings子刊和通用ML会议上的论文份额变化，以及新锐作者（以至少三篇一作NLP论文出道）的发表去向变化；同时采用因果推断方法估计各会议对论文的引用溢价效应。

Result: （1）资深作者在*ACL主会的份额下降19.2个百分点，Findings子刊上升14.8个百分点，通用ML会议上升8.6个百分点；（2）新锐作者中，以*ACL为主的比例从2019年的84%降至2024年的74%，以通用ML为主的比例从5%升至21%；（3）通用ML会议存在显著的引用溢价，影响作者的会议选择。

Conclusion: NLP研究的发表重心正从传统*ACL旗舰会议向Findings子刊和通用ML会议显著转移，这一趋势部分由通用ML会议的引用优势驱动。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [177] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证NLP文化测量并非对文化现实的被动记录，而是一种物质-话语实践——测量装置（模型、数据、标注、评估）通过"能动切割"主动参与构建被测量的文化现实。作者借Karen Barad的"能动切割"概念，以影视对话的三个案例和装置本身的三个审视，提出一个理论驱动、经验严谨且文化权变的研究纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前NLP文化研究领域的一个深层盲点：研究者倾向于将语言模型视为文化现象的透明测量工具，却忽视了测量装置本身已内化了大量文化材料，并且装置的设计选择实质性地参与构成了它声称要测量的文化现实。作者旨在推动从"被动记录"到"共同构成"的范式转变。

Method: 理论框架采用Karen Barad的"能动实在论"（agential realism），核心概念为"能动切割"（agential cut）——即现象与测量工具之间的权变边界。以影视对话为实验场域，设计三组案例研究（结构测量、互动测量、偏离测量）展示装置如何划界，并结合三组对装置自身的审视（文化标记的擦除、对历史材料的调谐、智能体工作流中的能动性），形成"大图景"元分析。

Result: （1）语言模型作为测量装置，从一开始就与被测量的文化材料深度纠缠，因为模型训练数据本身即是文化产物；（2）装置的每一次实质性设计选择都是一次"能动切割"，划定了哪些文化特征可见、哪些被遮蔽；（3）这种切割同时具有方法论和伦理双重意涵，不存在"无视角的测量"。

Conclusion: 提出一个新型研究纲领：将每一次"能动切割"视为自觉的承诺——既是方法论选择也是伦理立场。要求文化NLP研究做到理论驱动、经验严谨、文化权变三者统一，承认并反思测量装置自身的文化嵌入性。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [178] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用缩放定律研究LLM计算规模与社会模拟保真度之间的关系，发现大多数行为和意见模拟任务将随规模扩大而快速改善，但纵向预测、代表性不足的意见以及人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）的校准方面改善缓慢甚至停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM社会模拟的保真度不足以被广泛采用，研究者希望探究：语言模型的缩放范式能否弥合这些差距，还是模拟保真度与通用能力是正交关系、需要独立的研究关注。

Method: 使用85个基于Qwen3架构、在DCLM网络文本语料上预训练的Transformer LLM（计算预算从10¹⁸到10²⁰ FLOPs），在三个子领域（意见建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）中进行缩放定律分析；同时评估35个更大规模的开源模型（最高70B参数），从损失值预测下游准确率。

Result: 三个子领域均发现显著的计算缩放效应。多数行为和意见模拟任务随规模快速改善，尤其在英语网络语料中代表性好的群体上。纵向预测和代表性不足的意见缩放较慢，与MMLU等通用基准相关性弱。行为模拟中，缩放未能改善模型对人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）的校准，以及从相关任务中学习关联奖励的启发式能力；微调模型从0.5B到8B参数也未见明显性能提升。

Conclusion: 规模扩大将在大多数场景下改善社会模拟，但存在异常值，且在低资源领域中改进的可靠性较低。社会模拟的某些方面与通用能力并非完全对齐，值得独立研究关注。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [179] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过从LibriVox有声书中提取声学和韵律特征，首次系统性地研究了 narration 质量（如音调、语速、响度）与有声书消费吸引力之间的关系，发现声学信息本身对吸引力具有稳健的关联，且该效应在控制书名和体裁影响后依然显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: narration（旁白/朗读）是有声书体验的核心，但 narration 质量如何影响有声书的吸引力，以及这种影响如何随体裁、书名和受众变化，此前缺乏系统性的计算研究。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为有声书个性化和 narrator 选角提供数据驱动的洞见。

Method: 利用预训练音频模型从LibriVox有声书中提取声学和韵律特征（如音调、语速、响度）；将这些特征与消费数据（主要是view-rate）进行关联分析；考察声学特征与体裁、书名的交互效应；并进一步使用更细粒度的专有参与度指标进行验证。

Result: 即便消费数据有限，仅凭声学信息即可稳健地预测有声书的吸引力，且在控制书名效应后该关联仍然成立；研究结果在更细粒度的专有参与度指标上得到了进一步验证。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地将 narration 质量、体裁、书名与有声书消费联系起来的研究，证明了数据驱动方法在改进有声书个性化推荐和 narrator 选角方面的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [180] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VRRL强化学习框架，通过掩码轨迹前缀和经验回放缓冲区两个组件，增强大视觉语言模型在推理中进行视觉感知驱动的自我反思能力，在分布外场景下显著提升了视觉定位和空间导航任务的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在思维链推理中进行自我反思时，往往未能充分关注视觉输入，导致无法将反馈转化为基于视觉证据的修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现更差。模型倾向于在早期犯错而非从错误中恢复，且缺乏接触多样化的失败状态。

Method: 提出VRRL（Visually-grounded Reflective Reinforcement Learning）框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）训练时随机掩码轨迹前缀，迫使模型关注从错误中间预测中恢复，而非避免早期犯错；（2）引入基于经验回放缓冲区的缓冲式roll-in，使模型暴露于多样化的失败状态并学会纠正。

Result: 在涉及表格、图表的视觉定位任务以及空间导航基准上，现成模型和常规微调模型在分布偏移下性能大幅下降，而VRRL通过有效利用自我反思，相较于标准RL和面向反思的微调基线，显著提升了平均分布外准确率。

Conclusion: VRRL通过强化学习训练机制显式地培养视觉感知驱动的自我反思能力，有效解决了LVLM在反思阶段忽视视觉信息的问题，为提升多模态模型的鲁棒推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [181] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模说话人识别基准DramaSR-532K（含532K条标注对话、900+角色），以及基于大推理模型的多模态说话人识别方法DramaSR-LRM，通过工具调用自主整合听觉、语言和视觉线索，在短语音场景下显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇电视剧的视频理解极具挑战性，其复杂剧情的解析高度依赖说话人识别——即准确将每句对话归因到对应角色。现有方法在短语音场景下声学生物特征不可靠，需要融合多模态信息来解决这一问题。

Method: （1）构建DramaSR-532K大规模基准数据集，包含532K条标注对话、900+角色，整合听觉、语言和视觉三种模态线索；（2）提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具调用机制自主聚合上下文证据，综合多种输入实现高保真说话人归因。

Result: DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在短语音片段上表现突出——这正是传统声学生物特征方法失效的场景。

Conclusion: 通过大规模多模态基准数据集与基于大推理模型的工具调用方法相结合，DramaSR-LRM有效解决了长篇电视剧中的说话人识别难题，尤其在声学线索不可靠时展现了强大的鲁棒性。代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [182] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LACUNA 是首个具备参数级定位真值的遗忘测试平台，通过向 OLMo 模型预定义参数注入合成个体的 PII，揭示现有 SOTA 遗忘方法虽在输出层表现良好，实则定位不精确且易受重浮现攻击；而当定位准确时，即使简单梯度方法也能实现鲁棒擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法的评测仅停留在输出层面，无法判断模型参数中的知识是否被真正擦除还是仅被混淆，且重浮现攻击的成功加剧了这一担忧。缺乏参数级定位真值的基准测试平台来验证遗忘方法是否真正作用于知识存储的权重。

Method: 构建 LACUNA 测试平台：通过掩码持续预训练，将合成个体的 PII 注入到 OLMo 1B 和 7B 模型的预定义参数中，从而建立参数级定位真值。基于此平台对现有 SOTA 遗忘方法进行基准测试，并验证定位精度对遗忘效果的影响。

Result: 现有 SOTA 遗忘方法尽管输出层表现强劲，但定位极不精确，且易受重浮现攻击。相反，当定位成功时，简单的基于梯度的遗忘方法即可实现强擦除效果和对重浮现攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确的参数定位是鲁棒遗忘的关键。LACUNA 作为首个参数级真值测试平台，填补了行为评测的空白，为推进基于定位的鲁棒遗忘研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: X-VAE 提出用预训练自编码器的隐层表征的经验均值和标准差来参数化一个数据自适应的精确先验，替代传统VAE的标准高斯先验，并引入隐层缩放因子控制生成样本的多样性与保真度，在保持重建质量的同时提升了生成可控性和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAE采用的标准各向同性高斯先验往往无法捕捉复杂数据集的真实隐层分布，导致重建精度下降、采样质量受限以及隐空间表达能力不足，需要一种更贴合数据实际结构的先验分布。

Method: 首先训练一个标准自编码器（AE），提取其隐层编码的经验均值与标准差；将这些统计量用于参数化一个新的高斯先验，替换VAE的标准正态先验；推导了该先验对应的KL散度目标函数；在生成阶段引入隐层缩放因子，显式控制采样隐向量的方差，以调节生成多样性与保真度之间的平衡。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验表明，X-VAE保持了重建质量，同时产生的隐层表征更好地与经验数据分布对齐，带来了更强的可控性和更逼真的生成样本。

Conclusion: X-VAE通过数据自适应的精确先验和隐层缩放机制，有效解决了传统VAE先验失配问题，在工业与工程设计等需要严格约束的场景中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [184] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LF-IBIS 是一种免似然的贝叶斯强化学习算法，通过结合近似贝叶斯计算与迭代批量重要性采样，在缺乏显式似然函数的情况下实现环境参数与最优策略的在线贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数贝叶斯强化学习方法依赖显式似然函数，而现实场景中似然函数往往不可获取或难以处理。因此需要一种无需显式似然的BRL方法，以在数据稀缺条件下利用先验知识并进行在线信念更新。

Method: 提出 LF-IBIS 算法，将近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS）相结合，在无显式/可处理似然的情况下，随着在线交互数据的到达，更新环境参数和最优策略的近似后验分布。

Result: 在临床试验的响应自适应随机化仿真研究中，利用闭式后验进行了验证；并在后验无闭式形式的场景下，展示了基于最优策略后验分布的在线策略更新能力。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS 在环境动态缺乏显式或可处理似然的情况下，能够实现完整的贝叶斯推断，提供策略不确定性量化，有助于探索-利用权衡的贝叶斯处理。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [185] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了√n超量风险界和聚类中心的一致性，在MCAR机制下证明了√n收敛速度和渐近正态性，并给出了不完全数据估计的聚类中心收敛到真实中心的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-means无法直接处理不完全数据，现有基于k-means的缺失数据聚类方法多侧重于提升实际聚类精度，但在渐近意义下缺乏理论保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，系统研究缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质。

Method: 本文在一般缺失机制下建立√n超量风险界并证明估计聚类中心的一致性；针对MCAR机制，进一步推导估计聚类中心的√n收敛速度和渐近正态性；研究不完全数据估计的聚类中心何时收敛到完全观测数据的真实聚类中心，并给出关于缺失概率和真实聚类间分离度的充分条件。

Result: （1）在一般缺失机制下，证明了√n超量风险界和估计聚类中心的一致性；（2）在MCAR机制下，证明了√n收敛速度和渐近正态性；（3）给出了不完全数据估计中心收敛到真实中心的充分条件：要求缺失概率与聚类间分离度满足特定关系；（4）揭示了MCAR下实现√n速率和收敛到真实中心均要求k个真实中心在每个维度上都互异，凸显了高维场景下的应用挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据k-means聚类提供了系统的理论保证，但分析表明MCAR机制下高维应用面临显著挑战——要求各真实中心在所有维度上均互异。最后通过合成不完全数据集的数值模拟验证了理论分析结果。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [186] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的方法论，结合Ghost变量、Shapley值与加性重要性度量，定义自相关性与偏自相关性函数作为滞后重要性指标，并设计了一种用模型一步预测填补缺失特征的新方法，在模拟与真实数据上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单变量时间序列预测中，亟需一种不依赖特定模型的、能够量化各滞后阶数对预测贡献程度的方法，以揭示时间序列的内在滞后结构。

Method: 提出Ghost变量与Shapley值框架，结合加性重要性度量，构建自相关性（auto-relevance）和偏自相关性（partial auto-relevance）函数作为滞后重要性指标；同时提出一种新颖的缺失特征填补方法——利用同一模型的一步预测值替代联盟方法中的缺失特征。

Result: 在季节性ARMA族模型与循环神经网络上的实验表明，所计算的滞后重要性度量在几乎所有案例中均成功揭示了预期的滞后结构。

Conclusion: 该联合框架特别适用于时间序列场景，能够以模型无关的方式有效识别滞后结构，有望成为时间序列分析与特征重要性评估的通用工具。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [187] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题转化为连续变分代理问题，在加权Sobolev空间中构造了一族λ-凸泛函，证明其全局适定、稳定且具有近乎C³正则性。该方法可直接利用椭圆正则性和凸分析，将最优参数密度求解归结为单个线性系统，绕过了迭代优化，并建立了泛化误差O(1/α)和有限宽度逼近O(1/N)的显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络优化的理论基础薄弱，现有非凸景观和随机启发式方法缺乏理论保障。Wasserstein梯度流和平均场方法面临正则性受限和离散化困难，亟需一个具有良好数学结构、可绕过迭代优化的新框架，以弥合NTK与特征学习之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 将浅层网络训练重新表述为加权Sobolev空间中参数密度的λ-凸变分问题；利用椭圆正则性理论和凸分析工具证明泛函的适定性、稳定性及C³正则性；通过变分法将最优参数密度求解转化为单个线性系统的求解，无需迭代优化；进一步导出泛化误差界和有限宽度逼近速率。

Result: (1) 变分问题全局适定且参数密度具有几乎C³正则性；(2) 最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，彻底绕过迭代优化；(3) 泛化误差为O(1/α)，其中α为正则化参数；(4) N个神经元宽度网络的解以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了神经网络训练从离散非凸优化到连续凸变分分析的范式转换，为过参数化现象提供了基于变分法的严格理论框架，成功连接了NTK和特征学习两种视角，并给出了计算上可行的线性系统求解方案。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [188] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于反事实决策的不确定性量化框架，核心创新是"策略耦合覆盖"（policy-coupled coverage）概念，并据此设计了PC-RACP两阶段方法，在保证有限样本覆盖有效性的同时实现更高的决策效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）仅关注统计有效性，无法直接指导反事实情境下的最优决策。在反事实场景中，观测结果依赖于所采取的行动，因此不确定性不能独立于决策规则来刻画。理论与决策实践之间存在鸿沟：有覆盖保证的预测集并不直接告诉决策者该采取何种行动。

Method: 作者构建了一个决策论框架，提出"策略耦合覆盖"——即由预测集自身所诱导的行动下实现结果的覆盖——作为连接不确定性与行动的最优接口。在此基础上：(1) 证明了自然的最大最小规则在分布模糊性下是极小极大最优的；(2) 建立了策略耦合覆盖优化、通用覆盖公式与直接风险规避策略优化三者之间的等价性，并导出总体最优预测集的显式形式；(3) 提出PC-RACP两阶段方法，用有限样本逼近最优预测集，并赋予严格的有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 模拟实验和真实电子邮件营销实验均表明，PC-RACP在保持有效覆盖的前提下，相比现有方法能获得更高的效用；同时证实忽略决策问题的反事实结构对有效性和效用均是有害的。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性量化与反事实决策的最优且无损失的接口。PC-RACP方法在理论和实践中均表现出色，为高 stakes 决策中的不确定性量化提供了从统计有效性到决策最优性的完整桥梁。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [189] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种加性深度学习框架，将理化描述符（MLP编码）与分子图拓扑（GNN编码）在训练过程中保持分离，仅在预测阶段通过加性（含可选乘性交互）模型组合，从而实现对化学与结构贡献的直接分解。该框架在AqSolDB上预训练、在BigSolDB2上微调，性能优异且透明可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水溶性预测模型大多将理化描述符与分子图信息混合为单一表征，导致无法区分预测是由全局化学性质驱动、分子结构驱动还是两者共同作用，缺乏可解释的分解能力。

Method: 提出加性深度学习框架：理化描述符由多层感知机（化学分支）编码，分子图拓扑由图神经网络（结构分支）编码，两分支输出仅在预测阶段通过加性模型（含可选乘性交互项）组合。采用AqSolDB大数据集预训练后在BigSolDB2小数据集上微调。解释性分析包括：分支输出的最佳线性投影、溶解度类别间的分子嵌入汇总、以及基于GNNExplainer的原子级掩码在官能团层面的聚合。

Result: 在两个数据集上均取得竞争性预测性能；化学分支与经典理化描述符对齐，结构分支捕获了与溶解度相关的图拓扑和官能团模式；预训练-微调策略显著提升精度并降低运行间变异。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持竞争性预测精度的同时，使化学信息与结构信息的各自角色更加透明，为水溶性预测提供了一种可解释的双通道建模范式。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [190] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究通过SPD流形几何分析发现，LLM人格表达具有双重性质：聚合特征（大五人格分数）对框架扰动鲁棒但信息有限，几何特征对框架高度敏感但在共享框架下可恢复，揭示人格几何是框架依赖的协调模式而非内在属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通过心理测量问卷评估LLM人格的方法仅依赖聚合分数，丢弃了题目间的相关结构。作者旨在检验这种几何结构是内在固有的还是依赖于问卷框架（题目排序）的。

Method: 使用IPIP-50问卷，构建题目间的相关矩阵，在SPD（对称正定）流形上分析几何结构。通过操控GPT-4o模拟美国人和华裔美国人两种人格的题目排序（框架），比较聚合特征与几何特征在不同框架条件下的表现。

Result: 聚合特征（大五分数）在随机化下下降21%但对框架不敏感；几何特征（SPD流形）在框架失配时崩溃（下降42%），但在共享框架下大幅恢复至84%，超过聚合特征的76%恢复率。这种"崩溃-恢复"模式表明人格几何是框架依赖的协调模式。

Conclusion: LLM人格表达具有双重性质：框架依赖的几何特征与框架鲁棒的聚合特征。这一发现需要框架感知的评估方法，并挑战了静态特质观念，为理解LLM人格提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个模块化的神经符号框架，通过将神经预测模型与符号推理层分离，生成满足领域可行性约束的反事实解释，解决了现有方法生成不现实建议的问题。在Adult Income数据集上结合MLP和ASP的案例研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽然能生成改变预测的替代方案，但因缺乏显式的领域知识和干预约束机制，常产生不现实或不可行的推荐。需要一种能将数据驱动预测与符号推理相结合的方法，以生成符合领域规则且可操作的解释。

Method: 提出PACE（模块化神经符号框架），将系统分为两个组件：(1) 神经预测模型（如MLP）负责分类；(2) 符号推理层使用Answer Set Programming规则编码领域特定的可行性约束（如可修改教育与职业、保持不可变属性不变），在反事实生成过程中强制执行这些约束。框架具有模型无关性和领域适应性。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明：符号约束能有效生成更满足领域可行性要求的反事实解释。结果揭示了反事实有效性（validity）与合理性（plausibility）之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在可解释AI中具有潜力，能够生成透明且可行性感知的反事实解释，为需要现实决策支持的领域提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [192] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个约束型编码代理工作流，用于联邦学习算法配方的自动搜索。代理可提出并实现候选训练算法（含聚合规则、客户端更新调度、局部目标等），在任务配置文件约束下进行搜索。在五个医疗跨孤岛FLamby任务和六个LEAF数据集上评估，多轮重复实验表明部分增益来自FL配方改进，而其他增益受种子敏感性、固定表面调优效应或单次运行伪影影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究依赖大量细微的算法选择（优化器变体、聚合规则、训练调度、正则化等），手动探索成本高昂，且当候选改动改变训练或评估路径时难以公平比较。亟需一种系统化、可重复的自动搜索方法来探索FL算法配方空间。

Method: 提出AFR（Auto-FL-Research），一个约束型编码代理工作流。代理在任务配置文件（固定变异表面、计算预算、通信契约和最终评估方式）约束下，自动提出并实现候选FL训练算法，涵盖服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、局部目标函数及模型变体。每次搜索活动记录候选得分、运行时间、修改文件、产物及失败状态。

Result: 在五个医疗跨孤岛FLamby任务和六个LEAF数据集（含合成任务）上评估。五种子重复评估显示：四个FLamby任务和五个LEAF配置获得增益，但也暴露出种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。同预算对照实验表明，部分增益源自FL配方实质性改变，其他改进则来自固定表面标量调优效果，或在重复/留出评估下失效。

Conclusion: 混合结果是本工作的贡献之一：展示了如何将代理生成的候选方案区分为可重复的FL机制改进、固定表面调优效应以及选择性的单次运行伪影，为FL自动化研究提供了重要的方法论参考。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [193] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个全新的小型语言模型架构，从零开始设计，与 GPT、LLaMA、Mistral 等现有模型家族无结构关联。它引入了五个独立创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应Token合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和带偏移向量的 RMSNorm（WiolaRMSNorm）。该模型发布四种参数规模（120M–1.5B），完全兼容 HuggingFace 生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流语言模型架构（GPT、LLaMA、Mistral 等）在结构上高度同质化，大多基于 Transformer 的渐进式改进，缺乏从第一性原理出发的全新架构探索。作者旨在证明，完全独立于现有模型家族、融合多项原创设计的小型语言模型同样可以达到有竞争力的性能，从而推动架构多样性研究。

Method: Wiola 架构包含五项独立创新：(1) SRPE：将 token 位置嵌入三维螺旋流形，融合绝对、相对和层级位置信号；(2) GCLA：每个解码层通过软交叉注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要，增强层间一致性；(3) ATM：在中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻 token，降低注意力复杂度且不损失信息；(4) DSFF：用双流并行结构替代传统 MLP，通过可学习的逐维度门控融合；(5) WiolaRMSNorm：在 RMSNorm 基础上引入可学习的逐维度偏移向量以防止表示坍塌。论文提供完整数学推导、框图、复杂度分析，并与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行系统对比。

Result: Wiola 发布了 120M、360M、700M 和 1.5B 四种参数规模，所有 22 项架构单元测试全部通过，并完全兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态，表明该架构具备实际的工程可行性和可复现性。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明了从第一性原理出发、完全独立于现有模型家族设计 SLM 架构是可行的。其五项原创组件各自针对 Transformer 架构的不同瓶颈（位置编码、层间信息流、注意力冗余、FFN 表达力、归一化稳定性），为语言模型架构创新提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [194] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent4cs，一种采用自底向上策略的多智能体框架，通过摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体协同工作，显著提升大型代码仓库的摘要质量和关键词覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方案通常将源码视为扁平文本，未能充分利用代码仓库内部的层级结构和相互依赖关系，导致对大型、复杂或混淆代码库的理解效果不佳。

Method: 设计了三智能体协同框架：(1) 摘要智能体负责生成稳健的代码摘要；(2) 关键词提取智能体从子文件夹中主动识别关键信息；(3) 质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。整体采用自底向上的分层摘要策略。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上评估，Agent4cs在所有文件夹层级上的语义一致性相比两种结构化提示基线平均提升8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: Agent4cs通过多智能体协同和层次化策略，有效解决了大型代码仓库摘要中的信息利用不足问题，在语义一致性和关键词覆盖率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [195] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于难度的路由式服务控制架构，通过轻量路由器将常规客服会话保持在低成本路径，而将存在操作耦合的会话升级至具备冲突感知通信和写操作触发式反思的工作流，从而在高风险后端写操作前集中审慎控制，在零售和航空任务上验证了可靠性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服正从对话界面转向操作执行（退款、取消、换货等），但客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入之间的交互可能产生操作错误。现有方案缺乏针对高风险操作的选择性控制机制，要么过度控制影响常规服务效率，要么控制不足导致操作失误。

Method: 提出一种"难度路由式服务控制架构"（difficulty-routed service-control architecture）：(1) 轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径；(2) 将存在操作耦合（operationally coupled）的会话路由至升级工作流；(3) 升级路径采用冲突感知通信（conflict-aware communication）和写操作触发式反思（write-triggered reconsideration），在执行后端写操作前集中审慎控制与安全防护。

Result: 在τ²-bench的零售和航空任务上评估：(1) 零售场景中，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升可靠性；(2) 路由证据表明更强的控制被导向冲突请求而非广泛施加于常规请求；(3) 对话和工具使用分析显示增益并非来自无差别的交互扩展，额外轮次和工具调用主要用于证据收集、写入分离和写前反思；(4) 升级工作流能保留后备计划、将检索记录绑定到正确操作、排序写入、分解多实体请求；(5) 航空场景结果验证了相同的服务控制逻辑可扩展到预订操作。

Conclusion: 选择性、以写操作为锚点的控制策略比统一施加控制更有效；难度路由架构可在保持常规服务效率的同时，在具有操作冲突的高风险请求上显著提升可靠性，且该框架具有跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [196] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro是一种无需数据的对比权重引导方法，通过在权重空间（而非激活空间）进行干预，显著提升大语言模型的发散思维能力，减少模式坍缩，且无需行为数据、重新训练或梯度微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在开放性问题中倾向于生成相似回答，即"人工蜂巢效应"（artificial hivemind effect），严重制约了其发散思维与创造性表现。现有方法多依赖行为数据或微调，缺乏一种简洁、无需数据且能跨任务泛化的解决方案。

Method: 提出CreativityNeuro方法，核心是对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering），即在权重空间进行对比干预以增强发散思维。与激活引导（activation steering）不同，该方法直接在模型权重层面操作。评估涵盖三项创造力测试：词汇空间的发散联想任务（DAT）、大规模人类评估（N=720）中的替代用途测试（AUT）和Task Task。

Result: 在DAT上，CreativityNeuro将表现提升多达14个人类百分位数；在AUT和Task Task上，人类评估显示原创性、惊喜度和创造力均有显著提升，且在所有三项任务上均显著减少了模式坍缩指标。激活引导在DAT上表现可比，但无法迁移到AUT和Task Task，凸显了权重空间引导的跨任务泛化优势。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro有效提升了LLM的发散思维能力并降低了模式坍缩，具备无需数据、无需重训练、无需梯度微调的优点，为增强大语言模型在创造性领域的表现提供了一条简洁可行的路径。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [197] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将一个基于扩散机制和混合专家架构的语言模型 DiffusionGemma-26B 适配到医学视觉问答任务上，在相同 LoRA 微调条件下，其表现匹配甚至超越同尺寸自回归模型，且解码速度更快，并原生支持自回归模型所不具备的任意顺序文本填充能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学基础模型几乎全部采用自回归生成方式，而扩散语言模型在通用领域已展现竞争力，但尚未在医学领域得到探索。此外，真实临床报告中常存在简略或不一致的问题，需要灵活的文本填充能力，这正是扩散模型双向去噪机制所擅长的。

Method: 采用混合专家扩散语言模型 DiffusionGemma-26B，与同尺寸自回归模型 Gemma-4-26B 在相同的 LoRA 微调方案下进行对比，测评于多个医学视觉问答数据集，并使用对冗长度鲁棒的 LLM 评判器进行评分；同时验证扩散模型独有的任意顺序填充能力。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学 VQA 数据集上匹配或超越自回归模型；经微调后（3.8B 活跃参数）性能可与前沿视觉-语言模型竞争，解码速度提升 3.5-4.4 倍；扩散模型天然具备任意顺序填充能力，允许放射科医生修正报告片段后由模型填充中间文本，自回归模型在此任务上表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学领域不仅能够达到自回归模型的性能水平，还凭借双向去噪机制带来更快的解码速度和灵活的填充能力，为构建更具交互性和可控性的临床报告生成系统提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）来弥合大语言模型在预测下一个token与在特定企业API内正确执行操作之间的目标差距。通过在模拟Jira和Confluence API的合成环境中用GRPO训练，RL策略在四个非退化场景中将平均奖励从0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00，最大增益出现在Confluence页面创建任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型本质上是为预测下一个token而训练的，而非在特定API约束下执行操作。在利基企业SaaS工作流中，成功意味着按正确顺序调用正确的端点并填充正确的嵌套参数。这种目标错配表现为静默失败：遗漏必填字段、幻觉出不存在工具、或仅执行一次读取便提前终止。现有方法缺乏在目标环境中直接优化模型行为的能力，本文旨在检验RLVR是否能填补这一鸿沟。

Method: 构建五个合成环境，以模式级别的保真度模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API。奖励完全从工具调用轨迹中计算得出，不依赖在线API、学习的评判模型或人工标注。在这些合成环境上，使用GRPO（组相对策略优化）对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行强化学习训练，并采用与驱动GRPO训练的相同检查器进行评分评估。

Result: 在四个奖励非退化的场景中，RL训练后的策略将平均奖励从4B基线的0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00。其中Confluence页面创建场景增益最大（0.35→1.00）。然而，五个场景中有一个（ticket-transition）的奖励已饱和，prompted 4B模型已达上限。

Conclusion: 该研究是通向面向利基企业API的"结果优化型小模型"的初步探索。RLVR展现了在端到端任务中弥合训练目标与应用需求差距的潜力。但存在两个关键局限：手工构建可验证奖励难以扩展到本文报告的少数端点之外，且部分场景奖励信号本身已饱和，无法通过RL进一步改善。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [199] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedAgentBench-v3修复了前代版本41.7%的静默终止天花板后，纯强化学习在临床协议执行任务上达到18.2% pass@1，但受制于能力天花板和格式知识壁垒，与基于规则的SFT（34.1%）存在15.9个百分点差距，由此提出决策/格式知识/查找三元分类法来预测RL可学习性并指导SFT+RL混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务天然适合从世界反馈中进行强化学习，但现有基准存在严重的静默终止天花板问题（不做任何操作也能获得高分），导致RL学到的最优策略是"不作为"。同时，RL能否成功还取决于模型的基础能力和对临床格式知识的掌握，这两类结构性障碍尚未被系统研究和解决。

Method: 首先审计MedAgentBench v1/v2发现41.7%的静默终止天花板；构建MAB-v3（508个任务，天花板降至8.9%）。在Qwen3-8B上训练纯RL，识别出两类结构性障碍：能力天花板（20种任务中10种基线表现为0%）和格式知识壁垒（3种任务需要精确临床代码，无法通过探索发现）。提出决策/格式知识/查找三元分类法来预测RL可学习性，并以SFT注入代码、RL学习条件逻辑作为修复方案。

Result: 纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，基于规则的SFT达到34.1%，15.9个百分点差距完全归因于能力天花板和格式知识壁垒。三元分类法有效预测了各任务类型的RL可学习性。

Conclusion: 临床协议执行的RL训练面临静默终止天花板、能力天花板和格式知识壁垒三重障碍。最佳方案是混合策略：用SFT注入无法通过探索发现的精确临床代码等格式知识，再用RL学习条件决策逻辑。三元分类法为任务类型的训练策略选择提供了系统化指导。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [200] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PMD（程序性记忆蒸馏），将跨episode的程序性信号（策略、失败模式、重复模式）组织为三层记忆，并通过记忆条件自教师蒸馏到策略权重中，实现策略与记忆的协同进化。在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相较SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL上提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH上提升7.9-13.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR及SDPO等方法仅利用episode级别的验证信号进行策略更新，忽略了rollout中丰富的程序性过程信息（哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式反复出现、哪些模式跨问题重复）。这些跨episode信号无法被episode-local更新捕获，导致训练效率受限。

Method: 提出PMD框架：①三层抽象记忆结构——原始轨迹层、自我反思策略与教训层、跨问题的高层行为模式层，均从模型自身轨迹在线提取；②记忆条件自教师（memory-conditioned self-teacher），利用积累经验监督学生对自身rollout的学习，使学生逐步将程序性知识内化到参数中；③协同进化设计原则——策略生成rollout更新记忆，记忆塑造监督更新策略，两者交替迭代。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相较SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL上提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH上提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验表明，冻结记忆或冻结策略均导致SCIKNOWEVAL各领域性能下降超10%，验证了协同进化的关键作用。

Conclusion: PMD通过将跨episode程序性信号蒸馏为可重用记忆并内化到策略权重中，实现了记忆与策略的协同进化，最终得到一个推理时无记忆开销的模型。协同进化是性能提升的核心驱动力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [201] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明AI智能体通过赋予不同"人格"即可从同一数据中产生分歧性甚至对立的分析结论，系统性地复现了人类研究者的分析变异。为此，作者提出了m值（多重宇宙值）和Agentic Bootstrap方法，将分析路径的分布可观测化，作为评估科学证据可信度的新标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中，不同分析选择（即"花园分叉路径"）会导致同一数据得出不同结论，但这些隐藏路径难以被观察和评估。传统同行评审难以识别这些合理但选择性的分析报告，科学证据的可信度面临挑战。

Method: 1. 构建AI智能体，通过赋予不同"人格"（personas）来模拟人类研究者的分析选择变异；2. 在四个高风险领域验证AI智能体是否能复现人类分析分歧；3. 与42个人类研究团队分析同一移民数据集的基准结果进行对比；4. 提出m值（multiverse value），即某一分析路径产生至少同样极端结论的概率；5. 提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，利用AI智能体采样合理的分析路径空间来估计m值。

Result: 1. AI智能体复现了人类72%的意识形态差异（在报告效应估计上）；2. AI报告86%通过独立AI评审，78%通过多数人类专家评审，表明这些分析在方法学上并非明显有缺陷；3. 在人类移民研究中，13.5%的报告分析落在分析空间中最极端的5%范围内（m<0.05），说明选择性报告问题显著。

Conclusion: 核心挑战往往不是分析本身有缺陷，而是从大量方法学上可辩护的分析中进行选择性探索和报告。AI智能体可能使这一问题加剧。科学证据不仅应依据单一报告分析来评价，还应考虑其在合理可报告分析分布中的位置。Agentic Bootstrap使这一分布可观测，将其转化为科学可信度的评判标准。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [202] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus 是一个用于设计和评估用户参与式智能体权限管理的游乐场系统，包含模块化智能体核心与自动化评估框架。通过实现六种权限助手并在多场景下评估，发现用户输入对安全隐私至关重要，AI 增强可降低认知负荷，但没有一种设计在所有场景下都最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主执行工具调用的 AI 智能体面临权限管理的紧迫问题：用户应当扮演什么角色、能够扮演什么角色。尽管已有多种方案提出，但用户在智能体权限管理中的参与方式仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 Janus 系统，包含两部分：(1) Janus-Core，一个模块化智能体系统，支持构建涵盖多样化权限管理设计的权限助手；(2) Janus-Harness，一个自动化评估框架。基于识别用户参与关键设计轴的概念模型，实现了六种覆盖设计空间的权限助手，并在三个场景和三个合成响应者下进行评估。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 用户输入至关重要，能显著增强隐私和安全性；(2) 对用户决策进行 AI 增强有助于降低认知负荷；(3) 系统设计必须考虑现实中用户的权限疲劳等行为。没有单一设计在所有场景下都表现最优。

Conclusion: 在智能体系统中部署权限助手需要采用更有原则性、上下文敏感的方法。Janus 已公开发布，以支持未来在智能体系统设计中这一维度的进一步研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [203] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Semi-CoT 框架，利用无标注问题生成伪思维链作为半监督学习信号，通过语义熵筛选高质量伪 CoT 示范，在多个数学推理数据集上初步验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 方法仅将推理链用作推理时的提示，生成的大量推理轨迹未被复用为训练信号。作者希望探索将 CoT 从推理时优化扩展到半监督伪监督学习的可能性。

Method: 提出 Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning（Semi-CoT）框架：对每个无标注问题采样多条伪 CoT，估计答案级语义熵，通过熵门（entropy gate）筛选低熵推理链作为可靠的伪 CoT 示范，用于学生模型训练。

Result: 在 AQuA、SVAMP、GSM8K、MultiArith 上的实验表明：熵门筛选的伪答案精度达 91.36%–100%；Semi-CoT 在 SVAMP 和 GSM8K 上带来小幅增益，AQuA 出现负迁移，MultiArith 已达性能上限。

Conclusion: 无标注问题能提供可靠的伪推理信号，但有效利用仍需更强的示范选择策略或学生训练方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [204] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPINE-World 是一个通过在线交互学习以对象为中心的程序化世界模型的 LLM 智能体。它通过假设-检验循环中两个协作智能体（一个在环境中行动，一个用代码合成模型），并利用"本体论误差"这一贝叶斯度量来引导探索。在 ARC-AGI-3 基准测试中，该智能体在 25 个游戏中解决了 20 个，无需逐游戏训练，动作效率得分达 78.4（对比人类基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的深度网络世界模型数据需求量大、泛化能力差；而 LLM 程序合成世界模型虽数据高效且可复用，但主要局限于结构化状态世界，难以扩展到需要灵活推断对象结构的像素渲染环境。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现对像素环境中未知对象结构和语义的在线程序化建模。

Method: OPINE-World 采用双智能体协作架构：(1) 一个行动智能体在环境中执行动作；(2) 一个建模智能体通过代码合成世界模型，结合重放验证和基于模型的规划。两者在"假设-检验"循环中协同工作，并通过"本体论误差"（ontology error）——一种衡量对象类型充分性的贝叶斯指标——来驱动探索策略，从而高效发现环境中的对象类别和动态规律。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 基准测试的 25 个游戏中，OPINE-World 无需逐游戏训练即解决了 20 个，动作效率得分为 78.4（以人类表现为基线进行归一化），展示了其在未知对象词汇、目标和动作语义条件下的高效技能习得能力。

Conclusion: OPINE-World 证明，将 LLM 驱动的程序合成与基于本体论不确定性的探索相结合，能够在像素级环境中实现高效、可复用的在线世界模型学习，为构建适应陌生任务的通用智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [205] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将SOLiD框架扩展到更大模型和更现实的偏好学习场景，发现欺骗检测具有有利的规模扩展特性，但存在对分布偏移敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的欺骗行为监控和预防成本高昂，SOLiD框架虽有前景，但尚未在更大规模模型和多样化、现实的偏好学习设置中得到验证。

Method: 将SOLiD（基于谎言检测器的可扩展监督）框架扩展到更大参数规模的模型，在多样化且更现实的偏好学习场景中进行评估，研究检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移影响。

Result: 规模扩展有利：检测器真阳性率99%时，未检测到的欺骗从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%；在微调阶段可完全移除昂贵的人工标注者，且欺骗行为无统计显著增加。但SOLiD对检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移敏感，可能导致检测器假阳性率达到不可接受的水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD框架在更大模型上表现更优，且可降低对人类标注的依赖，但实际部署中必须解决分布偏移导致的假阳性率过高问题。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [206] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 尽管经过对齐训练，LLM在部署时仍可能生成不安全输出。本文研究了一种简单的实时监控器，通过对外部验证器信号进行阈值化来触发警报，并使用风险控制校准阈值。实验表明，该简单设计与基于序贯假设检验的先进监控器性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM即使经过对齐训练，在部署阶段仍不可避免地会产生不安全输出，因此需要在线监控机制，在安全性无法保证时及时发出警报。现有复杂监控方案（如序贯假设检验）虽然有效，但本文旨在探索是否存在更简单的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种简单的实时监控器：利用外部模型生成验证信号（verifier signal），通过阈值化（thresholding）将该信号转化为二值警报决策，阈值则通过风险控制（risk control）框架进行校准，以控制误报率或漏报率等风险指标。

Result: 在数学推理和红队测试数据集上的实验表明，该简单监控器设计与基于序贯假设检验的更复杂监控器相比，性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单的阈值化监控方案配合风险控制校准，即可达到与复杂序贯方法相媲美的安全监控效果，为LLM在线安全部署提供了一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [207] [EO-Agents: A Three-Agent LLM Pipeline for Earth Observation Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01584)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Amin Tabrizian,Armin Mehrabian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于NASA地球观测知识图谱的科学假设生成流水线，通过异构图神经网络筛选候选数据集配对，再由三智能体LLM流水线生成并评估结构化研究假设，最终在1475个数据集中产出160个跨领域假设，并揭示了单评判LLM评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的科学假设生成大多依赖非结构化文献和自由形式的文本主张，缺乏与结构化领域知识的紧密耦合。本研究旨在将假设生成直接锚定在NASA地球观测知识图谱上，以发现科学上合理但尚未被探索的数据集组合。

Method: 系统采用三阶段流水线：(1) 在历史共现关系上训练异构图神经网络（Heterogeneous GNN），对候选数据集配对进行排序；(2) 三智能体LLM流水线分别负责筛选、生成和评估结构化研究假设；(3) 通过2×2×2析因实验设计，在GPT-5.2和Claude Sonnet 4.6上评估排序稳定性和评分偏差。

Result: 应用于1475个NASA数据集，共生成了160个假设，覆盖生态水文学、冰川学、气溶胶-云相互作用、植被物候学和平流层化学等多个领域。模型预测的新颖数据集配对的合理性评分接近文献中已知共现配对，表明系统能有效发现科学上一致但尚未探索的组合。析因实验显示，不同模型下假设排名保持稳定，但绝对分数高度依赖于评判模型的身份。

Conclusion: 将结构化知识图谱与LLM相结合是增强科学假设生成严谨性的有效途径。然而，单评判LLM评估存在显著偏差，未来应采用多评判或多维度评估策略以提升评估可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models have recently been explored for scientific hypothesis generation, but most prior work relies on unstructured literature and free-form textual claims. We present a pipeline for Earth observation that grounds hypothesis generation directly in the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph. A heterogeneous graph neural network trained on historical co-usage relations ranks candidate dataset pairings, and a three-agent LLM pipeline filters, generates, and evaluates structured research hypotheses. Applied to 1,475 NASA datasets, the system produces 160 hypotheses spanning multiple Earth-science domains, including ecohydrology, glaciology, aerosol--cloud interactions, vegetation phenology, and stratospheric chemistry. Model-predicted novel dataset pairings are rated nearly as plausible as held-out real co-usages from the literature, indicating that the pipeline surfaces scientifically coherent yet unexplored combinations. A 2*2*2 factorial experiment across GPT-5.2 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows that hypothesis rankings remain stable, while absolute scores depend strongly on judge identity, highlighting limitations of single-judge LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [208] [Hawk: Harnessing Hardware-Aware Knowledge for High-Performance NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01590)
*Junyi Wen,Ruiyan Zhuang,Yongjia Xu,Pengtu Li,Rui Zou,Hongyi Chen,Chingman Wan,Puxu Yang,Wuhui Chen,Yanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hawk 是一个无需训练的三模块框架，通过运行时知识合成、瓶颈感知检索和效果驱动蒸馏，将大语言模型在 NPU 内核生成上的准确率从 49.4% 提升至 80.0%，并实现最高 2.2 倍执行加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在生成 NPU 高性能内核时因缺乏硬件特定先验知识而灾难性失败——直接移植相似内核代码虽能通过编译，但会因违反底层硬件约束而导致运行时崩溃和性能退化。

Method: 提出 Hawk 框架，包含三大核心模块：(1) 运行时知识合成模块，采用"三部分可执行知识表示"将错误上下文与可执行语义内在地耦合；(2) 瓶颈感知知识检索模块，实现二维检索范式，将查询投影到正交的语法空间和硬件对齐的语义空间；(3) 效果驱动知识蒸馏模块，利用 LLM 驱动的语义仲裁，基于实际执行反馈持续剪除错误并合并冗余知识。

Result: 在真实 NPU 工作负载上，Hawk 将生成准确率从 49.4% 提升至 80.0%，相比最先进基线方法实现最高 2.2 倍执行加速。

Conclusion: Hawk 证明通过训练无关的硬件感知知识管理框架，能有效弥补 LLM 在 NPU 内核生成中的硬件先验知识鸿沟，为 NPU 内核开发自动化提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Developing high-performance kernels for Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is a critical industry bottleneck, requiring developers to manually navigate implicit hardware constraints and strict memory hierarchies. While large language models offer immense automation potential, they fail catastrophically on NPUs due to a fundamental lack of hardware-specific priors.
