Naively transplanting code snippets from similar NPU kernels may pass the compiler, but it consistently triggers runtime crashes and performance degradation by blindly violating underlying hardware constraints. To overcome this, we introduce Hawk, a training-free framework that harnesses hardware-aware knowledge through three core modules: (1) Run-Time Knowledge Synthesis Module, which employs a Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation to inherently couple the error context with executable semantics; (2) Bottleneck-Aware Knowledge Retrieval Module, which implements a 2D-Retrieval paradigm to project queries into orthogonal syntactic and hardware-aligned semantic spaces; and (3) Effect-Driven Knowledge Distillation Module, which leverages LLM-driven semantic arbitration to continuously distill the knowledge by pruning errors and consolidating redundancies based on the empirical execution feedback. Extensive evaluations on real-world NPU workloads demonstrate that Hawk elevates generation accuracy from 49.4% to 80.0%, while achieving up to a 2.2x execution speedup over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [209] [Safe and Adaptive Cloud Healing: Verifying LLM-Generated Recovery Plans with a Neural-Symbolic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01595)
*Junyan Tan,Haoran Lin,Siyuan Guo,Yichen Fang,Xinyue Luo,Tianyu Shen,Zeyu Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASE重新构想了故障自修复框架，将恢复任务转化为神经符号程序合成，利用LLM生成结构化恢复计划，结合神经符号世界模型验证可行性，并通过DRL训练的元提示优化器指导规划，实现了动态、上下文感知的恢复策略生成，在真实云故障数据上将恢复时间缩短40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽结合了LLM的语义理解和DRL的策略优化，但采用串行、松耦合架构，未能充分发挥LLM的生成与推理能力，因此需要一种更紧密集成的范式来提升云AI系统的故障自修复效率。

Method: 提出PASE（Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM作为计划合成引擎，从语义原语库生成结构化恢复计划；(2) 神经符号世界模型通过仿真验证计划可行性；(3) 元提示优化器通过DRL训练，学习生成最优提示来引导LLM的规划过程。三者形成"推理-规划-验证-适应"闭环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上的实验表明，PASE显著超越现有最优方法，平均系统恢复时间缩短超过40%，且在未知故障场景下的检测准确率有显著提升。

Conclusion: PASE通过将LLM推理、模型辅助验证和元学习引导三者统一，推动了自主系统管理的发展，展示了神经符号融合在故障自修复领域的优势。

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>


### [210] [SemHash-LLM: A Multi-Granularity Semantic Hashing Framework for Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01601)
*Xinyi Fang,Kejian Tong,Jiabei Liu,Tao Ning,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemHash LLM 是一个多粒度文档去重框架，融合语义投影哈希、注意力加权 MinHash、对比边界学习与选择性 LLM 裁决，通过级联过滤在保持语义等价的同时将神经验证成本降至 1% 以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文档去重需要在海量语料上高效运行，同时必须保持语义等价——即识别语义相同但表述不同的文档。现有方法难以兼顾效率与语义保真度，且在模板污染、短文本扰动、文档包含关系和病毒式片段等复杂场景下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出 SemHash LLM 多粒度框架：(1) 语义投影哈希——在蒸馏后的 LLM 嵌入空间中学习紧凑二值编码；(2) 注意力加权 MinHash——利用注意力权重抑制模板/ boilerplate 内容，突出信息密集区域；(3) 门控融合机制——整合字符级、token 级和文档级信号；(4) 级联过滤管线——逐级淘汰候选对以降低计算开销；(5) 对比边界学习与不确定性估计——自适应决策边界提升鲁棒性；(6) 选择性 LLM 裁决——仅对模糊样本触发神经网络验证。

Result: SemHash LLM 在重复检测质量上表现优异，且仅需不到 1% 的神经网络验证成本即可达到该性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过多粒度信号融合与级联过滤策略，SemHash LLM 在大规模文档去重任务中实现了效率与语义保真度的有效平衡，为海量语料的高质量去重提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large scale document deduplication must preserve semantic equivalence while remaining efficient over massive corpora. We present SemHash LLM, a multi granularity framework that unifies semantic projection hashing, attention weighted MinHash, contrastive boundary learning, and selective LLM based adjudication. The method combines character, token, and document level signals through gated fusion, then applies a cascaded filtering pipeline for efficient candidate reduction. Semantic projection hashing learns compact binary codes in distilled LLM embedding space, while attention weighted Min- Hash suppresses boilerplate and emphasizes informative content. Adaptive decision boundaries and uncertainty estimation further improve robustness across template pollution, short text perturbation, containment, and viral fragments. Experiments show that SemHash LLM achieves strong duplicate detection quality with less than one percent neural verification cost.

</details>


### [211] [Profit-Based Counterfactual Explanations for Product Improvement: A Case Study of Manga Sales in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01610)
*Keita Kinjo,Takeshi Ebina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于利润的反事实解释框架（PBCE），将反事实解释重新定义为利润最大化问题，消除了对预设目标值和距离函数的外生依赖，并用产品属性修改成本重新诠释了距离项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在回归场景下存在两个核心缺陷：（1）需要外生指定目标输出值，但目标设定的有效性缺乏保证；（2）距离函数的实际解释意义不足。此外，现有方法侧重于改变预测结果，而非优化决策目标，这与现实决策中追求显式目标最大化的需求相脱节。

Method: 将反事实解释建模为管理与营销情境下的利润最大化问题，提出PBCE框架。该框架以利润最大化作为主优化目标，从而免除了对目标值的外生设定；同时将距离项重新定义为修改产品属性所产生的成本，赋予其清晰的经济学含义。

Result: 论文提出的PBCE框架无需外生目标值即可直接生成反事实解释，且距离项具有明确的经济学解释（产品属性修改成本），更贴合实际管理决策场景。

Conclusion: PBCE框架通过将CE重构为利润最大化问题，弥补了现有方法在目标设定有效性和距离度量可解释性方面的不足，为管理决策场景下的反事实解释提供了更具实践价值的方法论。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation (CE) is widely used to enhance the interpretability of machine learning models and support data-driven decision-making based on model predictions. However, existing CE methods typically require two exogenously specified inputs: a desired output value (target) and a distance function that quantifies changes in explanatory variables. In regression settings, neither the validity of target specification nor the practical interpretation of the distance metric has been sufficiently addressed. Furthermore, most existing CE methods focus on altering predictions rather than optimizing a decision objective, even though real-world decision-making often requires explicit objective maximization. To address these limitations, we formulate CE as a profit maximization problem in management and marketing contexts and propose a framework termed profit-based counterfactual explanation (PBCE). PBCE eliminates the need for exogenous target specification by directly maximizing profit as the primary optimization objective. Concurrently, the distance term is reinterpreted as the cost of modifying product attributes, providing a clear and economically grounded interpretation.

</details>


### [212] [Scaling with Confidence: Calibrating Confidence of LLMs for Adaptive Test Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01612)
*Xuqing Yang,Yi Yuan,Shanzhe Lei,Xuhong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C3RL是一种新型强化学习算法，通过同时优化回答正确性和置信度校准来训练LLMs，解决了现有RL方法导致模型过度自信的问题。进一步提出基于置信度的自适应推理策略CAS，可在保持性能的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练LLMs的奖励设计仅关注回答正确性，忽视了对模型置信度表达准确性的激励，导致模型在不确定时过度自信地产生幻觉，校准性能严重下降。

Method: 提出C3RL算法，将正确性奖励、校准奖励和数据集引导的参考准确率奖励三者融合到RL框架中。基于C3RL产生的良好校准的置信度，进一步提出CAS策略——一种根据响应置信度动态分配计算资源的自适应推理时扩展方法。

Result: 在8个文本和多模态数据集上，C3RL在保持准确率的同时提升了校准性能，超越现有SOTA方法。CAS在域内和域外数据集上均超越多数投票方法，且推理预算最高降低12.33倍。

Conclusion: C3RL与CAS的协同作用为部署更可靠、资源高效的LLMs铺平了道路。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced their performance on reasoning and question-answering tasks. However, prevailing RL reward designs typically prioritize response correctness, neglecting to incentivize models to express their confidence accurately. This leads to a critical problem: performance gains are often accompanied by poor calibration between confidence and accuracy, misleading models to overconfidently hallucinate when uncertain. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{C}$orrectness and $\textbf{C}$onfidence $\textbf{C}$alibration $\textbf{R}$einforcement $\textbf{L}$earning ($\textbf{C3RL}$), a novel RL algorithm integrating correctness, calibration and dataset-informed reference accuracy rewards together. Comprehensive evaluation across 8 text and multimodal datasets demonstrates that C3RL enhances calibration without sacrificing accuracy, outperforming the current state-of-the-art method in both performance and calibration metrics. Utilizing the well-calibrated verbalized confidence from C3RL, we further introduce $\textbf{C}$onfidence-based $\textbf{A}$daptive Test Time $\textbf{S}$caling ($\textbf{CAS}$), an adjustable inference-time strategy that allocates computational resources based on response confidence. Experiments show that CAS surpasses majority voting on both in-domain and out-of-domain datasets while reducing the inference budget by up to 12.33 times. We believe the synergy of C3RL and CAS paves the way for deploying more reliable and resource-efficient LLMs. The code, data and models will be released.

</details>


### [213] [Spatial Support Matters: Geometry-Aware Graph Fusion for Rainfall Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01621)
*Low Jun Yu,Niramay Kachhadiya,Herath Mudiyanselage Viraj Vidura Herath,Sanka Rasnayaka,Lucy Amanda Marshall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的多支持异质图神经网络，将不同测量支持（点、线、网格）的降雨观测作为不同节点层，通过跨支持消息传递融合，实现精细尺度降雨场重建。在新加坡数据上RMSE降低23.2%，并揭示了多源融合的增益取决于测站间距与空间相关长度的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际降雨感知系统通过不兼容的空间支持进行观测：雨量计测点、微波链路测路径、雷达/卫星测网格区域。现有异质图方法仅在特征空间融合这些源，赋予各自嵌入却丢弃了其几何支撑信息，导致无法有效利用不同观测的几何约束。

Method: 提出几何感知多支持异质图神经网络：将每种观测按支持类型（0D点、1D线、2D网格）建模为独立节点层，通过跨支持消息传递进行融合，最终在点支持预测层重建降雨场。采用归纳式掩码节点公式，将预测分辨率与感知分辨率解耦，允许同一模型在用户自定义目标位置或显示网格上重建场。

Result: 在新加坡数据上，相较经典反距离加权插值基线RMSE降低23.2%，并一致优于卷积融合和支持无关异质图等神经架构。在悉尼数据的泛化研究中，多支持融合的增益取决于雨量计间距与降雨场空间相关长度的关系：当场被欠采样（相对其相关长度）时融合收益最大，当场已被充分分辨时收益甚微。

Conclusion: 几何感知的多支持融合方法能有效整合不同空间支持的降雨观测，显著提升降雨场重建精度。融合的实际价值取决于观测密度与场空间相关长度的匹配程度，这为实际部署提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Fine-scale rainfall reconstruction is critical for urban flood modeling, but real rainfall sensing systems observe the field through incompatible spatial supports: gauges measure points, microwave links measure paths, and radar/satellite products measure gridded areas. These differences in measurement support impose geometrically distinct constraints on the rainfall field, yet existing heterogeneous graph approaches reconcile such sources in feature space, giving each its own embedding while discarding the geometry of its support. We propose a geometry-aware multi-support heterogeneous graph neural network that represents each observation according to its support type (0D point, 1D line, or 2D grid) as a distinct node layer, and fuses them through cross-support message passing into a point-support prediction layer from which the field is reconstructed. An inductive masked-node formulation decouples prediction resolution from sensing resolution, allowing the same trained model to reconstruct the field at user-defined target locations or display grids. On Singapore data, the proposed method reduces RMSE by 23.2\% over the classical interpolation baseline, inverse-distance weighting, and consistently outperforms other neural architectures such as convolutional fusion and support-agnostic heterogeneous graph baselines. A generalization study using data from Sydney, Australia lets us characterize when multi-support fusion helps: the available skill appears to depend on gauge spacing relative to the spatial correlation length of the field, so fusion delivers the largest gains where the field is under-sampled relative to its correlation length and little when it is already resolved. Code and models will be open-sourced upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [214] [Autonomous discovery of traffic laws with AI traffic scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01639)
*Xingyuan Dai,Yue Liu,Xiaoyan Gong,Qinghai Miao,Junyou Shang,Yutong Wang,Chao Guo,Yonglin Tian,Yizhang Chai,Chao Xiang,Yisheng Lv,Fei-Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrafficSci 是一个智能体 AI 系统，它将交通规律的发现形式化为一个包含证据界定、批判-判断式假设归纳和观测-干预验证的迭代可审计工作流，在四个案例研究中重新发现了三条已知交通规律，并识别出一条此前未报道的城市驾驶行为内在时间记忆尺度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交通普适规律的发现仍依赖专家驱动，难以从异构观测证据中识别候选规律或通过干预实验验证。已有自主 AI 系统虽在受控实验室环境中推动了科学发现，但将其扩展到复杂交通领域仍面临挑战。

Method: TrafficSci 将交通规律发现构建为一个迭代、可审计的工作流，核心包括：(1) 证据界定——从异构数据中划定分析范围；(2) 批判-判断式假设归纳——引入评判机制对候选假设进行筛选和归纳；(3) 观测-干预双重验证——结合观测数据验证与干预实验确认规律的稳健性和因果性。

Result: 在涵盖人口、网络、控制和轨迹四个尺度的案例研究中，TrafficSci 自主重新发现了三条已确立的交通规律（分别对应不同尺度），并识别出一条此前未报道的规律——城市驾驶行为中存在内在时间记忆尺度，该规律在八个城市和两个轨迹数据集上具有统计一致性。

Conclusion: TrafficSci 为将 AI 驱动的科学发现从受控领域拓展到复杂城市系统提供了一条可行路径，展示了智能体 AI 在交通科学自动化发现中的潜力。

Abstract: Universal traffic laws describe recurrent patterns in congestion, mobility and driving behavior across cities, providing a scientific basis for transportation planning, management and control. Their discovery, however, remains expert-driven, requiring candidate regularities to be identified from heterogeneous observational evidence or validated through intervention experiments. Although autonomous artificial intelligence (AI) systems have advanced scientific discovery in controlled laboratory settings, extending them to complex transportation domains remains a challenge. Here we present TrafficSci, an agentic AI system that formulates traffic-law discovery as an iterative, auditable workflow integrating evidence scoping, critic-judge hypothesis induction, and observational-interventional validation. Across four case studies spanning population, network, control and trajectory scales, TrafficSci autonomously rediscovers three established traffic laws and identifies an unreported intrinsic temporal memory scale in urban driving behavior, statistically consistent across eight cities and two trajectory datasets. TrafficSci provides a route for extending AI-driven scientific discovery from controlled domains to complex urban systems.

</details>


### [215] [Diverse Evidence, Better Forecasts: Multi-Agent Deliberation Under Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01661)
*Yuante Li,Yicheng Tao,Kate Zhang,Taozhi Wang,Gefei Gu,Yaxin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有Multi-Agent预测系统让所有Agent共享相同信息，导致 deliberation 退化为羊群效应。本文提出通过设计信息不对称（将证据划分为公共子集与互斥的私有子集）来降低Agent间误差相关性，并基于此构建了InfoDelphi框架，在PolyGym基准上显著优于单Agent和现有Multi-Agent基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多Agent系统虽被广泛用于预测任务，但现有方法忽视了一个关键设计选择：每个Agent接收什么信息。当所有Agent获得相同证据时，deliberation会退化为羊群效应（herding），而非真正的信念修正，导致多Agent系统相比单Agent几乎没有提升。作者将此识别为根本性缺口，旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出"设计的信息不对称"（designed information asymmetry）：将证据划分为共享的公共子集（public）和互斥的私有子集（disjoint private），使每个Agent持有只能通过deliberation传递给他人的独占知识。理论上证明该分解可降低Agent间误差相关性。基于此思路构建InfoDelphi框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）相关性感知的证据路由（relevance-aware evidence routing）；（2）基于理由的迭代deliberation（rationale-based iterative deliberation）；（3）置信度加权聚合（confidence-weighted aggregation）。

Result: 在PolyGym基准（包含375个来自真实世界预测市场的二元预测问题）上，InfoDelphi在Brier Score上相比最强单Agent和多Agent基线提升12–18%，准确率提升4–8个百分点。消融实验证实，移除信息不对称会消除大部分deliberation收益，确立了输入多样性是多Agent有效推理的关键使能因素。

Conclusion: 输入多样性（而非单纯的Agent数量或交互轮次）是多Agent系统实现有效集体推理的核心驱动力。设计的信息不对称是一种通用原则，可推广至其他需要多Agent协作的复杂推理场景。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly used for forecasting future events, as deliberation among multiple LLMs is believed to improve reasoning and calibration. Yet existing approaches overlook a critical design choice: what information each agent receives. When all agents are given identical evidence, deliberation collapses into herding rather than genuine belief revision, leaving multi-agent systems little better than a single agent. We identify this as a fundamental gap and propose designed information asymmetry to close it: by partitioning evidence into shared public and disjoint private subsets, each agent holds exclusive knowledge that can only reach others through deliberation. We theoretically show that this decomposition reduces inter-agent error correlation, and instantiate it in InfoDelphi, a framework combining relevance-aware evidence routing, rationale-based iterative deliberation, and confidence-weighted aggregation. On PolyGym, a benchmark of 375 binary forecasting questions derived from real-world prediction markets, InfoDelphi outperforms the strongest single-agent and multi-agent baselines by 12--18% in Brier score and 4--8 percentage points in accuracy. More detailed experiments confirm that removing information asymmetry eliminates most deliberation gains, establishing diversity of input as the key enabler of effective multi-agent reasoning.

</details>


### [216] [Separating Expert Retention from Autonomous Source Inference in Raw-ECG-Replay-Free Continual ECG Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01674)
*Yufan Lu,Xinhui Liu,Chenyang Xu,Yuxi Zhou,Hao Wang,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ECGBank，一种基于冻结ECGFounder特征的多源心电图增量学习框架。每个新数据源添加一个平衡softmax线性专家，并利用保留的训练特征和域标签训练轻量路由器进行无源ID推理。验证校准的top-2边缘融合策略用于提升路由鲁棒性。实验表明源感知专家选择与离线参考性能接近，而自主源推理仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源心电图模型部署中，早期原始ECG数据可能因隐私或存储限制无法保留或回放，需要增量纳入新数据源。冻结预训练骨干并分配孤立分类器可避免参数干扰，但在源元数据不可用时仍需自动选择专家。本文旨在研究这一无源ID场景下的专家路由问题。

Method: 基于冻结的ECGFounder 1024维特征构建增量专家库（ECGBank）。每个新域添加一个平衡softmax线性专家。轻量路由器仅在保留的训练特征和已见源域标签上拟合。推理时采用验证校准的边缘规则，融合最可能的前两名专家（top-2 margin fusion）而非硬路由到单一专家。方法不重放原始ECG，但保留冻结特征用于路由器更新，非完全无记忆。

Result: 在CPSC、PTB-XL、Georgia和Chapman-Shaoxing四个数据集上：(1)源感知专家选择Macro-F1达0.7915±0.0036，与离线独立头参考0.7885±0.0009接近；(2)无源ID时MLP路由器达0.7756±0.0027，top-2边缘融合达0.7782±0.0022，提升仅+0.0026且95%置信区间含零；(3)三种域顺序下top-2到oracle的差距为0.0111–0.0133，表明自主源推理是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 冻结骨干+增量专家库策略在多源ECG分类中有效，源感知性能接近离线联合训练水平。但无源元数据时路由器性能存在可量化差距，自主源推理仍是限制因素。Top-2融合相比硬路由增益有限，未来需改进路由器设计以缩小与oracle的差距。

Abstract: In multi-source ECG deployment, models may need to incorporate new data sources when earlier raw ECGs cannot be retained or replayed. Freezing a pretrained backbone and assigning each source an isolated classifier prevents parameter interference, but deployment still requires selecting an expert when source metadata are unavailable. We study this distinction through \ours{}, an incremental expert bank built on frozen 1024-dimensional ECGFounder features. Each arriving domain adds a balanced-softmax linear expert, while a lightweight router is fitted only on retained training features and domain labels from sources observed so far. A validation-calibrated margin rule fuses the two most likely experts instead of committing to a single routed expert.
  On CPSC, PTB-XL, Georgia, and Chapman-Shaoxing, source-aware expert selection reaches $0.7915\pm0.0036$ Macro-F1 and a matched offline independent-head reference reaches $0.7885\pm0.0009$, supporting strong source-aware expert retention. Without source IDs, an MLP router reaches $0.7756\pm0.0027$ and top-2 margin fusion reaches $0.7782\pm0.0022$. The top-2 gain over hard MLP routing is small ($+0.0026$), with a 95\% confidence interval from paired bootstrap that includes zero. Across three domain orders, the top-2-to-oracle gap remains $0.0111$--$0.0133$, identifying autonomous source inference as the main remaining bottleneck. No raw ECGs are replayed, but frozen training features are retained for router updates; the method is therefore not memory-free.

</details>


### [217] [Epistemic Goggles: A Pretrained Module that Induces an Epistemic Frame via Gradient Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01690)
*Joshua Penman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Goggles 模块，通过在微调梯度层面进行干预（而非修改训练数据），解决语言模型在标注为虚构的文档上训练后仍会"相信"文档核心主张的"否定忽视"（Negation Neglect）问题。Goggles 能为模型赋予特定的认知框架，使其在未见过的文档上也能有效识别虚构内容，准确率从约 9% 提升至约 91%，且不影响模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在微调时存在"否定忽视"现象：即使文档被明确标注为虚构，模型仍然会吸收并相信文档中的核心主张（准确率仅约 9%）。这揭示了当前微调范式在控制模型对训练数据认知立场方面的根本缺陷，亟需一种在模型学习机制层面而非数据层面施加认知框架的方法。

Method: 提出 Goggles，一个可学习的梯度干预模块。在监督微调过程中，Goggles 对 LLM 的 LoRA 梯度进行编辑，为文档内容施加选定的"认知框架"（epistemic frame），即模型对阅读内容性质的立场。Goggles 针对给定的基座模型、框架和 LoRA 配置仅需训练一次，随后可冻结应用于从未见过的文档。该方法支持多种认知框架（如"虚构内容"、"AI 安全评估的一部分"等）。

Result: 经过 Goggles 训练后，模型在无虚构标注的文档上识别虚构内容的准确率约 91%（基线仅约 9%），同时 GPQA 和 TruthfulQA 等通用能力指标匹配或超过基线。Goggles 施加的认知框架具有持久性：在后续持续微调将模型推向原始主张时，Goggles 的框架仍然保持，而此前其他干预方法则会失效回退。

Conclusion: Goggles 通过在梯度层面而非数据层面进行干预，为语言模型训练提供了一条新路径：可以在已知存在对齐问题的数据上进行训练，而不会吸收这些数据所展示的有害行为，对 AI 安全训练具有重要启示。

Abstract: Finetuning a language model on documents that are explicitly annotated as fictional results in a model that still actually believes the documents' core claims, an effect known as Negation Neglect. In our evaluations, models trained on documents prefixed and suffixed with such annotations correctly identify the relevant claims as fictional only about 9% of the time. To address this, we introduce Goggles, a learned module that intervenes on the finetuning gradient rather than the data. During supervised finetuning, a Goggles module edits the gradients an LLM LoRA receives, imparting a chosen epistemic frame (the stance the model takes toward the nature of what it reads) to whatever the documents teach. A Goggles instance is trained once for a given base model, frame, and LoRA configuration, then applied frozen to documents it was never trained on. Trained through Goggles on those same documents, now carrying no fictional annotation, the model flags the content as fictional roughly 91% of the time, while preserving capability (GPQA and TruthfulQA match or exceed baseline). The same architecture supports other frames: a Goggles instance can be trained to treat documents as "part of an AI safety evaluation by Redwood Research" rather than simply as fiction. The imparted frame persists under continued finetuning that pushes back toward the claim, where prior interventions revert. Goggles suggests a path toward training language models on known-misaligned data without absorbing the behaviors that data demonstrates.

</details>


### [218] [COMFYCLAW: Self-Evolving Skill Harnesses for Image Generation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01709)
*Zongxia Li,Dawei Liu,Fuxiao Liu,Yuhang Zhou,Xiyang Wu,Jingxi Chen,Jing Xie,Xiaomin Wu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMFYCLAW 是一个面向 ComfyUI 工作流的智能体技能演化框架，将工作流构建形式化为类型化图编辑，并利用 VLM 验证器将视觉失败转化为可操作的修复建议。通过渐进式技能库演化，将历史运行中的轨迹、错误和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的 Agent Skills，在图像生成任务中显著提升了智能体的可靠性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体工作流变得重复和领域特定化，智能体需要具备记忆能力以回顾以往的工作流模式、执行约束和用户偏好。然而现有方法缺乏有效的机制来积累和复用历史经验，导致在重复性视觉工作流构建中可靠性和性能不足。

Method: 1. 将工作流构建形式化为类型化图编辑（typed graph editing），并围绕构建阶段组织工具；2. 自动回退无效编辑；3. 使用区域级 VLM 验证器将视觉失败转化为可操作的修复建议；4. 构建渐进式披露的技能库，将历史轨迹、执行错误和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的 Agent Skills（技能演化机制）。

Result: 在四个基准划分、三种智能体模型和两种图像骨干网络上，COMFYCLAW 在所有六种智能体配置中取得了最佳的图像生成平均评估分数，优于无技能演化的仅验证器基线。人类标注结果进一步表明，标注者更偏好具备技能演化的 COMFYCLAW 变体。

Conclusion: 技能演化是提升智能体在重复性视觉工作流构建中可靠性和性能的有效机制。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly used to construct workflows and assist humans in completing recurring tasks more efficiently. As these workflows become repeated and domain-specific, agent memory and reusable skills become increasingly important: agents should be able to recall workflow patterns, execution constraints, and user preferences from previous runs. We study this problem in workflow-based image generation and introduce COMFYCLAW, an agentic skill evolution harness for controlling ComfyUI workflows. COMFYCLAW formulates workflow construction as typed graph editing, exposes tools organized by construction stage, automatically reverts invalid edits, and uses a region-level vision-language model (VLM) verifier to translate visual failures into actionable repair suggestions. The framework further evolves a progressively disclosed skill library, where trajectories, execution errors, and verifier feedback from previous runs are distilled into reusable Agent Skills. Across four benchmark splits, three agent models, and two image backbones, COMFYCLAW achieves the best average image-generation evaluation score across all six agent configurations, outperforming a verifier-only baseline without skill evolution. Human annotations further show that annotators prefer COMFYCLAW over variants without skill evolution. Our results suggest that skill evolution is an effective mechanism for improving agent reliability and performance in recurring visual workflow construction.

</details>


### [219] [Generic Expert Coverage for Pruning SparseMixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01710)
*Yongqin Zeng,Sicheng Pan,Jiale Wang,Hai-tao Zheng,Hong-Gee Kim,Chunxia Ma,XiuTeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于覆盖感知的MoE专家剪枝方法——Generic TB-Coverage，该方法仅使用通用文本语料（WikiText2和C4）进行校准，通过在多语料上分别评估专家效用并施加固定预算的覆盖规则来保留高价值专家，避免传统单一重要性评分带来的偏差。在Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B和DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base上，该方法在25%/50%/75%保留率下均优于随机剪枝、REAP和ExpertSparsity，尤其在大幅剪枝时效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE专家剪枝方法通常依赖单一聚合的重要性评分来选择保留专家，这会导致被保留的专家组合偏向于占主导地位的校准数据模式，忽视那些对通用知识重要但在主导模式中不突出的专家。此外，现有方法多依赖下游任务数据进行校准，缺乏仅使用通用文本语料的轻量剪枝方案。

Method: 提出Generic TB-Coverage方法：(1) 分别在多个通用文本语料（WikiText2和C4）上独立评估每个专家的效用，而非聚合为单一分数；(2) 对每个语料施加固定预算的覆盖规则，确保从每个语料中保留高效用专家；(3) 基于覆盖约束构造最终的剪枝掩码。整个过程无需下游任务校准数据。

Result: 在Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B和DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base上，于25%、50%、75%三种专家保留预算下评估：在六个常见zero-shot基准上的平均准确率超越随机剪枝、REAP和ExpertSparsity；在WikiText2和C4上的困惑度退化也更小。尤其在25%和50%的激进剪枝设置下增益最大。

Conclusion: 跨语料专家覆盖保留是一种有效的通用数据先验策略，用于MoE模型剪枝。该方法在固定剪枝预算且无下游校准数据的条件下稳定有效，证明了结构化冗余可以通过覆盖感知的剪枝策略有效消除。

Abstract: Sparsely activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models contain substantial structured redundancy among routed experts, but pruning them without downstream calibration data remains challenging. Existing expert-pruning methods typically rely on a single aggregated importance score, which can bias the retained set toward experts favored by dominant calibration patterns. We propose \textbf{Generic TB-Coverage}, a coverage-aware expert pruning method that uses only generic text corpora (WikiText2 and C4) for calibration. Instead of collapsing expert utility into one score, our method profiles per-expert utility separately on each corpus and enforces a fixed-budget coverage rule that preserves high-utility experts from each corpus before constructing the final pruning mask. Across Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B and DeepSeek-MoE-16B-Base at 25\%, 50\%, and 75\% retention budgets, our method improves average accuracy on six common zero-shot benchmarks over random pruning, REAP, and ExpertSparsity, while also reducing perplexity degradation on WikiText2 and C4. The gains are largest under aggressive pruning (25\% and 50\% retain), suggesting that preserving cross-corpus expert coverage is an effective generic-data prior for MoE pruning. Our improvements hold with fixed pruning budgets and no downstream calibration data.

</details>


### [220] [Distributionally Robust Listwise Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01715)
*Xudong Wu,Jian Qian,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究排序标签不确定性下的listwise偏好优化，提出基于点式总变差鲁棒的Plackett-Luce目标函数。该鲁棒损失可精确分解为名义PL损失加最坏情况PL修正项，且最坏排序可通过升序排列当前隐式得分得到（复杂度从O(K!)降至O(K log K)）。方法在离线固定列表和在线策略诱导设置下均具备收敛保证，实验表明其在干净标签下保持性能、噪声下提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒偏好优化主要关注成对监督，在数据集/提示/偏好对层面设计鲁棒性，但未研究listwise偏好优化中排序标签本身的不确定性——这种不确定性源于标注者不一致、近似平局、有损排序反馈或奖励模型噪声。

Method: 提出点式总变差鲁棒Plackett-Luce目标（TV-robust PL）：对候选列表条件下的排序标签直接进行鲁棒化。鲁棒损失精确分解为名义PL损失 + 最坏情况PL修正项；最坏情况排序通过将当前隐式得分按升序排列获得，将内层最大化从K!枚举降至O(K log K)。离线设置下目标为凸函数，用投影随机次梯度可达全局ε-次优；在线策略诱导设置下建立弱凸性和Moreau包络平稳性。

Result: 离线LLM对齐实验中，鲁棒修正在干净标签下基本保持性能，在噪声下显著提升鲁棒性。在线对齐实验中，该方法使基于奖励模型排序的候选扩展更可靠，在奖励模型和外部GPT-4评判指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: 该鲁棒listwise偏好优化方法通过高效的最坏情况排序机制，在离线与在线场景下均提供了理论收敛保证和实验验证的有效性，为排序标签不确定性下的语言模型对齐提供了新方案。

Abstract: Existing robust preference optimization for language-model alignment mainly studies pairwise supervision and places robustness at the dataset, prompt, or preference-pair level. We instead study listwise preference optimization under ranking-label uncertainty: given a prompt and a candidate list, the observed ranking over that list may be ambiguous due to annotator inconsistency, near-ties, lossy rankwise feedback, or reward-model noise. We propose a pointwise total-variation robust Plackett--Luce objective that directly robustifies the ranking label conditional on the candidate list. The robust loss admits an exact decomposition into the nominal PL loss plus a worst-case PL correction, and the worst-case ranking is obtained by sorting current implicit scores in ascending order, reducing the inner maximization from $K!$ enumeration to $O(K\log K)$. This tractable structure yields strong offline and online optimization guarantees. In the offline fixed-list setting, the robust objective is convex and projected stochastic subgradient reaches global $ε$-suboptimality with $O(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity. In the online policy-induced setting, where candidate lists are generated by the current policy, we establish weak convexity and $\widetilde O(ε^{-2})$ Moreau-envelope stationarity. Experiments in offline LLM alignment show that the proposed robust correction largely preserves performance under clean labels and improves robustness under noise. In online alignment, it makes reward-model-ranked candidate expansion more reliable and improves both reward-model and external GPT-4 judge metrics.

</details>


### [221] [DRL-CLBA: A Clean Label Backdoor Attack for Speech Classification via DDPG Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01729)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRL-CLBA，一种基于DDPG强化学习和深度音频隐写术的干净标签语音分类后门攻击，无需篡改标签即可将触发器嵌入源音频并在深度潜在空间中优化目标样本，有效绕过多种防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对语音分类的后门攻击中，样本特定攻击虽能绕过许多防御，但多依赖标签投毒（poisoned label attack），易被人工数据审查检测。因此需要一种无需修改标签的干净标签（clean label）后门攻击方法，以更隐蔽地暴露语音系统的安全漏洞。

Method: 作者提出DRL-CLBA框架：（1）利用深度音频隐写术将样本特定触发器嵌入源音频，在模型深层潜在空间中创建特征空间锚点；（2）采用DDPG（深度确定性策略梯度）强化学习框架，将目标样本向携带触发器的锚点进行优化，实现无需标签迁移的投毒。整个过程保持标签不变，从而实现干净标签攻击。

Result: 在三个数据集和四种不同DNN架构上的实验表明，DRL-CLBA实现了高攻击成功率，并能有效绕过微调、剪枝和频谱签名（spectral signature）等后门防御手段。

Conclusion: DRL-CLBA揭示了语音分类系统在面对干净标签后门攻击时的严重脆弱性，表明现有防御手段不足以应对此类基于强化学习和隐写术的隐蔽攻击，对语音控制系统安全具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Deep learning models for speech classification are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where malicious triggers cause misclassification at inference time. While sample-specific attacks can bypass many defenses, they often rely on poisoned label attack, making them detectable via manual data defense. In this paper, we propose DRL-CLBA, a novel clean label backdoor attack for speech classification that leverages Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) reinforcement learning. We also utilize deep audio steganography to embed sample-specific triggers into source audio, creating feature-space anchors. The proposed reinforcement learning framework effectively optimizes target samples toward trigger-bearing anchor points in the model's deep latent space, enabling label-migration-free poisoning of target samples. Experimental results across three datasets and four different DNNs demonstrate that DRL-CLBA achieves a high attack success rate, effectively bypassing some backdoor defenses. The attack demonstrates strong resistance against fine-tuning, pruning, and spectral signature defenses, exposing critical vulnerabilities in speech-controlled systems.

</details>


### [222] [Reformalization of the Jordan Curve Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01734)
*Simon Guilloud,Sankalp Gambhir,Samuel Chassot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了三个重新形式化（reformalization）案例研究——若尔当曲线定理从 Mizar 到 Lean、从 HOL Light 到 Lean、以及从 HOL Light 到 Agda 的转换，并分析了管道设计选择对实际重新形式化任务的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重新形式化（reformalization）——即将一个证明助手中的形式化开发转换为另一个证明助手的变体方法，以理解不同证明助手之间的形式化迁移可行性及其管道设计的关键因素。

Method: 以若尔当曲线定理（Jordan Curve Theorem）为目标，进行三个方向的重新形式化：Mizar → Lean、HOL Light → Lean、HOL Light → Agda。通过对比分析这些案例，识别和评估重新形式化管道中的设计选择。

Result: 成功完成了三个重新形式化任务，并对结果进行了系统分析，总结出在实际重新形式化管道中具有重要影响的设计决策要素。

Conclusion: 重新形式化是跨证明助手迁移形式化成果的可行方法，管道设计选择（如中间表示、映射策略等）对任务成效至关重要。

Abstract: We present a case study in reformalization, a variant of autoformalization in which the input proof is not natural language but a formal development in a different proof assistant. Concretely, we report three reformalizations of the Jordan Curve Theorem: from Mizar to Lean, from HOL Light to Lean, and from HOL Light to Agda. We analyse the results and identify pipeline design choices that matter for practical reformalization tasks.

</details>


### [223] [Meta-Benchmarks for Financial-Services LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01740)
*Blair Hudson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个元基准测试框架，将452个公开基准按O*NET工作活动和BIAN银行业务领域分类，通过乘法加权方案和成对Elo锦标赛，生成跨基准可比较的模型能力评分，以服务于金融机构的模型选型与治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开LLM排行榜仅优化全局平均性能，无法捕捉金融服务业特定认知需求（如文档合规推理、多轮客户交互），导致榜单领先模型在实际业务场景中可能表现不佳。

Method: 构建元基准测试框架：(1)将452个公开基准映射至41项O*NET通用工作活动和38个BIAN银行业务领域；(2)采用乘法加权方案（区分度×覆盖度×时效性），在滚动模型窗口上计算权重，自动抑制饱和的遗留测试；(3)以加权K因子进行成对Elo锦标赛，无需原始分数归一化即可产出跨基准可比的工作活动评分；(4)业务领域评分为其组成工作活动Elo的加权平均。

Result: 在截至2026年6月的公开快照上演示了该框架，覆盖25个组织的288个模型，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为面临模型选型和治理挑战的金融机构提供了一种可复现的、业务导向的模型评估方法，同时详细说明了方法论、分类体系、设计决策和局限性。

Abstract: Public LLM leaderboards optimise for global average performance and do not capture the specific cognitive demands of financial-services work: a model that leads on MMLU-Pro may underperform on document-grounded compliance reasoning, and a coding leader may handle multi-turn customer interactions poorly. We present a meta-benchmarking framework that organises 452 publicly reported benchmarks into 41 O*NET Generalized Work Activities and aggregates those into 38 BIAN banking business domains spanning sales, operations, risk, and support work. A multiplicative weighting scheme (discrimination x coverage x recency), computed over a rolling model window, rewards benchmarks that still separate the best models, are widely reported, and remain in active use, suppressing saturated legacy tests automatically. These weights scale the K-factor in a pairwise Elo tournament, producing cross-benchmark-comparable work-activity scores without raw score normalisation; business-domain scores are weighted averages of the constituent work-activity Elos. We demonstrate the framework on a point-in-time public snapshot covering 288 models across 25 organisations as of June 2026, and describe the methodology, full taxonomy, design decisions, and limitations with the aim of making the approach reproducible for institutions facing similar selection and governance challenges.

</details>


### [224] [Path-level Hindsight Instructions for Semantic Exploration in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01754)
*Sung June Kim,Sangpil Kim,Honglak Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Phi-Nav 是一种统一在线策略框架，通过事后推理将指令与智能体的实际探索轨迹对齐。它采用三阶段双监督循环：在线探索学习、事后指令合成、二次模仿学习，从而弥合在线策略中语义监督的缺口，在仅需少量专家演示的情况下取得有竞争力的导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略探索对训练鲁棒的视觉语言导航（VLN）智能体至关重要，但探索轨迹往往偏离专家演示，导致视觉流与原始语言指令之间出现语义不匹配。现有方法缺乏有效机制来为这些偏离的探索轨迹提供语义监督信号。

Method: 提出 Phi-Nav 框架，包含三阶段双监督循环：1）智能体在专家动作反馈指导下进行在线策略探索并采样轨迹；2）事后说话者（hindsight speaker）基于收集到的视觉观察合成路径级事后指令；3）智能体执行第二轮模仿学习，将合成轨迹-指令对作为额外专家演示。该过程将无语义标注的运动转化为密集训练信号。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 基准上，Phi-Nav 仅需现有基线方法一小部分专家演示即可取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 语义探索在 VLN 中不可或缺，Phi-Nav 为有限数据条件下训练具身智能体提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy exploration is a crucial component for training robust Vision-Language Navigation agents, as it exposes the policy to a broader state distribution. However, such exploration inevitably leads to trajectories that deviate from expert demonstrations, resulting in a semantic mismatch between the executed visual stream and the original language instruction. In this work, we address this challenge by introducing Phi-Nav, a unified on-policy framework that leverages hindsight reasoning to align instructions with the agent's actual exploratory journey. Specifically, Phi-Nav operates through a three-stage dual-supervision cycle: 1) the agent performs oracle-guided on-policy exploration, sampling a trajectory while learning from expert action feedback, 2) a hindsight speaker synthesizes a path-level hindsight instruction grounded in the collected visual observations, and 3) the agent conducts a second imitation pass, treating the synthesized trajectory-instruction pair as an additional expert demonstration. Through this process, Phi-Nav bridges the critical semantic supervision gap inherent in on-policy methods, transforming semantically unlabeled movement into dense training signals. Evaluations on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks show that Phi-Nav yields competitive performance while requiring only a fraction of the expert demonstrations used by current baselines. These results underscore the necessity of semantic exploration in VLN, positioning Phi-Nav as an effective solution for training embodied agents with limited data.

</details>


### [225] [Mastermind: Strategy-grounded Learning for Repository-Scale Vulnerability Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01764)
*Mingzhe Du,Luu Anh Tuan,Tianyi Wu,Renyang Liu,Zhijiang Guo,Dong Huang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mastermind框架，将策略学习与任务执行解耦：训练一个可复用的规划器来学习漏洞复现的高层策略，策略可跨不同执行器迁移，显著提升仓库级漏洞复现任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在仓库级漏洞复现任务中，即使单步操作正确，仍常因选择了错误的整体策略而失败。作者认为"策略"（而非完整动作轨迹）才是正确的学习单元——它足够紧凑可优化、足够具体可指导执行、足够稳定可存储复用。

Method: 提出Mastermind双循环框架：(1) 策略学习外循环——通过监督微调(SFT)和基于里程碑的分组相对策略优化(GRPO)训练一个可复用的规划器，学习漏洞复现策略，且规划器与执行器独立训练；(2) 经验内循环——维护任务局部策略记录，指导后续尝试。规划器训练不依赖特定执行器，可提升多个冻结执行器的表现。

Result: 在CyberGym数据集上（260训练任务/200测试任务），以GPT-5.5为冻结执行器时，Mastermind达到84.5%通过率，显著优于开放上下文(60.0%)、Best-of-8采样(63.0%)和迭代改进(77.0%)。同一规划器将GPT-5.4 mini从45.0%提升至60.0%，将GLM 5.1从58.5%提升至71.0%。

Conclusion: 学习高层策略是提升仓库级软件工程智能体的有效且可迁移的机制。将策略与执行分离，使策略学习成果能够跨模型迁移，为构建更强大的SE智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Repository-level vulnerability reproduction is a demanding software engineering (SE) task: an agent must inspect a codebase, infer the input grammar that reaches a vulnerable path, construct a proof-of-conceptv(PoC), and verify that the crash disappears on the patched build. Recent LLM agents can often execute these steps when the approach is correct, yet they still fail by choosing the wrong strategy. This paper argues that strategy, rather than the full action trajectory, is the right learning unit for such SE agents: it is compact enough to optimize, concrete enough to guide execution, and stable enough to store and reuse across attempts. We present Mastermind, a dual-loop framework that separates transferable strategy learning from task-specific experience. A trainable planner learns reusable vulnerability-reproduction strategies through SFT and milestone-based GRPO, while an experience loop maintains task-local strategy records that guide subsequent attempts. The planner is trained independently of the executor, allowing strategy learning to improve multiple frozen executors without modifying their action-generation capability. We evaluate Mastermind on CyberGym using 260 training tasks and 200 held-out evaluation tasks. With GPT-5.5 as the frozen executor, Mastermind achieves an 84.5% pass rate, outperforming open-book PoC context (60.0%), Best-of-8 sampling (63.0%), and iterative improvement (77.0%). The same planner also improves GPT-5.4 mini and GLM~5.1 from 45.0% and 58.5% to 60.0% and 71.0%. These results demonstrate that learning high-level strategies is an effective and transferable mechanism for improving repository-scale SE agents.

</details>


### [226] [SimWorlds: A Multi-Agent System for Dynamic 3D Scene Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01766)
*Chunjiang Liu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Haoyu Chen,Yizhou Zhao,Ming-Hsuan Yang,László A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimWorlds 是一个多智能体框架，用于从文本生成动态、可编辑的4D场景（包含流体、粒子、刚体、铰接机构等物理效果），通过规划-编码-审查工作流、分层场景协议和运行时状态检查工具解决了空间布局与多物理求解器协调及运动验证两大挑战，并在新基准4DBuildBench上超越了现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D场景系统仅关注静态输出，而能产生流体流动、粒子发射、刚体碰撞和铰接机构运动的动态4D场景仍未被探索。这限制了两个重要应用：可编辑的动态内容创作，以及为视频生成和具身AI提供物理真实感的训练数据。动态场景面临两大独特挑战——需要协调空间布局、多物理求解器、时间序列、相机和光照；且通过渲染视频验证运动正确性比静态图像判断困难得多。

Method: 提出SimWorlds多智能体框架，核心组件包括：(1) 集成Blender特定程序化知识；(2) 规划器-编码器-审查器(planner-coder-reviewer)工作流，驱动固定的有序构建阶段序列；(3) 由确定性验证器强制执行的分层场景协议(layered scene protocol)；(4) 运行时状态检查工具套件，可捕获渲染图像无法揭示的机制故障。同时构建了4DBuildBench基准，用于评估生成场景的视觉保真度和物理一致性。

Result: 在4DBuildBench基准上的实验表明，SimWorlds在动态Blender生成任务上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: SimWorlds证明了通过多智能体协作框架可以有效解决文本到动态4D场景生成的复杂挑战，为可编辑内容创作和物理真实训练数据生成开辟了新路径。分层场景协议与运行时检查工具的引入，使得仅依赖渲染图像难以发现的物理错误可以被系统性地检测和纠正。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used to translate natural language into 3D scenes in a procedural way, but existing systems focus on static output. Dynamic 4D scenes from text alone, in which liquids flow, particles emit, rigid bodies cascade, and articulated mechanisms move, remain largely unexplored despite their value as editable content and as physics-grounded training data for video generation and embodied AI. Two challenges set the dynamic case apart from static text-to-scene work: an agent must jointly coordinate spatial layout, multiple physics solvers, temporal sequencing, camera, and lighting in a single coherent scene, and verifying motion correctness from rendered video is fundamentally harder than judging a single image. We present SimWorlds: a multi-agent framework that produces dynamic, editable 4D scenes from text, with Blender-specific procedural knowledge, a planner-coder-reviewer workflow driving a fixed ordered sequence of construction stages, a layered scene protocol enforced by a deterministic verifier, and a runtime-state inspection tool suite that catches mechanism failures the rendered image cannot reveal. We also introduce 4DBuildBench, a benchmark for assessing both visual fidelity and physical consistency of the procedural dynamic 3D scenes generated from text prompts. Experiments show that SimWorlds outperforms prior dynamic Blender generation baselines.

</details>


### [227] [Repair the Amplifier, Not the Symptom: Stable World-Model Correction for Agent Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01767)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模Agent规划图中的故障修复问题，提出了WM-SAR（世界模型子图放大修复）方法，通过反向追溯放大误差的因果子图来精准定位修复目标。相比传统工程方法扫描可见症状并修复局部区域，WM-SAR在有限token预算下实现了接近全图稳定的修复效果，为LLM提供了更清晰的修复目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agent规划从短工具链转向包含数千甚至数万步的持久工作流，故障必然发生在大型规划图的内部节点而非孤立预测中。全图重放（replanning）既计算不现实又不可取：消耗大量上下文预算、让LLM暴露于大量无关症状、降低长上下文检索质量。因此亟需一种能够原位修复失败规划图的世界模型修正器。

Method: 本文比较了两类修正器：（1）工程方法：扫描节点和边，选择可疑局部区域，交由LLM修复。作者实现了多个强工程LLM修正器。（2）WM-SAR方法：不扫描可见症状，而是从子图放大（subgraph amplification）反向工作，识别持续放大错误的节点和边，仅将因果子图发送给LLM进行修复。

Result: 在图模拟和LLM修复实验中，WM-SAR在现实token预算下显著优于工程修正器，能够以紧凑的修复区域实现接近全图稳定，并为LLM提供了更清晰的修复目标。

Conclusion: WM-SAR通过因果子图放大逆向定位的方式，有效解决了大规模Agent规划图中的原位修复问题，证明精确的因果定位比盲目的局部扫描更高效、更节省上下文资源。

Abstract: As agent planning moves from short tool chains toward persistent workflows with thousands or tens of thousands of steps, failures will occur inside large planning graphs rather than in isolated predictions. Replanning the entire graph after every mistake is neither computationally realistic nor desirable: full-graph replay consumes large context budgets, exposes the LLM to many irrelevant symptoms, and can degrade long-context retrieval. This paper studies the missing component in such systems: a world-model corrector that repairs the failed planning graph in place. We compare two families of correctors. The first is the common engineering approach: scan nodes and edges, choose a suspicious local region, and ask an LLM to repair it. We implement strong engineering LLM correctors and find that they can help, especially when given very large contexts. The second family is our approach, WM-SAR (World-Model Subgraph Amplification Repair): instead of scanning for visible symptoms, it works backward from subgraph amplification, identifies the nodes and edges that keep re-amplifying error, and sends only that causal subgraph to the LLM. Across graph simulations and LLM repair experiments, WM-SAR substantially outperforms engineering correctors under realistic token budgets, achieves near-whole-graph stabilization with a compact region, and gives the LLM a cleaner repair target.

</details>


### [228] [Verifiable Knowledge Expansion through Retrieval-Grounded Formal Concept Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01773)
*Yujin Yang,Heejung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个检索增强的小语言模型框架，以形式概念分析（FCA）作为符号验证回路进行知识扩展，在罕见共济失调数据集上验证了该方法在关系抽取和蕴含推理上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体构建中需要判断哪些对象、属性和结构关系应被接受为有效知识，语言模型虽能从文本中提出这些结构，但其输出可能缺乏支持或存在不一致性，因此需要一个可验证、可解释的知识扩展机制。

Method: 提出检索增强的小语言模型（SLM）框架，将形式概念分析（FCA）作为符号验证回路：从种子属性出发，FCA在增长的形式背景上提出蕴含式；检索增强的SLM oracle对每条蕴含进行验证或返回反例，同时支持关联判断、一致性检查和属性提议，使所有决策可审查。

Result: 在基于Orphadata构建的罕见共济失调数据集上，10种子运行的关系F1为0.29–0.52，闭包基础的蕴含F1为0.22–0.30。更大种子集增加评估的蕴含数量且通常提升蕴含F1。蕴含得分较低反映了对推导蕴含的更严格评估。消融实验表明，在固定对象-属性设置下的关联判断可提升闭包基础的蕴含分数，但识别正例对象-属性对仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 检索增强的SLM与FCA符号验证回路的结合，为知识扩展提供了一种可解释、可验证的框架，但在精确识别正例关联方面仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Ontology construction requires deciding which objects, attributes, and structural relations should be accepted as valid knowledge. Language models can propose such structures from text, but their outputs can still be unsupported or inconsistent. This paper proposes a retrieval-augmented small language model (SLM) framework that uses formal concept analysis (FCA) as a symbolic verification loop for knowledge expansion. Starting from seed attributes, FCA proposes implications over a growing formal context. A retrieval-grounded SLM oracle then validates each implication or returns a counterexample. The oracle also supports incidence judgments, consistency checks, and attribute proposals, making accepted implications, counterexamples, contradictions, and corrections inspectable. In a rare ataxia setting constructed from Orphadata resources, retrieval-grounded 10-seed runs obtain relation F1 of 0.29-0.52 and closure-based implication F1 of 0.22-0.30. Larger seed sets increase the number of evaluated implications and often improve implication F1. The lower implication scores reflect a stricter evaluation of derived implications, where one missed or extra relation can affect several implication judgments. Ablations show that incidence judgments in a fixed object-attribute setting can improve closure-based implication scores. However, identifying positive object-attribute pairs remains difficult even when the candidate objects and attributes are fixed.

</details>


### [229] [Subliminal Clocks: Latent Time Modelling in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01774)
*Maximo Rulli,Thomas Fontanari,Simone Petruzzi,Federico Alvetreti,Giorgio Strano,Donato Crisostomi,Giorgos Nikolaou,Tommaso Mencattini,Andrea Santilli,Emanuele Rodolà,Simone Scardapane,Alessio Devoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散语言模型（DLM）虽然在训练时未显式以时间步为条件，但其残差流内部确实编码了与扩散时间步相关的潜在表征。该信号可通过探针跨层提取，且沿该子空间操控模型可系统性调节其"去噪进度"认知，影响置信度与熵。此外，该表征在激活空间中呈现结构化且可解释的几何特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）不同于标准扩散模型，训练时未显式以时间步为条件。这引发了一个核心问题：这些模型是否在内部表征了去噪进度？若存在，该信息如何被下游使用？理解这一问题对于揭示DLM的工作机制至关重要。

Method: 作者采用探针（probes）方法，跨层从DLM的残差流激活中解码与扩散时间步相关的信号；进一步通过沿着与推断时间步相关的低维子空间对模型进行"引导"（steering），操控其去噪进度认知；最后对该表征在激活空间中的几何结构进行分析，考察其结构化与可解释性。

Result: 1. 探针可跨层可靠地提取时间步信号，证明去噪进度可从内部激活中解码；2. 沿该低维子空间操控模型，可系统性调节其去噪进度认知，导致模型置信度和熵发生可预测变化；3. 该表征在激活空间中展现出结构化且可解释的几何特性。

Conclusion: DLM尽管未显式以时间步为条件，其内部残差流仍自发形成了关于扩散时间步的潜在表征。该表征不仅可被解码和操控，还具有清晰的几何结构，为理解DLM如何隐式处理去噪进度提供了机制性洞见。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Unlike standard diffusion-based approaches, DLMs are not explicitly conditioned on a timestep, raising a natural question: do these models internally represent denoising progress, and how is such information used downstream? In this work, we show that DLMs do in fact encode a latent representation related to the diffusion timestep within their residual streams. We find that this signal can be reliably extracted using probes across layers, indicating that denoising progress is decodable from internal activations. We further demonstrate that steering the model along a low-dimensional subspace associated with the inferred timestep allows us to systematically modulate its notion of denoising progress, leading to predictable changes in model confidence and entropy. Finally, we analyse the geometry of the identified representation, showing that it exhibits structured and interpretable properties in activation space, and shedding light on how such a signal is processed by these models.

</details>


### [230] [Safety Testing LLM Agents at Scale: From Risk Discovery to Evidence-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01793)
*Yunhao Feng,Ruixiao Lin,Ming Wen,Qinqin He,Yanming Guo,Yifan Ding,Yutao Wu,Jialuo Chen,Yunhao Chen,Xiaohu Du,Jianan Ma,Zixing Chen,Zhuoer Xu,Xingjun Ma,Xinhao Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vera 是一个端到端的自动化安全测试框架，将软件工程测试原则应用于非确定性 LLM agent。通过文献驱动的风险发现、组合式安全用例生成和自适应沙箱执行三阶段流水线，在四个主流 agent 框架上实现了高达 93.9% 的平均攻击成功率，揭示了当前 agent 系统的严重安全缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 安全测试存在两大痛点：(1) 依赖专家手工设计安全违规场景，扩展成本高；(2) 评估依赖硬编码规则，难以适应 agent 快速演进。需要一个可自动扩展、可维护的大规模安全测试基础设施。

Method: 三阶段自增强流水线：(1) 文献驱动探索——持续从文献中挖掘新兴风险，构建安全风险、攻击方法、工具执行环境三个维度的分类体系；(2) 组合式安全用例生成——跨维度组合生成可执行安全用例，每个用例包含具体安全目标、程序化构建的初始状态、基于可观测产物的确定性验证谓词；(3) 自适应执行——在隔离沙箱中运行异构 agent，控制 agent 根据运行时观察引导多轮交互，基于环境状态和工具调用证据（而非模型自报）进行验证判决。

Result: 在 OpenClaw、Hermes、Codex、Claude Code 四个生产级 agent 框架上评估：多通道攻击下平均攻击成功率达 93.9%。发布 Vera-Bench，包含 1600 个可执行安全用例，覆盖 124 个风险类别、三种执行环境。

Conclusion: 模块化、可执行的安全测试基础设施对于快速演进的 agent 系统的大规模、严格、可维护的安全评估是不可或缺的。Vera 证明了自动化组合式安全测试在此场景下的有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly perform autonomous actions through external tools, leading to complex and evolving safety risks. However, existing safety testing targets expert-designed safety violations, and the corresponding outcomes are evaluated by hard-coded rules, making them costly to extend as agents evolve. To this end, we present Vera, an end-to-end automated safety testing framework that instantiates software engineering testing principles for non-deterministic agents through a three-stage, self-reinforcing pipeline. First, a literature-driven exploration continuously discovers and structures emerging risks into taxonomies of safety risks, attack methods, and tool execution environments. Second, combinatorial composition across taxonomy dimensions produces executable safety cases, each specifying a concrete safety goal, a programmatically constructed initial state, and a deterministic verification predicate grounded in observable artifacts. Third, adaptive execution runs heterogeneous agents in isolated sandboxes where a control agent steers multi-turn interaction based on runtime observations, while evidence-grounded verifiers judge outcomes from environment state and tool-call evidence rather than model self-report. We evaluate Vera on four production agent frameworks (OpenClaw, Hermes, Codex, Claude Code), revealing substantial safety weaknesses, with average attack success rates reaching 93.9\% under multi-channel attacks; we also release Vera-Bench, comprising 1600 executable safety cases spanning 124 risk categories across three execution settings. These results indicate that modular, executable testing infrastructure is essential for rigorous and maintainable safety evaluation of rapidly evolving agentic systems at scale. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Yunhao-Feng/Vera.

</details>


### [231] [MMIR-TCM: Memory-Integrated Multimodal Inference and Retrieval for TCM Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01814)
*Lihui Luo,Joongwon Chae,Ziyan Chen,Yang Liu,Siyi Cheng,Weihan Gao,Zelin Zeng,Xiaoming Yin,Samaneh Beheshti Kashi,Dongmei Yu,Lian Zhang,Jing Sui,Zeming Liang,Jiansong Ji,Peter E. Lobie,Peiwu Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MMIR-TCM框架，结合多模态大语言模型、记忆增强分割与检索增强生成，模拟中医舌诊推理流程，并引入MedTCM数据集与TDEU评估指标，在中医临床决策任务上超越GPT-4o等主流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中医舌诊面临主观性强、可复现性差的问题，多模态AI在辨证论治和方剂生成中的应用受限于视觉舌象特征与文本推理之间的语义鸿沟，以及大规模标准化数据集的缺失。

Method: 提出MMIR-TCM三阶段框架：(1) 无需训练的Memory-SAM模块实现鲁棒舌象分割提取；(2) 微调的Qwen3-VL模型生成结构化舌诊描述；(3) 基于Qwen3的RAG组件生成有证据支撑的临床决策。同时构建了大规模多模态数据集MedTCM，并开发了融合语义理解与诊断重要性的领域专用评估指标TDEU。

Result: MMIR-TCM在中医临床诊断准确性上显著优于GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash等主流模型，TDEU指标能够捕捉现有通用指标无法反映的临床准确性。

Conclusion: MMIR-TCM框架有效弥合了中医舌诊中视觉与文本模态间的语义鸿沟，通过记忆增强分割和检索增强生成实现了可解释、有据可依的临床决策支持，MedTCM数据集和TDEU指标为中医AI研究提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) diagnosis, particularly through tongue inspection, faces persistent challenges in subjectivity and reproducibility. The application of multimodal artificial intelligence to TCM clinical tasks, such as syndrome differentiation and prescription generation, is significantly hampered by the semantic gap between visual tongue features and textual reasoning, as well as the lack of large-scale, standardized datasets. To address these challenges, we introduce MMIR-TCM, a novel framework that emulates the diagnostic process of TCM experts by integrating multimodal large language model(MLLM) with memory-augmented segmentation and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Employing a three-stage architecture, MMIR-TCM integrates a training-free Memory-SAM module for robust tongue extraction, a fine-tuned Qwen3-VL model for structured tongue diagnosis generation, and a Qwen3-based RAG component for evidence-grounded clinical decision support generation. The framework was developed and validated using MedTCM, a new large-scale multimodal dataset that we introduce specifically for advanced TCM research. To properly evaluate our framework's clinical accuracy, which existing metrics fail to capture, we also developed TDEU, a domain-specific evaluation metric incorporating semantic understanding and diagnostic importance. Our comprehensive experiments demonstrate that MMIR-TCM significantly outperforms leading models, including GPT-4o and Gemini 2.5 Flash.

</details>


### [232] [Pre-Flight: A Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models on Aviation Operational Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01829)
*Alex Brooker,Tim Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pre-Flight 是一个面向航空领域专业知识的开源基准测试，包含300道多选题，覆盖机场地面运营、ICAO/FAA法规及复杂操作场景。评测显示，即便最强模型准确率仅82.7%，与专家水平（约95%）存在显著且持续的差距，凸显了在航空非安全关键运营中部署生成式AI前进行领域专项评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用基准无法衡量大语言模型在航空特定运营知识上的安全性和正确性推理能力。航空领域具有高风险、强监管的特性，模型若缺乏专业准确性可能导致严重后果。因此，需要构建一个专门针对航空运营知识的评估基准，以填补这一关键空白。

Method: 构建包含300道多选题的Pre-Flight基准，题目来源于国际标准（ICAO、FAA法规）和机场地面运营材料，由空中交通管理、地面运营和商业飞行背景的从业者编写并审校。采用Inspect评估框架，以标准多选题协议下的准确率作为评分标准，并对当代商用和开源模型进行评测，同时以滚动方式维护排行榜。以一次会议上航空专业人员的小样本测验结果（约95%准确率）作为非正式专家参考基准。

Result: 表现最强的模型（2026年发布）准确率仅为82.7%，相较2025年初约75%的水平仅有渐进式提升，与约95%的专家参考水平之间存在约12个百分点的显著且持久差距。

Conclusion: 在航空非安全关键运营中负责任地部署生成式AI，领域专项评估是必要前提。当前大语言模型在航空专业知识上的可靠性仍远未达到专家水平，该差距需要在部署前得到充分重视和弥合。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for aviation business operations, from documentation and training generation to customer facing assistants. General purpose benchmarks do not measure whether a model reasons safely and correctly about aviation specific operational knowledge, and the high stakes, regulated nature of the domain makes that gap consequential. We present Pre-Flight, an open source benchmark of 300 multiple choice questions drawn from international standards and airport ground operations material, covering international airport ground operations, ICAO and US FAA regulations, aviation general knowledge and complex operational scenarios. Questions were authored and reviewed by practitioners with experience in air traffic management, ground operations and commercial flying. We evaluate a range of contemporary commercial and open weight models using the Inspect evaluation framework, scoring by accuracy under a standard multiple choice protocol, and we maintain the leaderboard on a rolling basis as new models are released. Against an informal expert reference of around 95%, obtained from a low sample quiz of aviation professionals at a conference, even the strongest model evaluated (released in 2026) reaches 82.7%, having improved only gradually from roughly 75% in early 2025. A substantial and persistent gap below expert level reliability therefore remains. We release the dataset, the evaluation harness and the results, and the benchmark is available within the community evaluations package distributed with inspect_evals. We argue that domain specific evaluation of this kind is a necessary precondition for responsible deployment of generative AI in non safety critical aviation operations.

</details>


### [233] [Actual causality in fault trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01840)
*Georgiana Caltais,Milan Lopuhaä-Zwakenberg,Mariëlle Stoelinga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从Halpern & Pearl的实际因果关系理论视角研究故障树，将故障树的极小割集与实际原因联系起来，给出了各种实际因果概念在故障树图结构与逻辑结构中的完整分类，使故障树不仅能回答"什么可能出错"，还能回答"为什么出错了"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障树传统上用于回答"什么可能出错"（通过极小割集分析进行风险评估），但无法回答故障诊断中的核心问题——"为什么出错了"。作者试图将实际因果关系理论与故障树结合，填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Halpern & Pearl的实际因果关系理论框架，对故障树的图结构（节点、边的拓扑关系）和逻辑结构（AND/OR门的布尔逻辑）进行形式化分析，将不同的实际因果概念（如实际原因、充分原因等）在故障树中进行完整分类，并建立极小割集与实际原因之间的对应关系。

Result: 给出了每种实际因果概念在故障树图结构和逻辑结构上的完整分类；证明了极小割集可以自然地产生实际原因，从而将故障树的结构化风险分析与因果推理建立了严格的桥梁。

Conclusion: 故障树不仅可以用于前向的风险评估，还可以通过实际因果关系理论用于后向的故障诊断，拓展了故障树的分析能力。极小割集为解释"为什么发生故障"提供了因果基础。

Abstract: Fault trees are a widely used as effective risk models for complex systems, answering the question "what can go wrong?", especially through minimal cut set analysis. We study fault trees from the perspective of Halpern & Pearl's theory of actual causality. This allows us to use fault trees to answer the question "why has it gone wrong?", which is fundamental to failure diagnostics. We give a complete classification of each of the different notions of actual causality in terms of the fault tree's graph structure and logical structure, and show how minimal cut sets give rise to actual causes.

</details>


### [234] [CLAP: Closed-Loop Training, Evaluation, and Release Control for Domain Agent Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01846)
*Fangfei Li,Chenyang Zhao,Long Wang,Feng Tian,Zhiyue Zheng,Lv Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLAP是一种面向领域智能体的闭环后训练方法，将业务数据转化为结构化SFT样本、决策偏好样本、留出集、风险诊断和发布门控记录，通过数据验证、奖励/KL诊断、离线门控和应用链回放等环节，决定适配器是否适合目标应用链。在制造业场景中，LoRA-SFT带来小幅平均增益，但批次间表现不一致，GRPO暴露高KL风险，应用链回放验证了RAG对事实提取的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域智能体面临噪声业务数据、不确定的后训练收益、离线与应用场景不匹配、以及适配器发布风险等问题。现有方法缺乏一套从数据到训练到评估再到发布的完整闭环流程来系统化管理这些风险。

Method: 提出CLAP（Closed-Loop Agent Post-training）闭环方法，包含五大组件：(1)将业务数据转化为结构化SFT样本、决策偏好样本、留出集、风险诊断和发布门控记录；(2)数据验证与目标/证据归一化；(3)奖励与KL散度诊断；(4)离线门控机制；(5)应用链回放，即在实际应用链路中回放测试，综合判断适配器是否适合发布。

Result: 在五个匿名化制造业场景批次上：(1)QLoRA风格SFT带来平均整体分数+0.0098、通过率+0.0240、证据准确率+0.0280，幻觉和错误事实下降；(2)但仅3/5批次改善，部分批次退化；(3)GRPO暴露高KL风险；(4)在3B基座和100条回放案例下，面向应用RAG的LoRA-SFT适配器在价值、核心字段、答案-证据文档/页匹配上优于base+RAG，但增加了延迟。

Conclusion: 领域智能体的后训练应通过集成的"数据-训练-评估-发布"闭环来管理，而非仅依赖训练完成或单一离线评分。CLAP为这一闭环提供了系统化的方法论支撑。

Abstract: Domain agents often face noisy business data, uncertain post-training gains, offline/application mismatch, and adapter-release risk. This paper presents CLAP (Closed-Loop Agent Post-training), a closed-loop method that converts business data into structured SFT samples, decision-preference samples, holdout sets, risk diagnostics, and release-gate records. CLAP combines data validation, target/evidence normalization, reward/KL diagnosis, offline gates, and application-chain replay to decide whether an adapter is suitable for the target application chain. On five anonymized manufacturing-scenario batches, QLoRA-style LoRA-SFT yields modest average gains: overall score increases by 0.0098, pass rate by 0.0240, and evidence accuracy by 0.0280, while hallucination and wrong facts decrease. Yet only 3 of 5 batches improve, some batches regress, and GRPO exposes high KL risks. Application-chain replay further shows that RAG is necessary for factual extraction; under the same 3B backbone and 100 replay cases, an application-RAG-oriented LoRA-SFT adapter improves value, core fields, and answer-evidence doc/page matching over base+RAG, but increases latency. These results support managing domain-agent post-training through an integrated data-training-evaluation-release loop rather than relying on training completion or a single offline score.

</details>


### [235] [Safety Targeted Embedding Exploit via Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01859)
*Joshua Adrian Cahyono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STEER 是一种基于梯度引导的对抗攻击方法，通过将触发模型拒绝的关键词迭代翻译为低资源语言，在开源模型中实现高达 96.7% 的攻击成功率，并具备向 GPT-4o-mini 的迁移能力，揭示了 LLM 安全对齐在多语言场景下的系统性脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的安全训练以英语为主，其在低资源语言和混合语言代码切换场景下的安全泛化能力尚不明确。作者旨在探究并揭示这一认知鸿沟（epistemic gap）：模型可能对分布外多语言输入自信地生成有害回复，而现有安全机制无法有效应对。

Method: 提出 STEER（Safety Targeted Embedding Exploit via Refinement），一种梯度引导攻击方法。具体步骤为：(1) 利用梯度信息识别输入中对模型拒绝行为贡献最大的关键词语；(2) 将这些词迭代翻译为低资源语言，从而抑制模型的拒绝响应；(3) 在保留有害意图的前提下，生成跨语言对抗提示。实验在六款开源 8B 参数模型上进行评估，并与随机代码切换攻击和 GCG 攻击进行对比。

Result: 在 JailbreakBench 上攻击成功率最高达 93.0%，在 AdvBench 上达 96.7%，显著优于随机代码切换和 GCG 基线。生成的对抗提示可迁移至 GPT-4o-mini，在无目标模型访问权限的情况下实现 35.5% 的攻击成功率，表明该漏洞并非特定架构独有。

Conclusion: 仅基于英语对齐的安全机制无法泛化至多语言输入。提升多语言安全性需要在安全对齐阶段覆盖更广泛的语言分布，并引入能显式检测并拒绝对分布外输入进行回复的机制。

Abstract: Safety training for large language models (LLMs) is conducted predominantly in English, leaving uncertain how well safety mechanisms generalize to low-resource languages and mixed-language code-switching. We show that this creates an epistemic gap in which models confidently generate harmful responses for inputs that fall outside the distribution of their safety training. To study this phenomenon, we introduce STEER (Safety Targeted Embedding Exploit via Refinement), a gradient-guided attack that identifies words contributing most strongly to the model's refusal behavior and iteratively translates them into low-resource languages to suppress refusal while preserving harmful intent. Across six open-source 8B-parameter models, STEER achieves attack success rates of up to 93.0% on JailbreakBench and 96.7% on AdvBench, outperforming random code-switching and Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG). The resulting prompts also transfer to GPT-4o-mini, achieving a 35.5% attack success rate without requiring access to the target model, suggesting that the underlying weakness is not specific to a single architecture. These findings demonstrate that safety mechanisms aligned primarily on English cannot be assumed to generalize across multilingual inputs. We argue that improving multilingual safety requires broader coverage during alignment and mechanisms that explicitly detect and abstain on out-of-distribution inputs.

</details>


### [236] [CamoNAS: Neural Architecture Search for Enhanced Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01870)
*Dawei Ren,Yan Zhang,Hongying Tang,Qiaoling Zhou,Jianpo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CamoNAS是一个面向伪装目标检测的自动神经架构搜索框架，通过频率感知的多分辨率搜索空间和RGB-频率双流架构，在四个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD模型依赖手工设计的架构和多尺度特征融合，受直觉驱动而非系统化搜索，导致对弱边缘和模糊边界的建模能力受限。亟需一种自动化架构搜索方法，为COD任务量身定制更优的网络结构。

Method: 提出CamoNAS框架：(1) 构建双层搜索空间——细胞级操作搜索和网络级下采样路径搜索；(2) 设计RGB-频率双流架构，利用可学习小波变换在频域提取互补特征，增强对伪装目标边缘和纹理的感知能力。

Result: 在CAMO、COD10K、NC4K、CHAMELEON四个COD基准数据集上达到最先进性能，验证了NAS在COD任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: CamoNAS首次将神经架构搜索引入伪装目标检测领域，通过频率感知双流设计和分层搜索空间，成功超越手工设计模型，为COD任务提供了新的自动化设计范式。

Abstract: Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) aims to locate and segment objects that blend into their surroundings, presenting challenges due to weak edge cues and ill-defined boundaries. Traditional COD models rely on hand-designed architectures and multi-scale feature fusion, which are often guided by intuition rather than systematic search. This paper introduces CamoNAS, a frequency-aware multi-resolution Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for COD. CamoNAS automatically searches both cell-level operations and network-level downsampling paths, forming a hierarchical search space tailored to detect camouflaged objects. Additionally, it adopts an RGB frequency dual-stream architecture, where a learnable wavelet transform complements the RGB spatial stream. CamoNAS achieves state-of-the-art performance on four COD benchmarks (CAMO, COD10K, NC4K, CHAMELEON), highlighting the effectiveness of NAS for COD. Our code is available at https://github.com/rendaweiSIMIT/CamoNAS.

</details>


### [237] [SkillCoach: Self-Evolving Rubrics for Evaluating and Enhancing Agentic Skill-Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01874)
*Jiayin Zhu,Kelong Mao,Yudong Guo,Dengbo He,Sulong Xu,Simiu Gu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillCoach 是一个自演化的评分量规框架，通过从真实轨迹中提取技能导向的过程量规，沿四个维度（技能选择、技能遵循、技能组合、技能反思）评估智能体技能使用，将过程质量与偶然任务成功区分开来，并为训练筛选高质量轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实技能库中，技能存在大量重叠，导致智能体难以可靠地选择和使用技能。仅依赖最终验证器的成功信号过于粗糙——智能体可能通过试错侥幸成功，同时混杂了干扰技能选择、跳过必要步骤、错误组合工作流或遗漏最终检查等问题。因此需要更细粒度的过程评估方法。

Method: SkillCoach 框架首先从真实的智能体执行轨迹中自动提取技能导向的过程量规（skill-grounded rubrics），然后沿四个维度评估轨迹：技能选择（skill selection）、技能遵循（skill following）、技能组合（skill composition）和技能反思（skill-grounded reflection）。外部验证器保留为独立的结局信号，使过程质量与偶然成功得以区分。演化后的量规进一步作为过程监督信号，用于筛选高质量训练轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，演化后的量规显著提升了评估质量，能够暴露被最终准确率掩盖的失败模式，并提供比仅使用结局过滤更强的监督信号来增强智能体的技能使用能力。

Conclusion: SkillCoach 通过自演化过程量规实现了对智能体技能使用的细粒度评估与增强，证明了过程监督优于结局过滤，并能有效发现隐藏的失败模式。

Abstract: Skills are becoming a reusable operational layer for LLM agents, encoding SOPs, domain rules, tool workflows, scripts, and validation routines. In realistic skill repositories, overlapping skills make reliable skill-use difficult. Final verifier success is too coarse for both evaluation and training, since an agent may pass through trial and error while selecting distractor skills, skipping required steps, composing workflows incorrectly or omitting final checks. We introduce SkillCoach, a self-evolving rubric framework for evaluating and enhancing agentic skill-use. SkillCoach derives skill-grounded process rubrics from real rollouts and evaluates trajectories along four dimensions: skill selection, skill following, skill composition, and skill-grounded reflection. It keeps the external verifier as a separate outcome signal, allowing process quality to be distinguished from accidental task success. The evolved rubrics further serve as process supervision for selecting high-quality training trajectories. Experiments show that evolved rubrics substantially improve evaluation quality, expose failures hidden by final accuracy, and provide stronger supervision signals than outcome-only filtering for enhancing agentic skill-use.

</details>


### [238] [Spec-AUF: Accept-Until-Fail Training under Train-Inference Misalignment for Masked Block Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01893)
*Tianjian Yang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUF（Accepted Until First-failure）是一种针对块状推测解码草稿器的训练目标改进：它仅保留从开头到草稿器首次预测失败位置的交叉熵损失支持，使监督信号集中于实际被目标模型接受的token前缀，从而无需修改推理流程即可提升平均接受长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块状草稿器（如DLM风格）用全块交叉熵训练，对每个位置都进行监督，但推理时首个拒绝点之后的token全被丢弃，造成训练与推理的失配。已有的acceptance-aware方法通过重加权缓解此问题，但作者希望从根本上让监督信号集中在被接受的前缀上。

Method: AUF将标准交叉熵损失的support截断至草稿器自身预测的第一个失败token处——即仅对从开头到首次预测错误的区间计算CE损失，后续token不贡献梯度。该方法是单一的损失侧修改：无辅助目标、无需验证器展开、不改推理管线、不破坏exactness契约。在DFlash掩码块草稿器和Domino双分支头上进行了验证。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，AUF将DFlash草稿器在六个基准上的平均发射长度τ从2.40提升至2.61（每个基准均有增益），并将Domino双分支头的τ从2.56提升至2.68。此外发现：仅用decay的基线在共享块掩码上token准确率更高但解码效果更差；AUF截断支持后，标准的指数位置衰减权重变得无效。

Conclusion: AUF通过简洁地限制CE损失的support范围，有效对齐了块状草稿器的训练与推理行为，在多个草稿器架构和基准上一致提升推测解码效率。其简洁性和即插即用特性使其成为块状草稿器训练中一个有吸引力的默认选择。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by drafting a block of tokens that the target model verifies left-to-right, committing only the longest accepted prefix. Block (DLM-style) drafters predict the whole block in parallel, which is fast but trained with a full-block cross-entropy that supervises every position against the gold continuation -- even though inference discards every token after the first rejection. Recent acceptance-aware objectives patch this by reweighting the full-block loss; we instead use teacher-forced learning as a motivation for how supervision should concentrate on the accepted prefix. A mask-only block drafter has no input-side channel for gold-prefix conditioning, so AUF approximates that prefix-sensitive supervision on the loss side by keeping the cross-entropy support only through the drafter's first predicted failure. AUF is a single, detached change to the CE support -- no auxiliary objective, no verifier rollouts, and no change to the inference pipeline or the exactness contract. Within fixed drafter backbones and serving settings on Qwen3-8B, AUF raises the DFlash drafter's average emitted length $τ$, averaged over six benchmarks, from 2.40 to 2.61, with a gain on every benchmark, and transfers to Domino's two-branch head (2.56 to 2.68). Two findings sharpen the picture: the decay-only baseline reaches higher token accuracy on the shared block mask yet decodes worse, and on DFlash, once AUF truncates the support, the standard exponential position-decay weighting becomes empirically inert.

</details>


### [239] [Rethinking Complexity Metrics for LLM-Integrated Applications: Beyond Source Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01903)
*Zihao Xu,Yuekang Li,Gelei Deng,Yi Liu,Zhenchang Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HECATE是首个同时评估LLM集成应用中提示层和代码层复杂度的工具。它基于Hoare逻辑启发的"提示即规约"思想，从25个复杂度维度生成52个候选度量指标。通过对18个开源项目118个组件的实证分析，仅10个指标在控制代码规模后仍具显著性，其中7个为新增的"结构广度"指标。提示层指标即使在最强代码指标作为协变量时仍保持显著，证明了提示复杂度的独立维度地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM集成应用将自然语言提示与程序代码融合，大量运行时行为源于提示层而非代码层，但现有复杂度度量工具仅作用于代码层，完全忽视了提示层的行为逻辑，造成了评估盲区。

Method: 提出HECATE工具，核心是"Prompt-as-Specification"形式化方法（受Hoare逻辑启发），将每个提示解释为预期行为的规约。基于已发表分类法中的25个复杂度维度，生成52个候选度量指标。以18个开源仓库的118个组件为样本，利用版本历史中的维护活动作为复杂度的实证代理，通过控制代码规模后检验各指标的统计显著性，剔除不显著指标。最终在6个留出仓库的20个组件上进行验证。

Result: 仅10个指标通过显著性检验：7个为新增的"结构广度"指标（如LLM调用点、内存属性、提示模板等结构独立元素的计数），3个为传统指标（RFC、Halstead N和V，其中RFC同样呈现广度特征，N和V仅作为规模的残余效应存活）。新指标性能超越传统指标。提示层指标在加入最强代码指标作为协变量后仍保持显著。留出验证中两个最佳指标持续预测维护工作量。

Conclusion: 提示复杂度是独立于代码复杂度的独特维度，HECATE提出的结构广度指标能有效捕捉这一维度。仅靠规模度量是不够的，计数结构上独立的元素比单纯测量体量更能反映LLM集成应用的真实复杂度。

Abstract: LLM-integrated applications blend natural language prompts with program code, and much of their runtime behavior originates in the prompt layer rather than in the code itself. Existing complexity metrics, however, operate solely at the code level and therefore overlook this behavioral logic entirely. We present HECATE, the first tool designed to assess complexity in both the prompt and code layers of such applications. Central to HECATE is Prompt-as-Specification, a Hoare-logic-inspired formalism that interprets every prompt as a specification of intended behavior. Grounded in 25 complexity dimensions identified across published taxonomies, the tool generates 52 candidate metrics. We assess each metric against 118 components collected from 18 open-source repositories, relying on maintenance activity derived from version history as an empirical proxy for complexity, and discard any metric that loses significance once code size is accounted for. Only ten metrics withstand this test. Seven belong to our newly introduced set; rather than measuring sheer volume, each tallies structurally distinct elements, such as LLM call sites, memory attributes, and prompt templates, an attribute we call structural breadth. Of the three surviving conventional metrics, RFC exhibits a similar breadth-oriented character, while Halstead N and V survive only as a residual effect of size; our top-performing metrics exceed all three. Crucially, the prompt-layer metrics retain significance even when the strongest code-level metric is added as a covariate, establishing prompt complexity as a dimension in its own right. A final validation on 20 components spanning six held-out repositories shows that the two best-performing metrics continue to predict maintenance effort, supporting their generalizability beyond the training set.

</details>


### [240] [ContextSniper: AntTrail's Token-Efficient Code Memory for Repository-Level Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01916)
*Chiwang Luk,Matin Mohammad Najafi,Zhifeng Jia,Wei Yang,Xiuchang Li,Jinwei Zhu,Yang Ren,Lei Chen,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContextSniper 是 AntTrail 智能体记忆引擎中面向代码修复的令牌高效记忆层，通过精准证据选择机制（Sniper），在不显著牺牲修复成功率的前提下，将令牌消耗降低 38.9%–51.5%，成本降低 27.3%–36.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在修复真实代码仓库缺陷时，常因全文件读取、宽泛搜索和冗长终端输出而消耗大量上下文令牌，其中有价值证据与无关信息混杂，导致效率低下、成本高昂。

Method: ContextSniper 实现 Sniper 精准证据选择功能，包含四个核心步骤：(1) 检索候选代码与运行时证据；(2) 利用混合检索信号对候选证据排序；(3) 通过意图感知上下文门控过滤长输出；(4) 返回紧凑证据包，同时在 prompt 外部保留可恢复的源上下文。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Lite 上使用 OpenClaw 和 Claude Code 分别运行 50 个任务进行评估：OpenClaw 令牌总量减少 51.5%，记录成本降低 36.4%；Claude Code 令牌总量减少 38.9%，估算成本降低 27.3%。提交分辨率率略有下降，OpenClaw 从 26.0% 降至 24.0%，Claude Code 从 32.0% 降至 30.0%。

Conclusion: ContextSniper 能够在保持接近原有修复能力的同时，显著压缩上下文开销，验证了令牌高效记忆层在代码修复智能体中的实用价值。其测试脚本已开源。

Abstract: Large language model agents can repair real repository issues, but they often spend large context budgets on whole-file reads, broad searches, and long terminal outputs where useful evidence is mixed with irrelevant code and logs. This paper presents ContextSniper, AntTrail's token-efficient code memory layer for repository-level program repair. As the coding specialization of AntTrail's broader agent memory engine, ContextSniper implements the Sniper feature for precision evidence selection: it retrieves candidate code and runtime evidence, ranks it with hybrid retrieval signals, filters long outputs through an intention-aware context gate, and returns compact evidence packets while preserving recoverable source context outside the prompt. We evaluate ContextSniper on SWE-bench Lite with OpenClaw and Claude Code, using 50 task runs per host-agent condition. ContextSniper reduces total token use by 51.5% and logged cost by 36.4% for OpenClaw, and reduces total token use by 38.9% and estimated cost by 27.3% for Claude Code. Submitted-resolution rates decrease slightly, from 26.0% to 24.0% for OpenClaw and from 32.0% to 30.0% for Claude Code. ContextSniper's pilot testing scripts are open-sourced at https://github.com/Calluking/ContextSniper

</details>


### [241] [ElephantAgent: Contextual State Continuity in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01919)
*Jiankai Jin,Xiangzheng Zhang,Zhao Liu,Wenzhuo Xu,Dongdong Yang,Deyue Zhang,Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ElephantAgent 是一种安全协议，通过强制上下文状态连续性（Contextual State Continuity）来防御智能体系统中的工具和记忆投毒攻击。它利用复制可信硬件维护可线性化的授权状态转换账本，对本地上下文状态摘要进行实时验证，并支持历史可追溯性以实现事后审计和状态恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统通过调用外部工具和持久记忆增强能力，但这些外部依赖引入了新的攻击面。恶意的工具描述符和投毒记忆可以隐蔽地操纵智能体行为。这些威胁暴露出一个更深层的问题：智能体在规划和执行过程中缺乏可验证的上下文状态连续性。

Method: ElephantAgent 定义上下文状态为智能体完整上下文中受安全约束的关键子集（如工具状态和记忆）。每次处理查询前，协议重新计算本地上下文状态的摘要，并与最新授权摘要进行比对验证。系统使用复制可信硬件维护一个可线性化的授权状态转换账本，能检测带外状态篡改。针对带内语义滥用，ElephantAgent 提供历史可追溯性机制，支持有条件的 post-hoc 审计以及回滚到已知良好状态。

Result: 该论文为协议设计类工作，主要贡献在于提出了上下文状态连续性的形式化定义和 ElephantAgent 协议架构，具体实验评估结果需参阅原文。

Conclusion: ElephantAgent 通过将 Nimble 等状态连续性机制扩展到智能体系统的动态上下文状态，为工具调用和持久记忆场景提供了一种防御上下文状态投毒的系统性解决方案，填补了智能体安全中可验证状态连续性的空白。

Abstract: Agentic systems enhance their capabilities by invoking external tools and maintaining persistent memory. However, these external dependencies introduce novel attack surfaces. Recent tool and memory poisoning attacks show that maliciously crafted tool descriptors and poisoned memory can covertly bias agent behavior. These threats reflect a deeper issue: the lack of verifiable continuity in the agent's contextual state for planning and execution. We present ElephantAgent, a protocol that enforces Contextual State Continuity to defend against contextual state poisoning. Inspired by prior state-continuity mechanisms (e.g., Nimble), ElephantAgent extends this protection to the evolving contextual state of agentic systems. We define the contextual state as the bounded, security-critical subset of the agent's entire context (e.g., tool state and memory). Before processing each query, ElephantAgent recomputes the digest of the local contextual state and verifies it against the latest authorized digest. Using replicated trusted hardware, ElephantAgent maintains a linearizable ledger of authorized contextual state transitions and detects out-of-band state tampering. To handle in-band semantic abuse, ElephantAgent additionally provides Historical Traceability, enabling conditional post-hoc audit and recovery to a known-good prior state.

</details>


### [242] [A-TMA: Decoupling State-Aware Memory Failures in Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01935)
*Zitong Shi,Yixuan Tang,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体记忆系统中的"幽灵记忆"问题——新旧事实在记忆库中混合导致状态协调失败，提出三层次优化框架（记忆库维护、检索、回答时消解）及状态感知覆盖系统ATMA，并构建了冲突密集型基准LTP用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使LLM智能体能作为持久助手，但用户事实会随时间变化。现有记忆系统无法有效区分"当前为真"、"曾为真"和"已变更"的事实，导致检索时新旧信息混合，误导回答模型。这种"幽灵记忆"问题缺乏系统性的理解和可测量的评估手段。

Method: 提出三层次记忆系统分析框架：记忆库维护层、检索层、回答时消解层。设计ATMA（状态感知覆盖系统），在现有记忆系统之上保留被替代记录和过渡记录，构建针对查询所请求状态视图的证据包（evidence packets），并将当前、历史和过渡标签暴露给问答模型。同时构建LTP（LoCoMo Temporal Plus）基准，专为幽灵记忆设计高冲突场景，并解耦评估三个层次的故障。

Result: 在LTP基准上，Graphiti+ATMA相比单独使用Graphiti，冲突准确率绝对提升0.240；在LoCoMo长对话泛化测试上，Graphiti+ATMA将时序F1从0.0295提升至0.1705。提升效果依赖于宿主系统，但结果表明显式的状态角色标注能减少最终QA准确率所掩盖的记忆故障。

Conclusion: 幽灵记忆是LLM记忆系统中的关键故障模式，需从记忆库维护、检索和回答三个层次分别优化。状态感知覆盖方案ATMA通过显式标注事实的时间状态角色，有效缓解了幽灵记忆问题。呼吁采用解耦评估方式，避免最终QA准确率掩盖中间层次的故障。

Abstract: Long term memory lets LLM agents act as persistent assistants, but user facts change. A useful memory system must know what is true now, what used to be true, and what changed. We study \emph{ghost memory}, a state coordination failure in which old, current, and transition facts coexist in the memory bank, remain mixed during retrieval, and mislead the answer model. We argue that memory systems should be understood and optimized from three levels: bank maintenance, retrieval, and answer time resolution. We propose ATMA, a state aware overlay for existing memory systems. ATMA keeps superseded and transition records in the bank, builds evidence packets for the query's requested state view, and exposes current, historical, and transition labels to QA. We further call for decoupled evaluation of bank, retrieval, and answer level failures, since final QA accuracy can hide where ghost memory occurs. To make this failure measurable, we build LTP (LoCoMo Temporal Plus), a conflict heavy benchmark for ghost memory, and evaluate on LoCoMo for long conversation generalization. On LTP, Graphiti+ATMA improves conflict accuracy by 0.240 absolute over Graphiti. On LoCoMo, Graphiti+ATMA raises temporal F1 from 0.0295 to 0.1705. The gains are host dependent, but they indicate that explicit state roles can reduce memory failures hidden by final QA accuracy.

</details>


### [243] [Atomic Task Graph: A Unified Framework for Agentic Planning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01942)
