Conclusion: 持久化代码库中的迭代开发场景引入了现有AI控制范式未能覆盖的新型攻击面。渐进式攻击与集中式攻击构成互补威胁，迫使防御方必须采用多类型监控器集成策略。有状态监控器（如链接追踪器）是应对跨PR分布式攻击的关键组件，但即使最佳集成方案仍未完全消除威胁，表明该方向仍需持续研究。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络(M-QCDNet)，将认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与深度学习相结合，利用Q矩阵作为结构先验约束技能-题目关系，并设计了L2惩罚损失函数与基于可解释对齐的评估指标，兼顾预测性能与诊断有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型虽具可解释性但灵活性不足，而深度学习模型预测能力强却缺乏心理测量学的透明度。研究者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，构建既保持诊断效度又具备神经网络灵活性的可解释AI系统。

Method: 提出M-QCDNet框架：(1) 将Q矩阵嵌入多层神经网络作为结构先验，约束题目-技能关系；(2) 设计带L2惩罚项的损失函数，抑制与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活；(3) 开发基于可解释对齐的评估矩阵，量化预测技能激活与题目级技能的对应程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持预测性能的同时，实现了与认知理论一致的潜在掌握模式，所提出的可解释对齐指标能有效量化模型诊断结果的结构一致性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet成功将心理测量学的透明度与神经网络的灵活性融合，实现了可解释、公平且可行动的认知诊断AI，为课堂实践中早期学习困难检测和掌握性干预提供了实用工具。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [278] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network (IIRMAN) 的方法，通过在频点级别构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到 SPD 切空间，结合频率聚类聚合和切片内-切片间注意力机制，有效融合频域判别信息与全局时序上下文，在三个数据集上取得了最高 82.78% 的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试 EEG 压力检测的挑战在于压力相关模式既依赖于被试又依赖于频率。传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽视了神经振荡对高级认知状态解码的关键作用；而标准时序分块（tokenization）会割裂切片间的时间连贯性。因此需要一种能同时保留空间几何结构、频域判别信息与时序上下文的方法。

Method: 提出 IIRMAN 方法，包含三个核心设计：(1) 在每个频点独立构建空间协方差矩阵，映射到对称正定（SPD）切空间，保留通道几何结构与频率特异性判别线索；(2) 引入频率聚类聚合模块，筛选有信息量的频谱成分，形成与 EEG 节律对齐的数据驱动频率簇以降低冗余；(3) 设计切片内-切片间注意力模块，自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态与跨序列的全局时序上下文。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，IIRMAN 一致优于五种最先进基线方法，最高达到 82.78% 的平衡准确率，同时仅需 1.60M 参数量和 31.95M FLOPs，兼具高效性。

Conclusion: IIRMAN 通过频点级 SPD 切空间建模、频率聚类聚合以及切片内-切片间注意力机制，有效解决了跨被试 EEG 压力检测中空间-频率-时序信息融合不足的问题，在保持参数高效的同时实现了性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [279] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程示例(PBE)系统在面对自适应对手的对抗性示例时的鲁棒性，提出了一种版本空间分区聚合(VPA)防御策略，但结论偏向负面：VPA仅在干净语义保持分区投票余量时才有效，而在现实任务中往往失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE鲁棒性研究通常将错误示例建模为随机噪声，但却忽视了一种更危险的失效模式：自适应对手可以观察合成器，并精心选择那些对返回程序造成最大破坏的示例进行污染。作者旨在形式化和评估这种对抗性威胁。

Method: 1. 对有限PBE版本空间形式化固定集最坏情况下的污染；2. 实现精确的池内有界搜索和启发式污染搜索，针对字符串变换DSL；3. 提出版本空间分区聚合(VPA)防御：将示例划分为不相交的分组进行独立合成，然后按语义签名投票。在策划/生成的DSL任务、SyGuS PBE_SLIA切片、SYNTRA Playgol v2及含噪PBE目标基线上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 一次策划编辑即可翻转全部8个尖峰任务，而200次随机类型错误控制仅成功10.3%；2. 生成的余量-1行在预算1下被翻转，但VPA可恢复；3. 在公共SyGuS上，投票余量接近1，自适应攻击将VPA准确率降至零；4. Playgol在141个接受行上显示正向配对自举间隙。小样本精确输出提示实验在20个受控余量-1任务上，本地和API模型均呈现相同的干净到攻击的退化模式。

Conclusion: 低余量PBE任务存在随机噪声评估所遗漏的对抗鲁棒性维度；语义分区聚合仅在分区投票余量充足时有效，而这在现实任务中常不成立。该边界由多项实验证据支撑。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [280] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于心电图识别，通过引入PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识，利用双流有向图分别建模心电周期内和周期间的时空依赖关系，在中文心电图智能竞赛数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能，尤其在稀有类别上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于端到端卷积神经网络的AI模型在专业领域（如心电图识别）中可解释性不足，且对稀有类别的检测性能较差。研究者希望借助领域知识（心电图PRQST关键点）来增强模型的可解释性和检测性能。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络：1）提取PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识；2）构建双流有向图——空间有向图捕获关键点间的位置关系，时间有向图刻画相邻心动周期间的时序依赖；3）在图结构上进行卷积学习以实现九类心电图分类。

Result: 在首届中文心电图智能竞赛数据集上，总体平均F1分数达88.1%，稀有类别平均F1分数达76.3%，均超越现有最优模型，验证了领域知识的引入有效提升了检测性能。

Conclusion: 融入领域知识的图卷积网络相比端到端CNN不仅性能更优、尤其在稀有类别上提升明显，而且通过显式建模心电图的关键结构特征增强了模型可解释性。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [281] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地分析了多探针网格（multiprobe grid）近似最近邻搜索算法在数据集规模N和维度d上的扩展特性，发现其在GloVe嵌入数据上存在此前未报道的维度扩展交叉现象——相比图、树和分区方法，网格方法保持了近似恒定的维度扩展指数，同时具有近线性的查询扩展和更低的索引构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的近似最近邻搜索方法在现代扩展性分析中一直缺席。作者希望填补这一空白，系统性地刻画多探针网格算法在大规模、高维场景下的性能表现，并探讨其对高效Transformer架构成本分析的潜在指导意义。

Method: 采用多探针网格算法（multiprobe grid），在GloVe嵌入数据集族上进行系统的N（数据集规模）和d（维度）扩展性实验，并与图方法、树方法、分区方法等主流ANN算法进行横向对比，测量吞吐量、查询扩展性和索引构建成本等指标。

Result: 实验揭示了一个此前未报道的d-扩展交叉现象：多探针网格搜索保持近似恒定的维度扩展指数，而其他方法（图、树、分区）的吞吐量随维度增加而退化。在N方面，网格方法呈现近线性的查询扩展。同时，其索引构建成本低于其他ANN方法。

Conclusion: 网格类方法（如多探针网格）在重建频繁或高维场景下具有竞争力，其维度鲁棒性和低索引成本是关键优势。此外，由于自注意力已被形式化为ANN操作，ANN算法的N-和d-扩展特性可为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [282] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该书从经典逼近论、优化与泛化出发，系统梳理了现代深度学习理论的统一框架，涵盖过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐及涌现等前沿议题，将广泛文献整合为以研究对象、假设条件和未解现象为线索的连贯叙述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已发展得过于庞杂，任何单一数学解释都无法全面覆盖。作者旨在构建一个统一、面向证明的深度学习理论体系，将孤立的研究成果组织成连贯的研究叙事，为研究者、研究生和数学训练有素的从业者提供一幅严谨的理论地图。

Method: 采用“对象—假设—未解现象”的三元分析框架：每种理论都通过其控制的对象（object）、使其成立的前提假设（assumptions）以及该理论尚无法解释的现象来加以审视。全书按从经典到现代的脉络组织，涵盖逼近论、优化、泛化、过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等主题。

Result: 该专著将深度学习的广泛文献整合为一条连贯的研究叙事，揭示了当前理论虽强大但不完备的现状，并指出核心问题正日益聚焦于：学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现。

Conclusion: 现代深度学习理论已形成一个强大但不完整的体系，其中心问题围绕“涌现”展开——即学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构与训练的交互中产生。该领域仍有大量未解现象等待理论解释。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [283] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 IonSense-QKG 框架，为公共锂电池数据集补充量子就绪元数据，并引入量子就绪评分 (QRS)，用于筛选适合近期混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共锂电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模等方面差异巨大，直接影响其在混合量子-经典机器学习中的可用性，缺乏系统性的量子就绪度评估框架来指导数据集选择。

Method: 基于 EV-Battery-IonSense 索引，为公共电池数据集记录补充量子相关元数据（任务类型、传感模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估算量子比特范围、NISQ 可行性等），并设计透明的量子就绪评分 (QRS) 作为数据集选择的启发式指标。

Result: 实现了基于查询的元数据发现，可识别适用于紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。发布物包括元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接校验工具及 SQL 风格查询示例。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG 将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为以数据为中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [284] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 条件推断森林（CIF）作为top-k特征排序方法在预测任务中表现优异，在分类和回归基准测试中分别排名第4和第3。运行时消融实验表明，自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大，关闭这些功能可使拟合时间增加1.9-10.8倍，而性能下降仅0.011。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推断树和森林通过先检验特征再选择分裂阈值来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算成本高昂。本文旨在研究CIT/CIF作为下游预测的top-k特征排序方法的实际表现，并分析其计算瓶颈。

Method: 通过真实数据基准测试、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验，系统评估CIT和CIF的性能。理论分析表明，在节点特征和置换预算不依赖节点响应时，Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值可在完全置换零假设下控制节点级拒绝。消融实验中固定Bonferroni校正，测试自适应停止和阈值搜索策略对运行时间和预测分数的影响。

Result: CIF在22个分类数据集上的17种方法中排名第4，在8个回归数据集上的18种方法中排名第3。运行时间方面，关闭自适应停止使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍，使用精确阈值搜索增加1.9-10.8倍，而下游得分变化最多仅0.011。稀疏高维模拟显示，森林特征采样可能导致许多分裂决策中遗漏有信息的特征。

Conclusion: 结果支持CIF作为下游预测基准中的top-k特征排序方法。自适应停止和阈值搜索数量是最关键的计算瓶颈，可通过调整这些参数在几乎不损失性能的情况下大幅提升效率。但高维稀疏场景下特征采样的信息遗漏问题值得关注。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [285] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归相结合的甲基化CNS肿瘤分类方法，在参考队列（2,801例）上达96%准确率，在独立临床验证队列（1,104例）上分别取得91类水平86%准确率和甲基化家族水平93%准确率，较现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有重要的临床意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DNA甲基化谱的中枢神经系统肿瘤分类方法在跨队列迁移性、方法论严谨性及多类别稳健评估方面仍存在不足，亟需一种方法论上更严谨的机器学习方法来提升分类可靠性和临床适用性。

Method: 提出一种将稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）用于降维、多项逻辑回归（multinomial logistic regression）用于分类的机器学习方法。在2,801例参考队列上进行分层3折交叉验证评估，并在1,104例独立临床验证队列上进行外部测试，分别在91类水平和甲基化类家族水平评估分类表现。

Result: 在参考队列上平均准确率达96%；在独立临床验证队列上，91类水平准确率为86%，甲基化类家族水平准确率为93%，相较现有最优方法的82%类别一致性和88%家族一致性分别提升了约4个和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在方法论上更严谨，在所有评估设置上均一致优于现有最优方法，5个百分点的提升可直接影响肿瘤亚型判定和治疗决策，能实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [286] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三项缩放定律，将模型大小、训练步数和批量大小三者同时纳入考量，能够准确恢复最优批量大小的缩放行为，且在使用次优批量大小的训练数据时也能稳健拟合，所需训练运行次数显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律通常仅考虑模型大小和训练数据量两个因素，未能显式地将训练步数和批量大小分开建模，导致无法准确刻画最优批量大小的缩放规律，且在数据拟合时需要大量采用最优批量设置的训练运行。作者希望提出一种更精细的缩放定律，能够统一描述这三者之间的关系。

Method: 提出三项缩放定律（three-term law），将模型参数量、训练步数和批量大小作为三个独立变量显式纳入损失函数的参数化形式中。在大规模训练运行数据集上对该定律进行拟合，验证其能否恢复最优批量大小的缩放关系，并展示即使利用次优批量大小的训练运行数据，也能稳健地估计定律参数，从而降低对训练运行数量的需求。

Result: 该三项定律能够正确恢复最优批量大小的缩放行为；由于可以利用次优批量大小的训练运行数据，拟合所需训练运行数量大幅减少；此外，该定律可推导出次优批量下的缩放规律，并与此前关于关键批量大小（critical batch size）的经验发现一致。

Conclusion: 三项缩放定律提供了一种更统一、更高效的框架来描述大规模语言模型训练中模型大小、训练步数与批量大小之间的缩放关系，具有更高的数据效率和更强的泛化能力，可推广至次优批量设置并与已有研究结果吻合。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [287] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了分布式学习中拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私（LDP）与泛化误差之间的关系，发现三者并非在所有隐私强度下都存在三元权衡：在高噪声（强隐私）区域，增强隐私反而降低泛化误差；而在低噪声（弱隐私）区域，隐私与鲁棒性之间的张力重新出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作确立了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和优化误差之间的三元悖论（trilemma），但该结论是否适用于泛化误差尚不清楚。本文旨在探究不同隐私强度下，鲁棒性与隐私对泛化误差的联合影响机制。

Method: 通过算法稳定性（algorithmic stability）框架，推导在LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的泛化误差上界与下界，分别在强隐私（高噪声）和弱隐私（低噪声）两个区域进行理论分析，并辅以实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了泛化误差关于隐私强度的非单调行为：强隐私下增加隐私会改善泛化（鲁棒性与隐私无冲突），弱隐私下增加隐私则损害泛化（张力再现）。实验进一步佐证了这一发现。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性与本地差分隐私之间的三元悖论并不能普遍地推广到泛化误差，其成立与否关键取决于所处的隐私区域。这一发现为隐私保护鲁棒学习系统的设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [288] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 即使当前商业LLM API仅暴露单个token的logit且不支持logit bias，NightVision攻击仍能通过公共集合提示技术和首token时间测量，还原模型的隐藏维度、深度和参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商为保护架构机密，已将API限制为仅返回每个解码token的单个logit，并移除了logit bias功能。本文旨在探究在这种严格受限的API下，模型架构参数是否仍可被恢复。

Method: 提出NightVision攻击方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 公共集合提示技术——通过多个提示暴露同一组输出token的对数概率，并利用谱分析（奇异值分解）从logit矩阵中推断隐藏维度；(2) 利用端到端首token时间（TTFT）测量结合已估计的隐藏维度，进一步估计模型深度和参数总量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上验证：隐藏维度恢复的平均相对误差为23%（MoE模型上仅9%）；对超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量的估计误差在53%以内。消融实验展示了精度随token预算和模型特性的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，NightVision证明了即使在最严格的API条件下，架构参数仍可被有效推断，提示LLM提供商需要进一步增强API的隐私保护措施。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [289] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种角色感知的神经凸散度头（role-aware neural convex divergence head），用于非对称表示学习。该头在对输入凸神经Bregman散度评估之前，先施加源角色和目标角色投影，在角色投影空间中产生非负结构化得分，在词汇蕴涵、句子蕴涵、本体层次等定向关系任务上一致优于普通ICNN-Bregman头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多表示学习问题涉及定向关系（如词汇蕴涵、句子蕴涵、本体层次、引用链接），但标准欧氏距离、余弦距离和马氏距离头都是对称的，无法建模方向性；而通用神经打分器虽能建模方向性，却缺乏几何结构。因此需要一种既能捕捉方向性、又具有结构化几何解释的表示学习模块。

Method: 提出角色感知的神经凸散度头：首先对输入表示施加可学习的源角色投影和目标角色投影，然后在投影空间中评估输入凸神经网络（ICNN）参数化的Bregman散度，得到非负的结构化方向性得分。论文对该头的投影空间恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解以及基于Hessian的局部曲率进行了理论刻画。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图四个基准上进行了实验，对比了对称距离、非结构化非对称打分器、序/双曲基线、普通ICNN-Bregman头以及所提角色感知变体。在主要语义和本体基准上，10个随机种子下的结果表明角色感知投影一致提升了方向准确性，同时保持零负散度率。但在大规模固定特征引用预测任务上，专用对称或双曲基线在排序准确性上仍更强。

Conclusion: 所提出的角色感知神经凸散度头最适合理解为一个结构化且可解释的即插即用距离模块，适用于定向关系重要的任务场景。其在大规模固定特征场景下的边界情况也提示了未来改进方向。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning. The head applies source- and target-role projections before evaluating an input-convex neural Bregman divergence, yielding a nonnegative structured score in the role-projected space. We characterize its projected-space identity, source-role convexity, directional-gap decomposition, and Hessian-based local curvature. Experiments on lexical, sentence, ontology, and directed graph benchmarks compare symmetric distances, unstructured asymmetric scorers, order/hyperbolic baselines, plain ICNN-Bregman heads, and the proposed role-aware variant. Across ten random seeds on the main semantic and ontology benchmarks, role-aware projections consistently improve directional accuracy over plain ICNN-Bregman heads while preserving zero observed negative divergence rate. The results also identify a boundary case: on large fixed-feature citation prediction, specialized symmetric or hyperbolic baselines remain stronger in ranking accuracy. Overall, the proposed head is best understood as a structured and interpretable plug-in distance module for tasks where directional relations matter.

</details>


### [290] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态AI系统，利用铁路道口图像和结构化事故报告数据，对道口进行安全等级分类和FRA安全评分预测，宏观F1达0.757、RMSE为0.078。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路道口安全评估传统上依赖人工巡检和专家判断，难以大规模推广。本文旨在探索能否利用视觉线索和结构化事故数据，构建自动化的多模态AI评估系统，使评估结果与联邦铁路局(FRA)评分及专家意见一致。

Method: 提出端到端概念验证流程：涵盖数据准备、多模态学习范式等关键环节。采用路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型(VLM)流水线，同时融合道口图像和结构化事故报告数据，实现对HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK道口的分类以及FRA安全评分的回归估计。

Result: 该系统在HIGH-RISK与LOW-RISK道口分类上宏观F1分数达0.757；FRA安全评分估计的RMSE为0.078，相关系数为0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估高度一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合视觉线索与结构化事故数据的多模态AI方法能够有效评估铁路道口安全水平，为大规模自动化安全评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [291] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在高维高斯位置模型中比较了变分估计与谱估计两种岭正则化对数密度比估计方法，推导了确定性的渐近等价表达式，并发现在大样本下变分估计更优、小样本下谱估计更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数密度比估计在机器学习中广泛应用，但在高维场景下不同估计方法的理论性质尚不清楚。本文旨在系统比较变分估计（基于KL散度拟合）与谱估计（基于连续谱的岭正则化最小二乘）在高维渐近框架下的风险表现，为实践中的方法选择提供理论指导。

Method: 作者在协方差矩阵相同的高斯位置模型中建立框架，利用仿射不变性简化为 q~N(0,I)、p~N(Δ,I)。变分估计通过带L2惩罚的经验KL拟合并用凸高斯极小极大定理（CGMT）导出标量熵最小化问题；谱估计则借助两个独立高斯样本协方差矩阵加权和的预解式（resolvent）得到确定性等价。两者均在观测数与维数同比趋于无穷的高维渐近体制下分析，同时探讨了核惩罚用于特征学习的可能性。

Result: 推导出了两种估计量的高维确定性渐近等价表达式。在固定信号的长宽比扫描和最优正则化实验中：观测数较多时，正确指定的变分估计风险更小；观测数较少时，谱估计因其基于协方差的构造具有更低的方差而占优。

Conclusion: 两种估计量在不同样本量体制下各有优势：大样本偏向变分估计，小样本偏向谱估计。这一差异源于谱估计的协方差构造带来了更低的方差。核惩罚有望用于特征学习，但仅得到了部分分析。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [292] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型中三种数值编码策略（离散、连续、混合），在合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务上评估其表现。研究发现，显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上最优，但混合token化方法（保留数值并进行分箱后投影）提供了更稳健且广泛适用的替代方案，其中最优分箱数遵循数据集规模的幂律分布。总体而言，模型的数值计算表现为"足够好"而非精确算术，临床收益取决于具体任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录(EHR)数据中包含大量数值型临床指标（如实验室检测值），但如何最优地在Transformer架构中编码这些数值尚未被充分研究。不同的编码策略（离散化、连续嵌入、混合方法）各有优劣，缺乏系统性的对比和指导原则。

Method: 研究者构建了嵌入真实EHR数据的合成算术任务，并在多个真实临床预测任务上进行评估。系统比较了三类编码策略：（1）离散编码（分箱/token化）；（2）连续编码（直接嵌入实数值）；（3）混合编码（保留数值但先分箱再投影），并分析了分箱数量与数据集规模之间的经验关系。

Result: （1）显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感的算术任务上表现最佳，但受限于架构约束；（2）混合token化方法（分箱+投影）提供了更稳健且通用的替代方案；（3）最优分箱数遵循数据集规模的幂律分布；（4）模型普遍展现"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术；（5）实验室数值对临床预测的增益取决于具体任务类型。

Conclusion: 在实际应用中，稳健性和可部署性往往比最大化数值精度更重要，因此混合token化方法应作为实用的默认选择。临床预测中是否纳入实验室数值需根据任务具体评估。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [293] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge 是一个临床引导的多任务 MRI 框架，结合大规模自监督预训练与海马分割、萎缩分类及重建目标，通过门控融合微调，在 ADNI 和 OASIS 队列上实现了领先的 AD/MCI 分类性能，并展示了跨队列泛化能力和基于概率的机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 MRI 准确识别阿尔茨海默病（AD）、轻度认知障碍（MCI）及相关痴呆仍面临挑战，因为疾病相关的脑结构变化往往细微且异质性强，传统单任务方法难以充分捕捉这些变化。

Method: 提出 NeuroBridge 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 大规模自监督 MRI 预训练，同时整合海马体分割、海马萎缩分类和图像重建三个辅助目标；(2) 采用门控融合（gated fusion）策略进行下游任务微调。在 ADNI 和 OASIS 两个独立队列上评估，涵盖跨队列迁移、基于预测概率的分析和机会性筛查场景。

Result: NeuroBridge 在所有评估的分类任务中均取得最高性能：ADNI 中 AD vs. 认知正常对照准确率达 88.17%，OASIS 中达 82.78%。最大收益出现在 MCI 相关和混合诊断场景。框架展现出强跨队列泛化能力、预测概率与准确率之间的系统性关联，以及概率驱动的机会性筛查可行性。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表示学习优于传统单任务方法，能有效提升神经退行性疾病 MRI 诊断性能。NeuroBridge 为痴呆评估和基于 MRI 的机会性筛查提供了一个稳健且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [294] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP 通过引入自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH），在保持 Gaunt 张量积（GTP）线性效率的同时，恢复了其缺失的反对称交互路径，实现了与完整 CGTP 可比的表现力，并在手性材料等任务中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 E(3) 等变网络受限于 CGTP 的 O(L⁶) 复杂度，而 GTP 虽降低复杂度却丢失反对称路径导致表达力不完整。需要一种既能保持线性效率、又能完整捕捉反对称交互的等变张量积方法。

Method: 提出 SpinGTP，将标量函数推广至自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH），利用 SWSH 的代数性质恢复 GTP 缺失的反对称交互，同时保留其渐近效率。该方法还自然涵盖张量积的奇宇称分量，构建出更富表达力的等变基。

Result: 在 Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF、OC20 等基准上，SpinGTP 达到与完整 CGTP 相当的精度。在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何结构的任务中，SpinGTP 表现出显著优越性。

Conclusion: SpinGTP 为大规模 3D 原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学上严谨的路径。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [295] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种代码Agent强化学习执行基板（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机），发现冷启动延迟差异高达110倍，百万轨迹的预计工时差异达1.8倍，主张应将执行基板优化纳入RL训练系统本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码Agent强化学习将执行基础设施视为后台实现细节，忽视了其性能开销。由于RL后训练需要大量交互式软件rollout，微小的单次开销在大规模场景下会累积成显著成本差异。因此有必要系统性地量化不同执行基板的效率差异，揭示优化执行基础设施的实际收益。

Method: 对四种执行基板进行对比实验：（1）单容器；（2）托管沙箱；（3）Kubernetes编排容器；（4）云虚拟机。测量指标包括冷启动延迟和一万个150步轨迹的预计工人小时数，以此评估不同基板在大规模RL训练场景下的效率表现。

Result: 四种执行基板的冷启动延迟差异最高达110倍；在模拟百万条150步轨迹的工作负载下，预计所需工人小时数差异达1.8倍。结果表明执行基板的选择对训练成本和效率有实质性影响。

Conclusion: 未来的代码Agent强化学习系统应将执行基板的优化作为训练系统设计的有机组成部分，而非仅视为部署层面的辅助管道。合理选择执行基板可带来显著的工程效率收益。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [296] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了混合专家模型的结构化剪枝对模型效用与事实可靠性的影响，发现在生物医学等高风险领域中，仅评估效用不足以确保剪枝后模型的安全性，需同时关注幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE剪枝研究主要关注基准效用指标，忽视了剪枝对模型事实可靠性（幻觉）的影响，尤其是在生物医学等高风险领域，这一评估缺失可能导致严重后果。

Method: 在四个MoE模型上应用六种结构化剪枝方法，设置多种剪枝比例，在生物医学（域内）和通用领域（跨域）的生成与分类任务上，同时评估模型效用和可靠性指标。

Result: 适度剪枝可保持域内效用而不立即损害可靠性；极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险显著上升；切换至通用领域时，效用和可靠性均快速下降。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖任务和领域；在高风险部署场景中，仅评估效用不足以衡量剪枝后MoE模型的真实可用性，可靠性评估不可或缺。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [297] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRS-KAN，将R函数与KAN结合，通过可微逻辑运算显式编码几何/逻辑约束，在含不连续性的回归任务中将测试RMSE降低最高67%，同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN擅长学习平滑非线性结构，但难以有效表示不连续性、可行域和隐式几何边界。需要一种能将已知几何先验或逻辑约束显式编码进神经网络架构的方法，同时保留KAN的学习能力。

Method: 将V.L. Rvachev的R函数引入KAN框架，通过R-合取和R-析取实现可微逻辑运算，显式编码几何支撑集。提出加法、乘法和不可知分支加权三种GRS-KAN变体架构，其中不可知变体可自动判断几何先验是否对任务有益。

Result: 在圆形和矩形支撑集的不连续回归问题上，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN显著提升预测精度和边界定位能力，测试RMSE最高降低67%。不可知变体展示了自动判断几何先验有效性的能力。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能大幅提升KAN在不连续回归问题上的表现，同时通过解析表示增强可解释性，为将领域知识融入神经架构提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [298] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ember，一种针对嵌入表和LM-head矩阵的轻量优化器，仅需O(V+D)显存（远低于Adam的O(2VD)），通过利用嵌入层独特的梯度几何特性，在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中改善Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入表和LM-head矩阵的梯度几何结构与稠密隐藏层权重存在本质差异，但现有优化器（如Adam）为所有参数统一维护二阶矩估计，导致大量显存浪费（O(2VD)），且在分布式训练中需要分片token优化器状态。利用这一差异性，可设计更高效的专用优化器。

Method: 提出Ember优化器：仅对嵌入/LM-head矩阵使用O(V+D)显存（仅千字节级优化器状态），替代Adam的O(2VD)。核心思想是利用嵌入层梯度的特殊几何结构——token优化轨迹可用简单一维射线描述，无需全量二阶矩估计。实现与ZeRO/FSDP无缝集成的分布式版本。

Result: Ember在监督微调、RL和预训练任务上均改善了Pareto前沿；可跨batch size和参数规模有效扩展；实验表明token优化轨迹确实遵循一维射线模式，挑战了神经网络参数在高度非凸空间中导航的流行观点。

Conclusion: Transformer训练所需的优化器空间出人意料地狭窄，嵌入层可采用极轻量的专用优化器。Ember以极低显存开销实现了与Adam相当或更优的性能，为大规模语言模型训练中的嵌入层优化提供了有原则的新方案。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [299] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，本文提出 FedCGNM，一种按类分组并对每组动量进行归一化的客户端优化器，通过均衡多数类与少数类的梯度幅值来提升稀有类性能；并进一步提出 FedHOO，一种基于 X-armed bandit 的联邦超参优化算法，用于在小规模联邦中高效优化重采样率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中类别不平衡导致稀有类预测性能差，但传统的集中式重采样/重加权方法因隐私约束和数据异构性无法直接移植到联邦场景。现有 FL 方法在解决类别不平衡时面临两大挑战：多数类和少数类梯度幅值不均衡，以及稀有类梯度本身噪声较大。

Method: 提出 FedCGNM：将类别按最小化组内方差划分为若干组，为每组维护一个动量，对每组动量进行单位长度归一化，以归一化后各组动量之和作为参数更新方向。理论上给出了考虑时变重采样率的收敛分析。进一步提出 FedHOO：基于 X-armed bandit（XAB）的算法，利用联邦并行性，以线性代价在每个客户端评估两个候选重采样率的多种组合，高效优化重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上的实验表明，FedCGNM 持续优于基线方法，FedHOO 在小规模联邦场景中带来进一步的性能提升。

Conclusion: FedCGNM 通过分组动量归一化有效缓解了 FL 中的类别不平衡问题，FedHOO 为小规模联邦提供了高效的重采样率优化方案，两者结合在多个数据集上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [300] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE（Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy），一种自适应优势函数，通过统一框架分解优势函数的正负梯度质量，沿符号轴和难度轴两正交维度分析训练动态，实现自动调度梯度权重，在推理基准上显著提升训练效率与准确率-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能提升LLM推理能力，但存在训练不稳定和多样性坍塌问题。优势函数可重塑训练目标并重加权rollouts，然而现有方法繁多，缺乏统一指导——何时该用何种优势函数尚不明确。

Method: 提出统一分析框架，将任意优势函数沿两个正交轴分解：(1) 符号轴：正负梯度质量不平衡会导致熵或权重几何坍塌；(2) 难度轴：聚焦难题可增强信号但牺牲样本效率。基于此发现训练早期探索阶段偏好平衡与难题聚焦、后期利用阶段偏好抑制与中等难度聚焦的规律，设计FADE——一种能读取训练动态、自动调度梯度权重的自适应优势函数。

Result: FADE在7B规模上比最佳静态基线提前2万步达到峰值pass@1，在32B规模上提前2千步；同时在LiveCodeBench和AIME的所有pass@k指标上取得最佳准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 优势函数在符号和难度两个轴上的权衡会随训练阶段演变，FADE通过动态自适应调度成功统一并超越了静态方法，为RL驱动的LLM推理训练提供了更高效、更稳定的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [301] [Towards Learning Representations of Policies in Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01498)
*Kevin Wang,Kevin Yang,Arjun Prakash,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究两人零和、不完全信息博弈中策略表示（嵌入）的学习问题，提出数据集构建、策略表示学习和下游评估任务三个方法，并在Kuhn和Leduc Poker上验证了自监督学习策略表示的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完全信息博弈中，策略的表示学习尚未得到充分探索。有效的策略嵌入可以捕捉博弈行为模式，为策略分析、策略聚类、策略迁移等下游应用奠定基础。本文旨在系统性地比较自监督学习技术在博弈策略表示学习中的应用。

Method: 论文的三方面方法：1）策略数据集构建：针对给定博弈生成多样化策略并构建数据集；2）策略表示学习：采用自监督学习方法（对比学习等）将策略映射为低维嵌入向量；3）下游任务设计：引入评估任务来衡量所学表示的质量。在Kuhn Poker和Leduc Poker上进行实验验证。

Result: 尽管所提方法较为基础，但在Kuhn和Leduc Poker上的实验表明，学习到的嵌入中确实包含有用的行为表示。这是较早系统比较自监督学习技术用于博弈策略表示学习的工作之一。

Conclusion: 自监督学习能够从不完全信息博弈的策略数据中提取有意义的行为表示，为后续博弈策略分析提供了新工具。代码已开源，供社区扩展研究。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of learning useful policy representations (embeddings) in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. We make three contributions: First, we introduce methods of creating datasets of policies for a given game. Second, we propose methods to learn policy representations. Third, we introduce downstream tasks to evaluate the effectiveness of such representations.
  We evaluate each dataset method, embedding method, and downstream task on Kuhn and Leduc Poker. Although our methods are very basic, we demonstrate that useful behavioral representations are present in the learned embeddings. To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

</details>


### [302] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度研究KV缓存压缩问题，通过刻画minimax风险与缓存内在可压缩性之间的关系，揭示了何时以及如何进行精确压缩，并基于因果掩码下的设计原则，提出了一种具有理论保证的实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法多依赖经验实验驱动，缺乏系统性的理论指导；已有理论结果仅表明最坏情况下压缩不可能，但未给出可实现精确压缩的条件和算法设计原则。

Method: 以minimax风险为框架，将KV缓存压缩的准确性刻画为缓存内在可压缩性的函数；在因果掩码约束下推导出设计原则，并映射到prefill和自回归解码阶段，最终实例化为实用算法。

Result: 得到了KV缓存压缩的minimax最优风险界，明确了精确压缩的条件；在LongBench上的实验表明所提算法具有良好的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有理论保证的实用KV缓存压缩提供了一条有原则的路径，弥合了理论与实践的鸿沟。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [303] [Multi-Head Recurrent Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01523)
*Jiatong Li,Samuel Yeh,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断了循环记忆智能体在长上下文中性能下降的根本原因——记忆保留是核心瓶颈，并提出多头循环记忆（MHM）框架，通过分区记忆头和分阶段"选择-再更新"策略，从架构层面防止记忆覆盖。其实例MHM-LRU在100K–1M token范围内显著提升保留率和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环记忆智能体将输入迭代整合到固定大小的记忆窗口中，虽然可扩展至任意长度上下文，但端到端性能随上下文增长而系统性退化。作者将此退化分解为记忆捕获和记忆保留两个因素，通过定量分析确认保留是主要瓶颈——原因是现有设计将记忆维护为单一文本块，每次更新都可能覆盖先前内容。

Method: 提出多头循环记忆（MHM）：一个无需训练的通用框架，将记忆划分为多个独立头（heads），采用分阶段的"选择-再更新"策略。每步仅选择一个头进行更新，其余头在结构上被保护免于覆盖，从而将保留负担从模型行为转移到架构设计。作为轻量级实现，提出最少最近更新策略（MHM-LRU），以零额外token开销保证均匀的头利用率。

Result: 在100K–1M token范围内的长上下文基准测试中，MHM-LRU显著改善了记忆保留和端到端准确率。在RULER-HQA 896K tokens上，记忆保留率从不足30%提升至73.96%。这些增益在不同模型家族、规模和任务类型中均具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 架构优化是实现可靠长上下文循环记忆的实用且高性价比路径，MHM框架通过将保留机制结构化，有效解决了循环记忆智能体的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Recurrent memory agents extend LLMs to arbitrarily long contexts by iteratively consolidating input into a fixed-size memory window. Despite their scalability, these agents exhibit a well-documented reliability problem: end-to-end performance degrades systematically as context length grows. We diagnose this failure by decomposing performance into two factors--memory capture and memory retention--and quantitatively confirm that retention is the dominant bottleneck. Retention collapses because existing designs maintain memory as a monolithic text block, forcing every update to risk overwriting previously retained content. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM), a general, training-free framework that partitions memory into independent heads governed by a stage-wise select-then-update strategy. At each step, exactly one head is selected for update while the remaining heads are structurally shielded from overwriting, shifting the burden of retention from model behavior to architectural design. As a lightweight instantiation, we introduce Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU), which guarantees uniform head utilization with zero additional token overhead. Extensive experiments on long-context benchmarks show that MHM-LRU substantially improves both retention and end-to-end accuracy across the 100K--1M token range, where baselines degrade sharply. On RULER-HQA at 896K tokens, MHM-LRU improves the memory retention rate from less than 30% to 73.96%. These gains generalize across model families, scales, and task types, positioning architectural optimization as a practical and cost-efficient path toward reliable long-context recurrent memory.

</details>


### [304] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)
*Abdullah Al Tasim,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段学习管道：先用注意力增强门控循环网络从机载运动学与动力学数据估算局部风场，再将冻结的风估计器嵌入强化学习飞行控制器，在4–12 m/s湍流风中使水平轨迹跟踪误差相比无风感知的PD控制器降低48%，并在13–15 m/s的分布外强风中保持优雅退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型多旋翼飞行器越来越多地在大气边界层中执行任务，该层湍流风速与飞行器空速相当，会严重劣化轨迹跟踪性能，传统反馈控制难以应对。因此需要一种能够感知并利用局部风信息的新型控制策略。

Method: 两阶段管道：(1) 风估计器——注意力增强门控循环网络（Attention-augmented GRU），在数千次穿越von Kármán湍流（含幂律风切变和风向偏转）的仿真飞行中训练，从机载运动学和动力学数据恢复水平风矢量；(2) 飞行控制器——基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习控制器，接收冻结风估计器的输出，进行端到端轨迹跟踪训练。此外进行三因素消融实验，将性能提升分解为运动学分量和风感知分量。

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下每飞行架次均方根误差0.40 m/s，风向误差3.2°，在垂直上升剖面中技能得分0.861（相对恒定风基准）。PPO控制器在4–12 m/s平均风中相对无风感知PD基线降低水平轨迹跟踪误差48%，100%评估回合获胜。消融显示风感知贡献随风速增加而增大，在强风中约占一半收益。在13–15 m/s分布外强风中控制器优雅退化，而基线灾难性失效。

Conclusion: 将基于学习的风估计与强化学习控制相结合，能显著提升小型多旋翼在湍流边界层中的轨迹跟踪鲁棒性，风感知的边际收益随风速增大而增加，验证了该方法在真实大气环境中的潜力与泛化能力。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>


### [305] [Certified World Models as Sensing Clocks: Drift-Aware Deadlines for Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01537)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将认证世界模型的预测有效期转化为可操作感知时钟的方法，推导出无感知区间的漂移感知截止期限，并在3D VN-JEPA模型和合成基准上验证了其在有效性和预算匹配方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身智能体中，持续高频率感知环境代价高昂。现有世界模型缺乏一个原则性的规则来决定何时可以"滑行"（不感知）以及何时必须重新感知。作者旨在利用认证世界模型的有效性视野，构建一个有部署保证的感知调度时钟。

Method: 从经过审计的等变世界模型出发，推导无感知区间的截止期限。核心发现是：仅靠流形上的李雅普诺夫率会高估滑行有效性，必须采用校准后的原生 rollout 漂移包络来承载部署保证。在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型上验证该时钟控制跨种子和数据分片的一段时间同步认证违规的能力。在"线索条件定理台"（一个所有调度器共享相同模型的合成基准）上，将该时钟与精确混合期望信念调度进行对比。

Result: 所提时钟在3D VN-JEPA模型上能控制跨种子和数据分片的一段时间同步认证违规。在合成基准上，该时钟在部署分布上保持有效，并在匹配感知预算下显著减少了事件尾部违规。在短视野冻结VN-JEPA场景下，经验共形视野在有效性和预算上与部署时钟匹配，且部分重置探索未发现谱项在预算匹配上的明显优势。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是提出了一种认证感知时钟原语和漂移感知部署方法，而非声称谱时钟在经验上普遍优于所有非谱调度器。作者明确报告了方法的局限性，包括在短视野场景中共形方法可匹配性能，以及谱项未显现预算匹配优势。

Abstract: Certified world models estimate how long their predictions remain valid. We turn this validity horizon into an operational sensing clock: a rule for when an agent should stop coasting and re-sense. Starting from an audited equivariant world model, we derive a deadline for no-sensing intervals and show that deployable deadlines in learned world models must be drift-aware: on-manifold Lyapunov rates alone overestimate coasting validity, while calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes carry the deployed guarantee. On a frozen 3D VN-JEPA model, the resulting clock controls held-out interval-simultaneous certificate violation across seeds and data shards. In a cue-conditioned theorem-bed (a synthetic bench where all schedulers share the exact model, isolating the scheduling rule), the clock remains valid on the deployment distribution and substantially reduces eventful-tail violations relative to exact-mixture expected-belief scheduling at matched sensing budget. We also report limits: in the short-horizon frozen VN-JEPA regime, empirical conformal horizons match the deployed clock on validity and budget, and a partial-reset exploration finds no clean budget-matched advantage for the spectral term. Thus the contribution is a certified sensing-clock primitive and drift-aware deployment method, not a claim that spectral clocks empirically dominate all non-spectral schedulers.

</details>


### [306] [Evolutionary Feature Engineering for Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01548)
*Ege Onur Taga,Yilin Zhuang,M. Emrullah Ildiz,Petros Mol,Abhimanyu Das,Karthik Duraisamy,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出进化特征工程（EFE）框架，利用大语言模型驱动的进化算法自动发现结构化数据的预处理变换。EFE将变换表示为标准化Python程序，可直接插入现有ML流水线。在时间序列预测（EFE-Time）和表格预测（EFE-Tab）两个场景中，EFE分别实现了预测误差降低3%-19%以及可解释特征构建，证明LLM驱动的进化可同时提升准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用作进化优化中的开放式搜索算子，但在结构化数据的自动特征工程方面，尚缺乏一种能将LLM的代码生成能力与进化搜索相结合、并直接融入现有ML流水线的统一框架。

Method: EFE框架将特征变换表示为具有标准fit/transform接口的Python程序。进化过程中，利用数据集上下文、汇总统计量和验证集下游性能反馈对候选程序进行迭代优化。EFE-Time学习可逆的、数据集特定的归一化方法；EFE-Tab进化出紧凑的特征程序，添加有用可解释特征并移除冗余特征。

Result: EFE-Time在多个数据集上平均降低预测误差（MASE、WQL、MAE）3%以上，在COVID-Deaths数据集上改善高达19%，且对Chronos-2等最新时间序列基础模型同样有效。EFE-Tab在经典决策树上尤为有效，少量进化特征即可获得有竞争力的准确率，同时保持可解释性，效果优于或匹配现有LLM特征工程方法。

Conclusion: EFE证明，基于LLM的进化算法能够在自动处理结构化数据时同时提升准确性和可解释性，为将LLM作为进化搜索算子应用于特征工程领域提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as open-ended search operators in evolutionary optimization. We introduce Evolutionary Feature Engineering (EFE), a framework for using LLM-based evolution to discover preprocessing transformations for structured data. EFE represents transformations as Python programs with a standardized fit/transform interface, allowing them to be inserted directly into existing machine learning pipelines. During evolution, candidate programs are refined using dataset context, summary statistics, and downstream performance feedback on validation set. We instantiate EFE in two settings. For time-series forecasting, EFE-Time learns invertible, dataset-specific normalizations that improve off-the-shelf time-series foundation models. It reduces forecasting errors (MASE, WQL, MAE) 3% or more when averaged across datasets and improvements are as much as 19% on the COVID-Deaths dataset. Notably, these improvements occur with recent TSFMs such as Chronos-2. For tabular prediction, EFE-Tab evolves compact feature programs that add useful interpretable features and remove redundant ones, improving or matching existing LLM-based feature-engineering methods. We found EFE-Tab to be particularly effective on classical decision trees, where small sets of evolved features yield competitive accuracy while preserving interpretability. Overall, EFE demonstrates that LLM-based evolution can improve both accuracy and interpretability when automatically tackling structured data.

</details>


### [307] [X-LogSMask: Expand Transformer for Graph-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01553)
*Leyan Li,Rennong Yang,Zhenxing Zhang,Liping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: X-LogSMask 是一种可解释的多头对数结构掩码，通过将对数变换后的归一化图邻接矩阵直接注入注意力 logits，使标准Transformer能够高效处理图数据，无需修改架构即可在多项图学习基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的自注意力机制假设全连接交互，与图数据的稀疏、结构化、多尺度特性不匹配。现有图Transformer通过结构编码、混合消息传递或学习注意力约束来解决这一问题，但往往引入额外复杂性且可解释性有限。作者希望以简单、可解释的方式将图拓扑结构直接注入注意力机制。

Method: 提出X-LogSMask，核心思想有三：(1) 对数变换：将对称归一化的邻接矩阵进行对数变换，转换为拓扑感知的门控信号，直接加到注意力logits上，抑制不存在边的节点交互，同时保留特征依赖的注意力；(2) 多头结构半径：为不同注意力头分配归一化邻接矩阵的不同幂次，使每个头拥有定义明确的结构感受野半径，在单层内实现多跳信息传播；(3) 理论动机：证明标准Transformer编码器可解释为完全图上的单步消息传递，X-LogSMask则是其拓扑约束的替代方案。

Result: 在20个节点级、边级和图级基准数据集上进行评估，X-LogSMask在13个数据集上达到最优性能。即使在轻量级单层配置下也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单、可解释的结构掩码可以使标准自注意力成为有效的图学习算子，无需修改Transformer架构本身。X-LogSMask提供了一种优雅的拓扑注入方式，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Transformers have become general-purpose architectures, but their all-to-all self-attention is poorly matched to graph data, whose interactions are sparse, structured and multi-scale. Existing Graph Transformers address this mismatch through structural encodings, hybrid message-passing modules or learned attention constraints, often introducing additional complexity and limited interpretability. Here we introduce X-LogSMask, an explainable multi-head logarithmic structural mask that injects symmetrically normalized graph topology directly into attention logits. The logarithmic transform converts structural connectivity into a topology-aware gating signal, suppressing unsupported node interactions while preserving feature-dependent attention. By assigning different powers of the normalized adjacency matrix to different attention heads, X-LogSMask gives each head a defined structural radius and supports multi-hop information propagation within a single layer. We further show that a standard Transformer encoder can be interpreted as one-step message passing on a complete graph, motivating X-LogSMask as a topology-constrained alternative to unrestricted self-attention. Across 20 node-, edge- and graph-level benchmarks, Transformers equipped with X-LogSMask achieve state-of-the-art performance on 13 datasets and remain competitive in a lightweight one-layer configuration. These results show that simple, interpretable structural masks can make self-attention an effective graph-learning operator without changing the Transformer architecture. The code is available at https://github.com/LiLeyan-0120/X-LogSMask.

</details>


### [308] [Geometric Signatures of Reasoning: A Spectral Perspective on Task Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01571)
*Aria Masoomi,Mahsa Bazzaz,Adel Javanmard,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了链式思维推理的几何特性，将推理轨迹形式化为Transformer隐藏状态空间中的离散曲线，提出有效维度指标衡量任务难度，并利用运动学特征在生成完成前预测答案正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注CoT推理链的长度和内容，而对其内部几何结构知之甚少。作者希望从几何视角揭示推理轨迹的形状与任务难度、解答质量之间的内在关联。

Method: 将CoT推理链形式化为ℝᵈ空间中的离散曲线；引入谱几何泛函（有效维度d_ρ，基于特征值谱平坦度衡量轨迹复杂度）、位置几何泛函（均值位置、位置离散度、初始与当前隐藏状态）及运动学泛函（平均速度、平均速率、速率离散度）；理论证明更平坦的特征值谱对应更难的任务。

Result: 在MATH500数学推理数据集上，d_ρ区分难题与简单题的AUC达0.93；仅用前20%生成token的运动学特征即可预测答案正确性；这些正确性信号可跨不同难度问题迁移。

Conclusion: 模型内部推理轨迹的形状是洞察任务难度与解答质量的原则性窗口，可为未来早停策略提供理论基础。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables large language models (LLMs) to solve complex problems by generating intermediate reasoning steps. While much attention has been paid to the length and content of these reasoning chains, far less is known about their internal geometry. We study the \emph{geometry} of CoT trajectories in the hidden state space of transformer models, formalizing each reasoning chain as a discrete curve in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and characterizing it through spectral, positional, and kinematic geometric functionals. We introduce the effective dimension $d_ρ$ as a measure of trajectory complexity and show theoretically that trajectories with flatter eigenvalue spectra correspond to harder tasks, as they explore more of the hidden dimensions. Lastly, we explore how kinematic features of the trajectory, mean position, positional dispersion, initial and current hidden states, mean velocity, mean speed, and speed dispersion, can be used to predict solution correctness before generation is complete, and may inform future early-stopping strategies. Experimentally, on mathematical reasoning problems from the MATH500 dataset, $d_ρ$ achieves $0.93$ AUC in distinguishing easy from hard problems, while kinematic features potentially can predict correctness from only the first $20\%$ of generated tokens. These correctness signatures transfer across questions of varying difficulty, establishing that the shape of a model's internal reasoning trajectory is a principled window into both task hardness and solution quality.

</details>


### [309] [BOUNDARY_SYNC: Measuring Communication-Induced Representational Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01600)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出BOUNDARY_SYNC协议，通过耦合放大因子(CAF)测量LLM智能体间的表征耦合程度。实验表明文本和图像通信均会导致显著的同质化(CAF<1)，群体规模可调节耦合方向，耦合由提示上下文驱动而非累积更新，且跨模型复现显示出极大差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被部署为通信智能体，一个核心问题是：智能体间的通信是否会导致输出趋同？理解这种表征耦合现象对于多智能体系统的设计至关重要，但此前缺乏系统性的测量协议和定量证据。

Method: 提出BOUNDARY_SYNC协议，核心指标为耦合放大因子CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline，其中CAF<1表示同质化，CAF>1表示多样化。在GPT-4o上进行受控实验(N=30, ~9900次API调用)，测量文本和图像通信中的耦合程度，并通过无通信消融和提示扰动对照进行验证，同时检验群体规模(K=3 vs K=5)的调节效应、跨模型复现(含DeepSeek)以及耦合状态性(有状态vs无状态)。

Result: (1)文本通信导致显著同质化(CAF=0.803 [0.740, 0.873], d=1.30, p<0.001)；(2)图像通信在模态内基线条件下同样产生同质化(CAF=0.834 [0.811, 0.858])；(3)群体规模调节耦合方向：K=5时同质化，K=3时CAF>1.0(点估计1.14和1.06)，转向多样化；(4)跨模型复现显示极端差异(CAF 0.034-0.803)，DeepSeek受格式伪影主导；(5)耦合是无状态的，由提示上下文驱动而非累积更新，连续共识产生单调收敛。

Conclusion: LLM智能体耦合是真实存在、可测量且在提示层面可控的现象，对多智能体系统设计具有直接指导意义——提示工程可有效调节智能体间的同质化与多样化倾向。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as communicating agents, does inter-agent communication cause outputs to converge? We introduce BOUNDARY_SYNC, a protocol measuring representational coupling via the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline), where CAF < 1 indicates homogenization and CAF > 1 indicates diversification. In controlled GPT-4o experiments (N=30, ~9,900 API calls), we measure coupling in text and image communication. Key findings: (1) text communication causes significant homogenization (CAF=0.803 [0.740, 0.873], d=1.30, p<0.001), confirmed by no-communication ablation and prompt-perturbation controls; (2) image communication also homogenizes under within-modality baselines (CAF=0.834 [0.811, 0.858]), with comparable proportional effect; (3) group size moderates coupling direction -- K=5 produces homogenization while K=3 yields CAF > 1.0 (point estimates 1.14 and 1.06, CI pending), suggesting a directional shift toward diversification; (4) cross-model replication shows extreme variation (CAF 0.034-0.803), with DeepSeek dominated by format artifacts; (5) coupling is stateless -- driven by prompt context rather than cumulative updating, with continuous consensus producing monotonic convergence. These results establish LLM agent coupling as real, measurable, and controllable at the prompt level, with direct implications for multi-agent system design.

</details>


### [310] [SINA: A Fully Automated Circuit Schematic Image to Netlist Generator Using Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01609)
*Saoud Aldowaish,Yashwanth Karumanchi,Kai-Chen Chiang,Mohammed Ayman Habib,Finn Murphy,Rishen Cao,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SINA 是一个开源的电路原理图图像到网表的自动生成工具，结合深度学习组件检测、连通分量标记、OCR 和 VLM，可处理 IC 和 PCB 级原理图，网表生成准确率达 96.67%，比现有最优方法高 2.72 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在数字 EDA 中应用广泛，但模拟/混合信号领域因缺乏机器可读的电路设计知识而受限。大量电路原理图以图像形式存在于论文、教科书和网站中，无法被 EDA 工具直接处理。现有图像转网表方法缺乏对 IC 和 PCB 级原理图的泛化能力，且在元器件识别、连接关系推断以及区分连接点与交叉导线方面存在不足。

Method: 提出 SINA 全自动流水线：(1) 深度学习实现鲁棒的元器件检测；(2) 连通分量标记实现精确的连接关系推断；(3) OCR 提取元器件参考标识符；(4) VLM（视觉语言模型）实现可靠的参考标识符分配；(5) 专用交叉导线检测模块区分导线交叉与连接；(6) 使用图同构技术验证生成网表的正确性。

Result: SINA 在 IC 和 PCB 级原理图上均有效，整体网表生成准确率达 96.67%，相比现有最优方法提升 2.72 倍。

Conclusion: SINA 作为首个统一处理 IC 和 PCB 级原理图的开源网表生成工具，通过多技术融合解决了元器件识别、连接推断和交叉导线区分等关键难题，为 AI 驱动的模拟/混合信号 EDA 提供了高质量机器可读数据集构建能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have revolutionized Electronic Design Automation (EDA), particularly through Large Language Models (LLMs) for circuit design tasks. However, their application to analog and mixed-signal domains remains limited by the lack of machine-readable representations of existing circuit design knowledge. Circuit schematic images found in research manuscripts, textbooks, and websites constitute a vast repository of validated designs; however, these visual representations cannot be directly processed by EDA tools. Converting them into machine-readable netlists is essential for enabling simulation, verification, and building comprehensive databases for AI-based models. Current conversion methods lack generalization across both Integrated Circuit (IC) and Printed Circuit Board (PCB) level schematics. Moreover, they struggle with component recognition and connectivity inference, and fail to distinguish between connected junctions and crossing wires. In this paper, we propose SINA, an open-source circuit schematic image-to-netlist generator. SINA is a fully automated pipeline that integrates deep learning for robust component detection, connected-component labeling for accurate connectivity inference, Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for component reference designator extraction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reliable reference designator assignment. SINA handles both IC- and PCB-level schematics and incorporates dedicated crossing-wires detection to differentiate wire intersections from connections. We validate the correctness of the generated netlists using graph isomorphism techniques. Our experiments demonstrate an overall netlist generation accuracy of 96.67%, which is 2.72x higher compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [311] [MKGR: Multimodal Knowledge-Graph Representation Learning for Cold-Start Protein-Protein Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01627)
*Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向冷启动蛋白质互作（PPI）预测的多模态表示框架，结合区域感知的蛋白质序列编码与四种生物医学知识图谱（蛋白质-药物、疾病、miRNA、lncRNA），通过桥接重构目标正则化和配对级门控模块自适应融合序列与图谱证据，在冷启动场景下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动PPI预测的关键挑战在于：待预测的候选蛋白质在训练阶段没有任何PPI边，仅依赖网络拓扑的模型会失去有效上下文信息。因此需要引入额外的生物医学知识来弥补网络拓扑的不足。

Method: 提出多模态框架：①序列分支从结构感知的序列区域提取上下文表示；②四个蛋白质中心的知识图谱（蛋白质-药物、蛋白质-疾病、蛋白质-miRNA、蛋白质-lncRNA）通过图注意力编码器学习模态特异的蛋白质嵌入；③桥接重构目标通过恢复共享的蛋白质-实体关联来正则化图学习；④配对级门控模块自适应整合序列与图谱证据，为每个候选蛋白质对生成预测。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上，分别在"新-旧"和"新-新"两种冷启动设置下评估，该方法在ACC、F1、AUC、AUPR和MCC五项指标上均一致优于序列、网络和知识图谱等竞争性基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过融合蛋白质序列的结构区域信息和多源生物医学知识图谱，并结合桥接重构正则化与自适应门控融合，该框架有效解决了冷启动PPI预测中网络拓扑信息缺失的问题，展现出优越的预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate protein-protein interaction (PPI) prediction is central to functional genomics, disease mechanism discovery, and drug development. A difficult setting arises when candidate interactions include proteins that have no observed PPI edges during training, where models relying on network topology alone often lose useful context. This paper presents \method, a multimodal representation framework for cold-start PPI prediction. \method\ combines region-aware protein sequence encoding with four protein-centered biomedical knowledge graphs, including protein-drug, protein-disease, protein-miRNA, and protein-lncRNA associations. The sequence branch extracts contextual representations from structurally informed sequence regions, while graph attention encoders learn modality-specific protein embeddings from sparse biomedical associations.
