The approach consists of three steps: i) selecting confounders and effect modifiers based on prior knowledge (or alternatively using data-adaptive methods to learn effect modifiers), ii) estimating exposure effects in hypothetical populations with different effect modifier prevalences using transportability methods, and iii) modeling the estimated effects as a function of prevalence values. This approach provides two types of outputs: estimation of the change in the population-level exposure effects attributable to increases in effect modifier prevalence and ranking of effect estimates across multiple effect modifiers and prevalences to identify population characteristics most strongly associated with differential vulnerability. We demonstrate the approach using Demographic and Health Surveys data to examine heterogeneous effects of drought on child stunting and provide a Shiny application to implement this approach in any setting.

</details>


### [382] [Pattern-Calibrated Multimodal Prediction under Blockwise Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01821)
*Junhan Yu,Kejian Zhang,Doudou Zhou,Guojun Zhu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOSAIC 提出一种面向多模态数据块缺失的模式校准框架：学习共享与模态特异性表征，利用与目标模式重叠的可用表征拟合第一阶段预测器，再从目标模式数据估计校准间隙，从而在不同缺失模式间借力而不混淆各模式的预测规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将多模态块缺失视为不完全输入问题（插补、零填充或单一池化预测器），虽然能跨模式借力，但会混合不同缺失模式特有的预测规则。作者关注的是：给定一个预先指定的观测模态组合（目标模式），如何在不坍缩各模式预测规则的前提下，从其他模式中有效借用信息。

Method: MOSAIC 框架分三步：(1) 学习共享表征和模态特异性表征；(2) 利用与目标模式重叠的可用表征，拟合第一阶段预测器（重叠借力）；(3) 利用目标模式自身的样本，估计并校正第一阶段预测与目标模式真实规则之间的校准间隙（模式校准）。理论分析给出非渐近误差界，将误差分解为重叠有效样本量、校准间隙和表征学习误差三项。

Result: 模拟实验验证了表征恢复和目标模式校正的有效性。在 ICU 死亡率预测、情绪识别和青光眼分类三个实际应用中，当目标模式样本有限或模式间预测规则存在差异时，MOSAIC 均展现出性能增益。

Conclusion: 跨模式借力能否优于局部拟合，取决于规则不匹配程度和表征学习误差的控制。MOSAIC 通过模式校准机制，在借用多模式信息的同时保留了各模式的预测特异性，为块缺失多模态学习提供了理论上有保障、实践中有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Blockwise missingness in multimodal data is usually treated as an incomplete-input problem. We instead focus on prediction for a prespecified observed-modality pattern, where the observed modality set determines the information on which the prediction rule can condition. A procedure that imputes missing modalities, zero-fills unobserved modalities, or trains a single pooled predictor may borrow information across patterns, but it can also mix pattern-specific prediction rules. We propose Multimodal Overlap-aware Shared-specific Alignment and Inter-pattern Calibration (MOSAIC), a pattern-calibrated framework for borrowing across missingness patterns without collapsing their prediction rules. MOSAIC learns shared and modality-specific representations, uses the available representations that overlap with the target pattern to fit a first-stage predictor, and then estimates the calibration gap from target-pattern data. We establish non-asymptotic bounds that decompose the error into overlap effective sample size, calibration gap, and representation-learning error, clarifying when cross-pattern borrowing improves over local fitting and when the improvement is controlled by rule mismatch or representation-learning error. Simulations examine representation recovery and target-pattern correction, and applications to ICU mortality prediction, emotion recognition, and glaucoma classification show gains when target-pattern samples are limited or pattern-specific rules differ.

</details>


### [383] [Beyond Laplace: Closed-form wrapped Gaussian posterior approximations on statistical manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01909)
*Marcelo Hartmann,Luu Hoang Phuc Hau,Anton Mallasto,Albert Kjøller Jacobsen,Georgios Arvanitidis,Søren Hauberg,Håvard Rue,Mark Girolami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文利用对比函数理论，在配备Fisher-Rao度量和先验分布几何的统计流形上，推导了对数映射和指数映射的可计算近似，以解决包裹高斯分布中涉及测地线方程求解、Jacobi场、Christoffel符号和曲率张量等计算瓶颈问题，在保持对复杂后验几何捕捉能力的同时，速度比现有方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯近似虽计算高效，但高斯形式局限于椭圆形状，无法捕捉偏态、厚尾和狭窄高概率区域等重要后验特征。现有包裹高斯方法虽灵活性更强，但采样需通过指数映射求解测地线方程，密度评估依赖对数映射和Jacobi场，涉及昂贵的微分方程求解器和逆矩阵、Christoffel符号、曲率张量等几何量，计算开销极大。因此需要一种既能保留灵活性又能规避这些计算瓶颈的方法。

Method: 利用对比函数（contrast functions）理论，在配备Fisher-Rao度量和先验分布几何的统计流形上，推导指数映射和对数映射的易处理近似，从而绕过对微分方程求解器以及Christoffel符号、曲率张量等几何量的显式计算。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验表明，所提近似方法能有效捕捉复杂的后验几何结构，且计算速度比当前最先进的近似方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过对比函数理论构建的近似方案，成功消除了包裹高斯方法的主要计算瓶颈，在保持对复杂后验分布建模能力的同时实现了显著的效率提升，为贝叶斯推断中的后验近似提供了新的高效途径。

Abstract: In Bayesian statistics, the Laplace approximation provides a computationally efficient approximation to posterior distributions. However, its Gaussian form restricts it to elliptical shapes, limiting its ability to capture important posterior features such as skewness, heavy tails, and narrow high-probability regions. Recent work has addressed this limitation by exploiting Riemannian geometry to push forward Gaussian distributions from the tangent space to the manifold, referred to wrapped Gaussians. While offering greater flexibility, they introduce substantial computational challenges. Sampling requires solving geodesic equations through the exponential map and density evaluation additionally depends on the logarithmic map and Jacobi fields, involving costly differential equation solvers and geometric quantities such as inverse matrices, Christoffel symbols and curvature tensors. To overcome these limitations, we employ the theory of contrast functions to derive tractable approximations of the logarithmic and exponential maps on statistical manifolds endowed with the Fisher--Rao metric and the prior distribution geometry. The resulting methodology bypass the need to compute these geometric quantities and numerical solvers thereby removing the principal computational bottlenecks of existing wrapped Gaussian approaches. Empirical results across a range of models demonstrate that the proposed approximation captures complex posterior geometries while remaining orders of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art approximation.

</details>


### [384] [Moment-Based Selection of Multiresponse Linear Mixed-Effects Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01971)
*Yifan Chen,Yuedong Wang,Guo Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOMENT 是一种基于二阶交叉矩等式的分阶段矩方法框架，用于在混合效应模型中同时进行随机效应协方差矩阵的稀疏选择与估计以及固定效应系数的选择。该方法将问题转化为带正半定约束的光滑凸优化问题，并利用投影梯度下降高效求解，同时提供了有限样本下的选择一致性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多变量纵向数据分析中，现有的混合效应模型方法在同时选择随机效应和固定效应方面存在不足，尤其是当响应变量之间存在相关性时，单独的单变量分析往往效率低下。作者希望提出一种能够利用响应间相关性、同时实现随机效应协方差矩阵稀疏选择和固定效应系数选择的统一框架。

Method: 提出 MOMENT 框架：1）利用二阶交叉矩等式建立矩条件；2）通过对随机效应协方差矩阵的对角线施加稀疏性诱导，并结合正半定约束，将随机效应选择问题转化为光滑约束凸优化问题；3）采用投影梯度下降法进行高效求解；4）在联合 sub-Weibull 误差假设下，建立有限样本的选择一致性理论。

Result: 模拟研究表明 MOMENT 整体上具有竞争力，并且在响应变量相关时显著优于单独的单变量分析。在血液透析数据集上的应用表明，该方法为多变量纵向数据提供了可解释且灵活的分析途径。

Conclusion: MOMENT 框架为混合效应模型中的随机效应和固定效应联合选择与估计提供了一种理论有保障、计算高效且实际有效的解决方案，特别适用于响应相关的多变量纵向数据场景。

Abstract: We propose MOMENT (\textbf{MO}ment-Based \textbf{M}ixed-\textbf{E}ffects Selectio\textbf{N} and Es\textbf{T}imation), a stage-wise moment-based framework that exploits second-order cross-moment identities to select and estimate the random-effects covariance matrix and fixed-effects coefficients. By inducing sparsity through its diagonal under a positive semidefinite constraint, the random-effects selection problem reduces to a smooth constrained convex optimization problem that can be solved efficiently by projected gradient descent. We further establish finite-sample theoretical guarantees for the proposed procedure, including random-effects selection consistency and fixed-effects selection consistency under joint sub-Weibull errors. Simulation studies show that MOMENT performs competitively overall and can substantially outperform separate univariate analyses when responses are correlated. An application to the hemodialysis dataset demonstrates that the proposed method yields an interpretable and flexible approach for multivariate longitudinal data.

</details>


### [385] [Plausibility: Exact inference in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01976)
*Stefan Böhringer,Jesse Swen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个R包"plausibility"，该包基于Plausibility理论框架，能够在广义参数族中进行精确推断，支持多种回归模型（包括glmnet中的惩罚回归模型），并具备良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Plausibility理论框架虽能在一般参数族中进行精确推断，但缺乏便捷的软件实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个易于使用且可扩展的R包，使研究人员能够在实际数据分析中应用该框架。

Method: 基于Plausibility理论框架，开发R包"plausibility"，采用基于类的面向对象机制实现，使其能够兼容广义回归模型（如glmnet的惩罚回归模型），并支持用户通过类继承的方式扩展至新的模型。

Result: 通过多个R内置数据集展示了该包的功能，验证了其在真实数据分析中的有效性，并讨论了实现中的计算问题及其对实际分析的影响。

Conclusion: "plausibility"包成功地将Plausibility理论框架转化为实用的R工具，为回归模型的精确推断提供了便捷途径，且其基于类的设计保证了良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Plausbility is a theoretical framework that allows to conduct exact inference in general parametric families. We introduce R-packages {\em plausibility} that implements this framework for a wide class of regression models. Plausibility can also be used to test penalized regression models such as estimated by package {\em glmnet}. We illustrate the package using a number of R data sets Through a class-based mechanism, the package can be easily extended. We illustrate and discuss computation aspects of the implementation and their impact on real-data analysis.

</details>


### [386] [Grouped Orthogonal Arrays from Orthogonal Arrays and Difference Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02027)
*Meixin Liu,Chunyan Wang,Guangzhou Chen,C. Devon Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对分组正交阵列的构造问题，提出了多种新的构造方法，大幅扩展了现有目录，生成了具有更多分组和更大组规模的分组正交阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算机实验（含分组输入）和物理实验中，常假设不同组因子之间的交互作用可忽略，分组正交阵列正是为解决此类设计问题而引入的。然而，现有分组正交阵列的目录有限，无法满足日益增长的灵活高效设计需求。

Method: 本文提出了多种构造方法，用于扩展分组正交阵列的现有目录。具体构造技术未在摘要中详述，但目标在于生成具有更大组数和更大组规模的新型分组正交阵列。

Result: 所提出的构造方法成功生成了大量新的分组正交阵列，这些阵列在组数和组规模上均显著超越现有目录中的记录。

Conclusion: 本文的构造方法有效扩展了分组正交阵列的可用目录，为分组因子实验设计提供了更丰富、更灵活的设计选择。

Abstract: Grouped orthogonal arrays were introduced to address experimental design problems arising in computer experiments with grouped inputs, as well as in physical experiments where interactions between factors from different groups are assumed to be negligible. Motivated by the growing need for flexible and efficient designs under such settings, this article develops several constructions to expand the existing catalogs of grouped orthogonal arrays. The proposed constructions provide a large collection of new grouped orthogonal arrays with significantly larger numbers of groups and group sizes.

</details>


### [387] [Sequential Structure-Sensitive Residual Diagnostics for PDE Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02101)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于e-processes的结构敏感序贯诊断方法，用于检测逆问题中残差范数诊断无法发现的模型误设。通过空间残差模式专家的投资组合，该方法能够在观测数据处理的早期阶段就检测到结构化模型误差，并提供探索性模型修正的依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统残差范数诊断（如Morozov偏差准则）在平滑逆问题中存在盲区：结构性模型误差可能在观测空间被衰减，导致残差幅值低于阈值但残差模式仍存在系统性偏差，从而使有偏的模型被错误接受，影响参数估计和预测的可靠性。

Method: 基于e-processes构建结构敏感序贯诊断框架：(1) 设计一组空间残差模式专家（字典），每个专家捕捉特定的结构化残差模式；(2) 序贯处理观测数据，更新各专家的似然比财富（likelihood-ratio wealth）；(3) 当聚合财富超过预设阈值时拒绝拟合模型，提供anytime-valid的I类错误控制；(4) 专家财富分布可归因残差模式来源，指导模型修正。

Result: 在三个逆问题上验证：椭圆扩散、二维Stokes流、冰川冰流反演（icepack）。标准偏差检验接受了产生实质性错误感兴趣量的误设拟合；结构敏感的批量检验可用全数据集检测到这些失败；e-process方法仅需部分观测即可更早检测。拒绝后，专家财富有效归因残差模式。

Conclusion: e-process序贯诊断方法在平滑逆问题中比传统残差范数检验更早、更可靠地检测结构化模型误设，且能在不预设替代模型的情况下通过残差模式归因为模型修正提供方向。

Abstract: Computational models in science and engineering are often assessed by checking whether the residual norm is consistent with the assumed noise level. This can be misleading in smoothing inverse problems: structured model errors may be attenuated in observation space, leaving residual magnitudes below practitioner discrepancy thresholds while coherent residual patterns remain. As a result, residual-norm diagnostics can accept fitted models that still give biased parameters, predictions, or quantities of interest. We propose a structure-sensitive sequential diagnostic based on e-processes. The method uses a portfolio of spatial residual-pattern experts, updates their likelihood-ratio wealth as observations are processed, and rejects the fitted model when the aggregate wealth crosses a prescribed threshold, giving anytime-valid type-I error control for a fixed fitted model. We compare the method with Morozov discrepancy checks, fixed-sample residual tests, and batch projection tests. Across three inverse problems (elliptic diffusion, two-dimensional Stokes flow, and a glaciological ice-stream inversion implemented in the community finite-element model icepack) we demonstrate how standard discrepancy checks accept misspecified fits that produce materially wrong quantities of interest. Structure-sensitive batch tests detect these failures using the full dataset, while the e-process detects them earlier from a fraction of the observations. After rejection, the expert wealth attributes the evidence to residual patterns in the chosen dictionary and provides a basis for exploratory model correction.

</details>


### [388] [Conformal Bayes for Two-Sided Censored Gaussian Regression under Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02173)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对删失标签偏移下的共形预测问题，提出了一种结合后验预测倾斜与加权共形校准的共形贝叶斯方法。在双边Tobit高斯模型下，倾斜预测分布具有左原子、内部连续密度和右原子三部分结构，校准权重包含两个原子比率和一个内部密度比率，最终构建出混合最高密度区域预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签偏移下的预测在响应变量被删失时变得非标准。双边删失导致观测分布为混合型（边界原子+连续密度），而隐标签偏移无法直接在删失观测尺度上表示为普通密度比，需要发展新的理论和方法来处理这种混合空间的共形预测问题。

Method: 采用共形贝叶斯框架，结合后验预测倾斜与加权共形校准。在双边Tobit高斯预测头下使用拉普拉斯后验近似，倾斜预测分布分解为左原子、内部和右原子三部分，具有三项闭合形式的归一化因子。隐指数倾斜在删失边界诱导出尾部平均原子权重，内部保持密度比，形成混合观测空间校准权重（两个原子比率+一个内部密度比率）来修正校准测度，由预测倾斜提供目标适应的混合HDR几何结构。

Result: 合成实验表明，加权倾斜共形贝叶斯方法恢复了边际覆盖，且预测集比加权源得分校准更小；同时揭示了边际覆盖与各组件（原子和内部观测）行为之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的共形贝叶斯方法有效解决了双边删失下的标签偏移预测问题，能够在混合空间上构建紧凑且覆盖保证的预测集，但边际覆盖与组件覆盖之间存在内在权衡。

Abstract: Prediction under label shift becomes nonstandard when responses are censored. In a two-sided censored Gaussian model, latent values below $L$ and above $U$ are recorded at the boundary values, so the observed predictive distribution is mixed, with atoms at $L$ and $U$ and a continuous density on $(L,U)$. In this paper we develop conformal Bayes for this mixed-space setting by combining posterior predictive tilting with weighted conformal calibration. Under a two-sided Tobit Gaussian Bayesian prediction head with a Laplace posterior approximation, the tilted predictive distribution has left-atom, interior, and right-atom components, with a three-term closed-form normalizer. The resulting prediction set is a mixed highest density region that can combine boundary atoms with an interior interval and can reduce to atom-only sets under strong censoring. The main technical issue is that latent label shift does not directly give an ordinary density ratio on the observed censored scale. A latent exponential tilt induces tail-averaged atom weights at the censored boundaries, while the interior ratio remains density based. This yields a mixed observed-space calibration weight with two atom ratios and one interior density ratio. The weight corrects the calibration measure, while predictive tilting gives target-adapted mixed-HDR geometry. Synthetic experiments show that weighted tilted conformal Bayes restores marginal coverage with smaller sets than weighted source-score calibration, while revealing a trade-off between marginal coverage and component-wise behavior across atoms and interior observations.

</details>


### [389] [Goodness of Fit Tests Based on Joint Densities of Multiple Sample Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02285)
*Roman Guchenko*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出两类基于模拟置信集的拟合优度检验方法：第一类使用超矩形置信集对次序统计量主成分进行检验；第二类基于最高密度区域的k近邻方法构建任意形状置信集。同时探讨了双样本扩展、矩检验与能量距离检验的比较，以及概率积分变换之外的其他目标分布变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟合优度检验方法在功效上存在局限，尤其是针对高维统计量向量的检验。作者旨在开发更灵活、更强大的检验程序，利用模拟置信集和最高密度区域来改进对联合分布假设的检验能力。

Method: 第一类方法：基于次序统计量主成分的超矩形置信集检验，将早期水平和垂直置信带的工作推广到主成分空间，并研究主成分的几何意义。第二类方法：利用k近邻方法检测最高密度区域，构建任意形状的置信集，适用于更高维统计量向量（包括次序统计量、经验分布函数值、矩以及经典统计量的组合）。双样本扩展：基于多个统计量联合分布的置换检验。其他技术：探讨将数据映射到其他目标分布（如标准正态）的变换策略。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法在功效上具有竞争力，且常优于现有方法（包括经典检验、Zhang检验及相关图形检验）。在正态零分布下，第一主成分对应样本均值，第二主成分与方差的线性模拟相关。基于最高密度区域和k近邻的检验在多种备择假设下均表现出高效能。

Conclusion: 所提出的两类基于模拟置信集的拟合优度检验是对现有方法的有效补充，尤其在高维设定和多样化的备择假设下展现出更强的检验功效。k近邻方法在高维统计量场景中优于传统核密度方法。映射至其他目标分布的策略可进一步利用针对该分布已有的高效检验，拓展了方法的适用性。

Abstract: We propose goodness-of-fit tests based on simulated confidence sets for joint distributions of multiple sample statistics, focusing on absolutely continuous null distributions with known parameters. One class of tests uses hyperrectangular confidence sets for principal components of order statistics and related statistic vectors. Extending earlier work on horizontal and vertical confidence bands for cumulative distribution functions, these tests are compared with some classical, Zhang, and related graphical tests. Simulations show that the proposed procedures are competitive with, and often more powerful than, existing methods. We also study the geometry of principal-component-based statistics; under a normal null distribution, the first principal component corresponds to the sample mean, while the second is related to a linear analogue of variance.
  A second class of tests uses confidence sets of arbitrary shape constructed through highest density regions. Unlike earlier kernel-density-based approaches, we use a k-nearest-neighbor method for detecting highest density regions, which is better suited to higher-dimensional statistic vectors. We study tests based on order statistics, empirical distribution function values, moments, and combinations of classical goodness-of-fit statistics. The resulting procedures are powerful against a wide range of alternatives.
  We also outline a two-sample extension via permutation tests based on joint distributions of several statistics and compare moment-based versions with energy-distance permutation tests. Finally, we discuss transformations other than the probability integral transform, showing that mapping data to another target distribution, such as the standard normal, can be advantageous when powerful tests are available for that distribution.

</details>


### [390] [MATCH: Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses in Non-Euclidean Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02295)
*Leheng Cai,Xu Guo,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出MATCH框架，利用样本分割与独立随机乘子解决非欧氏数据Fréchet回归中条件假设检验的一阶退化问题，统一涵盖全局显著性、部分显著性和模型充分性检验，并引入交叉拟合与p值合并以提升稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Fréchet回归中，检验非欧氏数据是否符合目标模型时，传统的held-out损失差在原假设下是一阶退化的——oracle损失重合，plug-in统计量被冗余估计误差主导，无法直接构建有效的检验统计量。

Method: 提出MATCH（Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses）程序：(1)样本分割后，在held-out损失上引入独立随机乘子，构造非退化高斯主导项，无需残差或切空间坐标；(2)引入交叉拟合检验以改善数据利用率；(3)采用重复交叉拟合与p值合并策略增强稳定性。理论推导包括渐近原假设有效性、固定备择下的一致性及局部功效保证。

Result: 理论证明MATCH在原假设下渐近有效，在固定备择下一致，并具有局部功效。模拟实验涵盖分布响应、对称正定矩阵响应和球面响应，验证理论结果；实证应用于县级家庭收入分布和北大西洋热带气旋位置数据，展示方法的实用性。

Conclusion: MATCH为Fréchet回归中的条件假设检验提供了统一、有效的框架，成功绕过了非欧氏设定下的一阶退化问题，兼具理论严谨性与实际可操作性。

Abstract: We propose a new procedure MATCH (Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses) to test whether the non-Euclidean data match the target model, which is a general framework for significance and specification testing in Fréchet regression. MATCH covers global significance, partial significance, and the adequacy of global Fréchet regression, providing a unified way to compare unrestricted conditional Fréchet means with restricted alternatives. One of the key challenges is that the ordinary held-out loss difference is first-order degenerate under the null: the oracle losses coincide, and plug-in statistics is dominated by nuisance estimation error. MATCH uses sample splitting and independent random multipliers on held-out losses to create a nondegenerate Gaussian leading term without residuals or tangent-space coordinates. To improve data use and stability, we further develop cross-fitted tests and repeated cross-fitting with p-value merging. We establish asymptotic null validity, consistency under fixed alternatives, and local power guarantees. Simulations for distributional, symmetric positive-definite (SPD) matrix-valued, and spherical responses support the theoretical findings, and applications to county-level household income distributions and North Atlantic tropical-cyclone locations demonstrate the practical use of the proposed tests.

</details>


### [391] [Cross-Audit Projection for Model Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02328)
*Yijian Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对二分类模型风险评估，本文揭示K折交叉验证在估计类别特定风险时可能比经验估计量更差的现象，并通过高阶渐近分析解释了原因，进而提出了一种结合重采样与渐近理论投影的两步估计方法——交叉审计投影（CAP），它兼具一阶渐近等价于经验风险与二阶渐近无偏的优良性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K折交叉验证被广泛认为能缓解经验风险的高估乐观性，但作者在二分类经验风险最小化场景中发现，K折CV在估计类别特定风险时表现可能甚至差于经验估计量。这一反直觉现象促使作者深入探究其渐近行为，并寻求更好的模型风险预测方法。

Method: 提出交叉审计投影（CAP）两步法：(1) 交叉审计步——采用与K折CV相同的重采样方案在子样本中估计过度乐观偏差；(2) 投影步——基于高阶渐近理论，针对子样本量缩减进行经验风险的偏差校正。此外，还配套开发了相应的推断程序。

Result: 理论表明CAP估计量一阶渐近等价于经验风险，同时达到二阶渐近无偏。模拟研究验证了其理论优势并展示了良好的有限样本性能，乳腺癌检测的实际应用进一步说明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: K折CV在特定场景下并不可靠，经验估计量的过度乐观源于其二阶渐近偏差。CAP方法通过巧妙结合重采样与渐近理论校正，在保持一阶效率的同时实现了二阶无偏性，为模型风险预测提供了更优的方案。

Abstract: For training-data-based model risk prediction, $K$-fold cross-validation~(CV) is widely used to mitigate the well-known over-optimism of the empirical risk and is often regarded as reliable. However, for binary classification via empirical risk minimization, our numerical studies reveal a surprising phenomenon: $K$-fold CV may perform poorly in estimating class-specific risks, even worse than the empirical estimator. We perform a higher-order asymptotic analysis showing that $K$-fold CV may converge at a slower rate, whereas the empirical estimator exhibits a second-order asymptotic bias that explains its over-optimism. These findings motivate a novel two-step procedure for model risk prediction, termed cross-audit projection (CAP). The cross-audit step adopts the same resampling scheme as $K$-fold CV to estimate over-optimism in subsamples, while the asymptotic-theory-informed projection step adjusts for the reduced sample size in bias correction of the empirical risk. The resulting CAP estimator is first-order asymptotically equivalent to the empirical risk while achieving second-order asymptotic unbiasedness. An accompanying inference procedure is also developed. Simulation studies support theoretical advantages of CAP and demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance. An application to breast cancer detection further illustrates the proposed method.

</details>


### [392] [Merging of Bayes and quasi-Bayes empirical Bayes procedures for Poisson compound decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02340)
*Stefano Favaro,Sandra Fortini*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究泊松复合决策问题，从g-modeling视角比较两种非参数策略：基于狄利克雷过程后验的贝叶斯经验贝叶斯方法，以及基于牛顿算法的准贝叶斯经验贝叶斯方法。文章建立了两种估计诱导的边际概率质量函数的浓度速率，并将其转化为遗憾的衰减速率，证明了两种策略的频率学派合并结果。数值实验表明准贝叶斯策略在精度相当的情况下计算资源需求显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松复合决策问题是统计学中长期存在的难题。准贝叶斯策略（基于牛顿算法）计算上具有吸引力，但其与贝叶斯策略之间的关系缺乏理论论证。本文旨在为二者的关系提供理论支撑。

Method: 在泊松混合模型框架下，假设存在一个"真实"（oracle）混合分布，作者：(1) 推导了贝叶斯和准贝叶斯估计所诱导的边际概率质量函数的浓度速率；(2) 将这些速率转化为对应遗憾（即超额贝叶斯风险）的衰减速率；(3) 利用上述结果证明两种经验贝叶斯策略之间的频率学派合并（merging）结果；(4) 将分析扩展至多维泊松复合决策问题；(5) 通过合成数据进行数值实验验证。

Result: 理论上，建立了边际PMF的浓度速率和遗憾的衰减速率，证明了贝叶斯与准贝叶斯策略的合并性质。数值实验显示，准贝叶斯策略在精度上与贝叶斯策略相当，但计算资源需求显著减少，尤其在高维情形下优势更为突出。

Conclusion: 准贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略是一种兼具统计精度和计算效率的可行方法。理论分析为两种策略的关系提供了严格论证，合并结果从频率学派角度验证了准贝叶斯策略的合理性，多维扩展进一步增强了方法的实用性。

Abstract: The Poisson compound decision problem is a long-standing problem in statistics, in which empirical Bayes methods are used to estimate Poisson means under a mixture model. We study this problem from the viewpoint of $g$-modeling, comparing two nonparametric strategies for estimating the unknown mixing distribution: a Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on the Dirichlet process posterior, and a quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on Newton's algorithm. The latter is computationally attractive, but its relationship with the Bayesian strategy requires theoretical justification. Under a Poisson mixture model with a ``true'', or oracle, mixing distribution, we establish concentration rates for the marginal probability mass functions induced by the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian estimates. These rates are then translated into rates of decay for the corresponding regrets, interpreted as excess Bayes risks, and used to prove a frequentist merging result between the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategies. We also extend the analysis to the multidimensional Poisson compound decision problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic data illustrate that the quasi-Bayesian strategy achieves accuracy comparable to the Bayesian strategy, while requiring substantially fewer computational resources, especially in the multidimensional setting.

</details>


### [393] [Inference for Group Interaction Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02385)
*Jiawei Fu,Cyrus Samii,Ye Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为群体互动实验（个体随机分配至不同处理条件下的群体）建立了基于设计的推断框架，涵盖群体固定/随机形成以及潜在结果有无干扰等多种情景，并在稀疏抽样渐近体系下证明了聚类稳健推断在干扰存在时仍能提供有效的边际暴露效应推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验研究中常见个体随机分配到群体、群体接受不同处理的设计，但现有文献对这种"群体互动实验"的基于设计的推断缺乏系统研究，尤其在对群体形成方式（固定/随机）和干扰（interference）存在与否的不同组合下，应使用何种推断策略尚不明确。

Method: 作者在稀疏抽样渐近体系下，对四种情景（群体固定/随机形成 × 有无干扰）分别刻画了设计所瞄准的因果估量（causal estimand）及相应的推断策略；引入了一种新颖的耦合（coupling）策略来推导常用估计量的渐近分布。

Result: 结果显示：(1) 当干扰存在时，聚类稳健推断保持一致性并能解释多种来源的依赖关系，对边际暴露效应提供有效推断；(2) 当无干扰且群体随机形成时，设计退化为个体随机实验，个体层面的异方差稳健推断即可满足平均处理效应的推断需求。

Conclusion: 本文为群体互动实验提供了统一的基于设计的推断框架，明确了不同情景下正确的因果估量与推断策略，所引入的耦合方法对其他复杂实验中的基于设计推断也具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: A common experimental research design is one in which individuals are randomly allocated into groups that then interact under different group-level treatment conditions. We develop design-based inference for such "group interaction" experiments, covering scenarios in which groups are either fixed or randomly formed and in which potential outcomes are either fixed relative to others' group assignments or subject to interference. For each scenario, we characterize the causal estimand that the design targets and the inferential strategy appropriate to it. Working in a sparse-sampling asymptotic regime, we show that cluster-robust inference remains consistent and accounts for dependencies from various sources when interference is present, delivering valid inference on marginalized exposure effects. When interference is absent and groups are formed randomly, the design reduces to an individually randomized experiment, and individual-level heteroskedasticity-robust inference suffices for the average treatment effect. Our results on the asymptotic distribution of commonly used estimators rely on a novel coupling strategy that may be useful for design-based inference in other complex experiments.

</details>
